piṉṉar ammalaic cāraliṭattuḷḷa civanta pūkkaḷaiyum malai erukku, civanta kōṭal, āmpal, aṉiccam, ceṅkaḻunīr, kuṟiñci, veṭci, ceṅkoṭuvēri, tēmā, cemmaṇi, perumūṅkil, eṟuḻ, marāmaram, kūviram, vaṭavaṉam, veḷḷiya voṭṭappālai, pañcāyk kōrai, veṇkāk kavaṇam, karuviḷam, payiṉi, vāṉi, kuravam, paccilai, makiḻ, kāyā, āvirai, ciṟu mūṅkil, cūrai, ciṟupūḷai, kuṉṟi, murukkilai, marutu, kōṅru, mañcāṭi, pātiri, cerunti, puṉali, karantai, kāṭṭumallikai, mā, tillai, pālai, mullai, kañcā, piṭavam, ceṅkaruṅkāli, vāḻai, vaḷḷi, neytal, teṅkiṉ pāḷai, centāmarai, ñāḻal, mauval, kokuṭi, pavaḻakkāṉ mallikai, cāti karuntāmaraikkoṭi, veṇkōṭal, tāḻai, curapuṉṉai, kāñci, karuṅku vaḷai, ōmai, māvam, iṇṭam, ilavam, koṉṟai, aṭumpu, ātti, avarai, pakaṉṟai, palācam, acōkam, vañci, picci, karunocci, tumpai, tiruttuḻāy, tōṉṟi, nantiyāvaṭṭam, naṟai, puṉṉai, parutti, pīrkku, kurukkatti, cantaṉam, akil, nārattam, nākam, iruvāṭci, kuntam, vēṅkai, oṇ ceṅkāntaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ virumpappaṭum pūkkaḷai ellām paṟittu maḻaiyiṉāṟ kaḻuvappaṭṭa pāṟaimītu kuvittōm; āyō eṉṟu iṭaiyiṭaiyē olittuk kiḷikaḷai ōṭṭiṉōm; taḻaikaḷaik koytu araiyilē taḻai uṭaiyai uṭuttōm; melliya malarkaḷai mirutuvāṉa kariya kūntalil aḻakupeṟac cāttiṉōm.
aḻakiya tōṉṟal avviṭattē vantāṉ
piṉṉar neruppup pōṉṟa niṟattuṭaṉ viḷaṅkum aḻakiya taḷiruṭaiya acōkiṉ pūntātu utirkiṉṟa kuḷirnta niḻalil pōyirun tōm. appoḻutu ōr aḻakiya tōṉṟal vantāṉ. avaṉ mayiriṭattu mayirc cāntaip pūci ulartti, akiṟpukai ūṭṭi, akil ney taṭavi muṭinta nīlamaṇi pōṉṟa koṇṭai uṭaiyaṉ. avaṉ, malaiyiṭattum nilattum marakkiḷaikaḷiṭattum cuṉaikaḷiṭattum pūtta paṉṉiṟa malarkaḷāl toṭutta mālaikaḷaiyum tāḻampūvaiyum muṭiyil aṇintu murukaṉ pōl viḷaṅkiṉāṉ; cantaṉam pūciya avaṉatu mārpiṭattuppala āparaṇaṅkaḷ viḷaṅkiṉa; ceṉṉiyilē cuṟṟiya piccimālaiyum kātiṟ cerukiya acōkantaḷirum aḻaku ceytaṉa. avaṉ āṭaiyiṉmēl tulaṅkumpaṭi kaccuk kaṭṭiyiruntāṉ. pūṇ iṟukap peṟṟa kaiyil villaiyum ampai yum ēnti niṉṟāṉ. avaṉ, taṉṉoṭetirtta ēṟukaḷai ellām veṉṟu putiya āvaikkāṇum ēṟupōla nammuṉ tōṉṟiṉāṉ. mūṅkil mukai pōṉṟa veḷḷiya paṟkaḷuṭaiya nāykaḷ imai koṭṭātu eṅkaḷaip pārttu vaḷaintu varalāyiṉa. yām mikunta accam aṭaintēmāy vēṟu iṭattē cellā niṉṟōm. appōtu apperuntakaiyām veruvutaṟku añci iṉiya moḻikaḷaic colli ematu aḻakaip pukaḻnturaittāṉ. piṉṉar, “iḷaiyīr” eṉa emmai viḷittu, “ivviṭattē kaṇaikkut tappi vanta yāṉaiyaip pārttatuṇṭō?” eṉṟu kēṭṭāṉ. ataṟku yām patil etuvum kūṟātu niṉṟōm. atukaṇṭu apperuntakai “melliyalīr tappi ōṭivanta vilaṅkaik kāṭṭittārīrāyiṉum eṉṉuṭaṉ oru vārttai pēcutal nuṅkaṭkup paḻiyākumō” eṉṟu kūṟiṉāṉ; taḻai paranta pūkka ḷuṭaiya komparai muṟittup parikkōlai matiyāta matayāṉaipōla ōccik kalleṉṉum ōcaipaṭak kuraikkum vēṭṭai nāykaḷiṉ ōcaiyai māṟṟi yāṅkaḷ vārttai colvatōr kālattai etirpārttu niṉṟāṉ.
avaṉ nammai yāṉaiyiṉiṉṟum kāttāṉ
iḵtu ivvāṟākak kuṟiya kālkaḷaiyum tiṉai vaikkōliṉāl vēynta kūraiyaiyum uṭaiya kuṭicaiyilirukkum kuṟamakaṉ piṇai pōṉṟa nōkkaiyuṭaiya maṉaivi tāṉē camaitta kaḷḷai eṭuttuk koṭukka ataṉai uṇṭu tiṉaip puṉattaik kāval ceyya maṟantāṉ. ākavē yāṉai tiṉaippuṉattai aḻittatu. ataṉaikkaṇṭu varuttam mikuntavaṉākiya kuṟavaṉ pāmpiṉ vaṭiviṉatāki villai vaḷaittu ampai eytu, taṭṭaiyait taṭṭi, vāyai maṭittuc cīḻkkai aṭittu yāṉaiyaip puṉattiṉiṉṟum veruṭṭiṉāṉ. kōpam mikunta yāṉai maraṅkaḷai muṟittu iṭiyēṟupōl āravārañceytu kaiyai nilattil aṭittu emaṉaip pōla eṅkaḷmītu neruṅki vantatu. nām ataṉaikkaṇṭu naṭukkamuṟṟu uyir piḻaikkum ōriṭattaip peṟavēṇṭi nāṇaik kaiviṭṭu vaḷaikaḷ olippa akkuricilaic cārntu teyvamēṟiṉa mayilpōla naṭuṅki niṉṟōm. akkuricil villai naṉṟāka vaḷaintu yāṉaiyiṉ mukattil ampaic celuttiṉāṉ. puḷḷiyiṉaiyum pukariṉaiyumuṭaiya mattakattil ampupaṭṭu uruviṉa puṇṇiliruntu, murukaṉāl varuttamuṟṟa makaḷirkku veṟiyāṭu kaḷattil kuruti kutikkumāṟu irattam perukiṟṟu. avvēḻam avviṭattil niṟṟal āṟṟātu puṟaṅkoṭuttu ōṭiṟṟu.
āṟṟōṭṭam eṅkaḷai iḻuttuc ceṉṟatu
ataṉpiṉ nām, murukavēḷukku malaiyiluṟaiyum teyvamakaḷir kaikōttāṭuvatu pōṉṟu kaṭampiṉ araiyiṉai neruṅkac cūḻnta mālaipōl kai kōttukkoṇṭu āṟṟiṉ perukkilē kutittu viḷaiyāṭiṉōm. iṭikaraiyiṉiṉṟa vāḻai marattai iḻuppatu pōla āṟṟōṭṭam eṅkaḷai iḻuttuc ceṉṟatu. atu kaṇṭu aḻakiya mālai aṇinta āṇṭakai viraintu vantu eṅkaḷai eṭuttut talaiviyaip pārttu, “niṉatu aḻakiya nalattai nukarvēṉ, niṉṉai nīṅkuvēṉeṉac ciṟitu mañcātē” eṉṟu coṉṉāṉ; avaḷiṉ oḷi poruntiya neṟṟiyait tuṭaittu neṭunāḷ ikkaḷavoḻukkam nikaḻavēṇṭumeṉa niṉaittu eṉ mukattai nōkkic cirittāṉ. aṅṅaṉam avaṉ aṇukiya pōtu avaḷukku iyalpākiya nāṇamum accamum avviṭattu vantu tōṉṟukaiyiṉālē viraiya avaṉiṭattiṉiṉṟum nīṅka muyaṉṟāḷ; avaṉ viṭātavaṉāy aṅṅaṉam niṉṟa nilaiyilē kaiyālē aṇaittu ivaḷ mārpu taṉ mārpilē oṭuṅkumpaṭi taḻuviṉāṉ.
mayilkaḷ kayiṟṟil ēṟi āṭumakaḷ pōl taḷarum malai nāṭu
miḷaku paḻam cintikkiṭakkum nīṇṭa pāṟaiyiṭattu māmpaḻaṅkaḷum palāppaḻaṅkaḷum utirntu kiṭakkum. ivaṟṟiṉ cāṟṟiṉāl muṟṟiya kaṭṭeḷivai nīreṉak karuti mayilkaḷ uṇṇum; uṇṭu ūrkaḷiṉ naṭuvē koṇṭāṭum viḻākkaḷil vāttiyaṅkaḷ olippak kayiṟṟilē ēṟi āṭukiṉṟa makaḷir tāḷattiṉāl taḷarumāṟu pōlat taḷarum. ivviyalpiṉa tākiya malai nāṭṭaiyuṭaiyavaṉ tāṉ muyaṅkutalāl ivaḷ uḷḷattu virumpuvatu mēl varaintu koṇṭu illaṟam naṭattuvatāyirukku meṉṟu uṭkoṇṭāṉ; palarum vantu uṇṇumpaṭi akaṉṟa katavu tiṟantu kiṭakkiṉṟa vāyilaiyuṭaiya celvamikka nakariṭattu miṭākkaḷil varuvārk kellām iṭumpaṭi cōṟu camaikkappeṟum. ivvāṟu celvamikka maṉai polivu peṟumpaṭi uyarnta ematu cuṟṟattiṉar viruntuṇṭu eñciya niṇamoḻukum neymikka cōṟṟai nī iṭa nāṉuṇṇal uyarntatākum” eṉṟu kūṟiṉāṉ.
mālaik kālam
avaṉ malaiyiṭattuḷḷa murukaṉaik kai toḻutu piriyēṉ eṉakkūṟi avviṭattuḷḷa teḷinta aruvi nīraik kuṭittuc cūḷuṟavu ceytāṉ. tēvarkaḷum virumpum kukaikaḷiluḷḷa pūñcōlaiyilē kaḷiṟu kūṭṭiṉa kūṭṭattai aṉṟaip pakaṟ poḻutellām pōkkiṉāṉ. ñāyiṟu ēḻu kutirai pūṇṭi tērai yēṟip pakaṟ kālam kaḻiyumpaṭi mēṟku malaiyil maṟaintatu. māṉ kūṭṭam marattaṭiyiṟ tiraṇṭatu. pacukkūṭṭaṅkaḷ kaṉṟukaḷai aḻaikkum kuraluṭaiyaṉavāy maṉṟukaḷ niṟaiyumpaṭi pukuntaṉa. kompu pōṉṟa vaḷainta vāyaiyuṭaiya aṉṟil uyarnta periya paṉaiyiṉ uḷ maṭalilēyiruntu pēṭṭai aḻaittatu. pāmpu tāṉ mēytal kāraṇamākat taṉṉiṭattuḷḷa māṇikkattai īṉṟatu. iṭaiyar pala iṭaṅkaḷiṉiṉṟum āmpal eṉṉum paṇṇiṉaiyuṭaiya aḻakiya iṉiya kuḻaliṭattut teḷinta ōcaiyaip palakālum parappiṉar. āmpaliṉ aḻakiya itaḻkaḷ muṟukkaviḻntaṉa. pārppārantik kālattuc ceyyum toḻilkaḷaic ceytaṉar. celvamuṭaiya maṉaikaḷil vaḷaikaḷai aṇinta makaḷir viḷakkai ēṟṟi antikkālak kaṭamaikaḷaic ceytaṉar. kāṭṭil vāḻvār viṇṇaittīṇṭum paraṇiliruntu tīkkaṭai kōlālē neruppaip piṟappittu erittaṉar. mēkam periya malaiyiṭattaic cūḻntu iruṇṭatu. kāṭṭiluḷḷa vilaṅkukaḷellām kalleṉṉum ōcai yuṭaiyavāy oṉṟaiyoṉṟu māṟik kūppiṭṭaṉa. paṟavaikaḷ kāṭukaḷi liruntu olittaṉa. ivvāṟu kōpitta vēntaṉuṭaiya pōraip pōla iruṇṭa mālaik kālam vantatu.
talaivi varaintu pōvatai virumpiṉāḷ
“viḷaṅkukiṉṟa pūṇiṉaiyuṭaiyāy! uṉatu cuṟṟattār uṉatu muṉkaiyaip piṭittu namakkuttara nāṭṭiluḷḷār ellōmum aṟiyum nalla kaliyāṇattaip piṉpu nikaḻttuvōm. kaḷavoḻukkattāṟ peṟum pēriṉpam peṟutaṟku iṅṅaṉam cila nāḷ oḻukiṉēm eṉṟu niṉaintu neñcu kalaṅkā tiruppāy” eṉa avaḷ neñcil varuttan tīrumpaṭi coṉṉāṉ; collip pacuvōṭu niṟkum kaṉṟupōla viṭāmal emmōṭu kūṭa vantu nammūr vāyililuḷḷa nīruṇṇum tuṟaiyil emmaiviṭṭu mīṇṭāṉ. accērkkai toṭaṅkiya an nāṭṭōṉṟiya viruppattōṭē ennāḷum avaṉ iravuk kuṟiyiṭattē varuvāṉ. aṅṅaṉam varum pōtellām ūr kāvalar kaṭukik kāttārāyiṉum, kōpamuṭaiya nāy kuraittatāyiṉum, nī tuyiluṇarntā yāyiṉum, nilavu veḷippaṭṭu eṟittatāyiṉum, mūṅkilai otta avaḷ melliya tōḷil peṟum iṉiya tuyilaip peṟātu pōvāṉ. nām ceyyāta kuṟiyaik kuṟiyeṉak karutit talaiviyai etirppaṭātu ceṉṟālum avaṉ maṟupaṭiyum namatu maṉaikku varuvataṟku veṟuppaṭaiya māṭṭāṉ. avaṉ iḷamaip paruvattaik kaṭantavaṉum allaṉ; naṟkuṇaṅkaḷ māṟutaṟkuriya celvac cerukkāl taṉkulattukkuriya naṟkuṇaṅkaḷil ennāḷum nīṅkiyavaṉumallaṉ. piṟar kūṟum paḻimoḻi mutaliyavaṟṟāl iravuk kuṟiyilē kūṭutaṟku varutal oḻukka maṉṟeṉṟu niṉaittu avaṉ varaintu koṇṭu illaṟam naṭattutalē nalloḻukkameṉṟu talaivi tuṇintāḷ. ataṉāl ivaḷuṭaiya matartta kaṇkaḷ kalaṅkiṉa. piṉṉar ivaḷ, avar varukiṉṟa malaiyiṭam puli, yāḷi mutaliyaṉa tuṉpam ceyvaṉavāyirukku meṉṟu niṉaittāḷ. appōtu avaḷ valaiyilakappaṭṭa mayilaip pōlat taṉatu nalam pōmpaṭi melintu, kaṇkaḷil vīḻkiṉṟa nīr nāḷ tōṟum mārpilē tuḷippak kalaṅkā niṉṟāḷ. itukāṇ nalviṉai nikaḻnta vaṇṇam. (ivvāṟu tōḻi naṟṟāykku aṟattoṭu niṉṟāḷ.)
9. 1paṭṭiṉappālai
kāviri āṟu vaṟṟāta vaḷamuṭaiyatu
kuṟṟamillāta pukaḻaiyuṭaiya kāviriyāṟu mēṟkut toṭarcci malaiyiṟ ṟōṉṟik kaṭaliṭattē pāyum. veḷḷi eṉṉum viṇmītu tāṉ niṟṟaṟkuriya vaṭaticaiyil nillātu teṉ ticaikkaṇ cēriṉum taṉṉaip pāṭukiṉṟatum tuḷiyākiya uṇavai uṭaiyatumākiya vāṉampāṭi maḻait tuḷiyaip peṟātu pularumpaṭi vāṉam poyppiṉum poṉkoḻikkum kāviriyiṭattil nīr vaṟaḷātu. ivvakaic ciṟappamainta āṟṟiṉ karai yiṭattē cōḻa vaḷanāṭuḷḷatu.
maruta nilam
maruta nilattē oṉṟaṟkoṉṟu aṇmaiyiluḷḷa ūrkaḷuṇṭu. vayalkaḷ mikka viḷaivaik koṭukkum. āṅkāṅku iṭappaṭṭuḷḷa karup pañcāṟu kāyccum koṭṭilkaḷil eḻunta tīmpukai paṭutalāl vayaliṭattē yuḷḷa neytaṟpū vāṭum. cenneṟ katirai uṇṭu vayiṟu parutta erumaikaḷ āṅkāṅku kiṭakkum neṟkutirkaḷiṉ niḻalil uṟaṅkum. viḷaivu nīṅkāta akaṉṟa kaḻaṉikaḷil kulaitteṅkum, kulai vāḻaiyum, kāykkamukum, maṇanāṟum mañcaḷum, pala iṉa maraṅkaḷum, kulaikaḷaiyuṭaiya paṉaiyum, paranta aṭiyiṉaiyuṭaiya cēmpum, muḷaiyiṉaiyuṭaiya iñciyum neruṅki vaḷarum.
muṟṟattē ciṟuvar tēruruṭṭi viḷaiyāṭuvar
cīṟūrkaḷiṭattē maṉaikaḷiṉ muṟṟattu ularavaitta nellaik kōḻi tiṉṉātapaṭi oḷḷiya nutalum aḻakumuṭaiya ciṟu peṇkaḷ kāvalāka iruppar. avarkaḷ nellait tiṉṉum kōḻikaḷait tamatu poṉṉāṟ ceyta makarak kuḻaikaḷāl eṟintu ōṭṭuvar. kāliṭattē taṇṭai aṇinta ciṟār kutirai pūṭṭāmaṟ kaiyāluruṭṭum mūṉṟu uruḷaikaḷuṭaiya ciṟu tērkaḷai muṟṟattē uruṭṭi viḷaiyāṭuvar. ammarak kuḻaikaḷ attēruruḷkaḷai uruḷātapaṭi taṭukkum.
neytal nilattē nellēṟṟiya paṭakukaḷ kaḻikaḷil kaṭṭi niṟkum
cōḻanāṭṭiṉ pala ūrkaḷilum ceṉṟu umaṇar viṟṟa uppiṉ vilaiyākap peṟṟa nellai ēṟṟivanta paṭakukaḷ pantiyilē niṟkum kutiraikaḷaip pōlak kaḻicūḻnta neytal nilattē kaṭṭi niṟkum. putu varuvāyuṭaiya tōppukaḷukku veḷiyē pūñcōlaikaḷ kāṇappaṭum. accōlaikaḷiluḷḷa poykaikaḷil tiṅkaḷaic cūḻntu makam eṉṉum viṇmīṉ viḷaṅkiṉāṟ pōṉṟu maṇaṅkamaḻum paṉṉiṟa malarkaḷ nirampa malarntirukkum. kāvirippūmpaṭṭiṉattil immaiyilum maṟumaiyilum kāmaviṉpattaik koṭukkum ērikaḷ eṅkum kāṇappaṭum.
aṭukkaḷaiyiṉiṉṟum perukiya kañciyaik kuṭikkac ceṉṟa māṭukaḷ pōriṭum
uṇavu aṭum aṭukkaḷaikaḷukkup puliyiṉ vaṭivaṅkaḷ cetukkap paṭṭaṉavum palakaikaḷaip poruttic ceyyappaṭaṉavumākiya katavukaḷ uṇṭu. ataṉaic cuṟṟiyuḷḷa matiliṉ vāyilil tirumakaḷiṉ vaṭivam tīṭṭappaṭṭirukkum. aṭukkaḷaiyiṟ camaitta mikka cōṟṟai vaṭittalāl oḻukiya kañci āṟṟiṉ veḷḷam pōlap peruki ōṭum. ataṉaik kuṭikka virumpic ceṉṟa erutukaḷ oṉṟōṭu oṉṟu caṇṭaiyiṭutalāl atu maṇ kalantu cēṟāyppiṉ tērkaḷ ōṭutalāl puḻutiyāy eḻum; eḻuntu palavēṟu ōviyaṅkaḷ tīṭṭappaṭṭa veḷḷiya araṇmaṉai mītu paṭiyum. ataṉāl ataṉ cuvarkaḷ veṇṇīṟṟiṟ puraṇṭa kaḷiṟṟiyāṉaiyiṉ tōṟṟam pōla viḷaṅkum.
amaṇappaḷḷi
kuḷirnta ciṟiya kuḷaṅkaḷai uṭaiya muṟṟattaiyum, periya erutukaḷukku vaikkōliṭum pala cālaikaḷaiyu muṭaiya tavañ ceyyum amaṇap paḷḷikaḷum āṅkāṅku kāṇappaṭum.
ōmañceyyum muṉivar
viḷaṅkukiṉṟa caṭaimuṭiyuṭaiya muṉivar tām taṅkum iḷamarak kāvilē neruppil ney peytu ōmañ ceyvar. appukaiyiṉaik kaṇṭu mukileṉa añciya kuyiṟ cēvalkaḷ accōlaiyil iruttalai veṟuttut tam peṭaikaḷōṭum avviṭam viṭṭuc cellum; pūtaṅkaḷ kāvalāyiruttalāṟ pukutaṟkariya kāḷi kōṭṭattiṟ ceṉṟu kallaittiṉṉum puṟākkaḷōṭu ōr otukkiṭattē taṅkiyirukkum.
paratavar āṭṭuk kaṭākkaḷaiyum kavutārikaḷaiyum pōr ceyyaviṭṭu viḷaiyāṭuvar
paṉaiyilirukkum paṟavaikaḷ añcumpaṭi kal eṟikiṉṟa kavaṇkaḷai uṭaiyavarkaḷum valiya toḻilkaḷaic ceykiṉṟavarkaḷumākiya paratavar, iṟāl mīṉiṉ cuṭṭa tacaiyaiyum, puḻukkiya vayalāmai iṟaicciyaiyum uṇpar; vayalilē paṭarnta aṭampiṉ pūvai mālaiyākak kaṭṭit talaiyil aṇivar; nīril niṉṟa āmpaṟ pūvaip paṟittuc cūṭuvar. ākāyattē valamāka eḻukiṉṟa viṇmīṉkaḷaippōla akaṉṟa maṉṟilē kūṭuvar; āṭṭukkaṭākkaḷaiyum kavutārikaḷaiyum pōr ceyyaviṭṭu viḷaiyāṭip poḻutu pōkkuvar. avvāṟu pōr ceyvittaliṉālē māṟupaṭṭu eḻunta ciṉattāl vāraṭitta karumaṇaluṭaiya maṇal mēṭukaḷuḷḷatum mutiya kiḷaitta maraṅkaḷ niṟkap peṟuvatumākiya veḷiyiṭattē celvar; ceṉṟu kalliṭattē niṉṟu teyvamāṉavaṉaiyum avaṉukku vaitta kiṭukaiyum vēlaiyum pōlat tamatu kiṭukaiyum vēlaiyum ūṉṟi mutuku koṭāmal kaiyāl kuttiyum paṭaikkalaṅkaḷāl veṭṭiyum oruvar uṭampōṭu oruvar uṭampu muṭṭumpaṭi kalantu poḻutu pōkkākap pōr ceyvar. pākkaṅkaḷiluḷḷavarkaḷ ivvakaip pakaiyai allatu piṟapakaiyai aṟiyār.
paratavar cuṟākkompu naṭṭu varuṇaṉai vaḻipaṭuvar

kuṭṭikaḷaiyuṭaiya paṉṟikaḷum kōḻikaḷum ciṟiya kiṇaṟukaḷu muḷḷa cempaṭavar puṟac cēriyil cemmaṟik kaṭākkaḷōṭu kautārikaḷ viḷaiyāṭum. aḻakaṟṟa talaiyiṉaiyuṭaiya paratavar pūraṇaikkālattē kariya kaṭaliṭattē mīṉ piṭikkac cellātu maṉaikaḷil taṅkiyiruppar; paciya taḻai uṭaiyaṇinta kariya niṟattiṉarākiya tamatu makaḷirōṭu neṭiya tūṇṭiṟ kōlkaḷ cārttik kiṭakkum kuṟiya iṟappiṉaiyuṭaiya vīṭukaḷiṉ naṭuvē iruḷaippōla valaikaḷ kiṭantu ularum maṇaluṭaiya muṟṟattē kūṭuvar; aṅkuc ciṉaic cuṟāviṉ kompai naṭuvar. ataṉiṭattu ēṟiya teyvattai vaḻipaṭutal kāraṇamāka viḻutuṭaiya tāḻaiyiṉ aṭiyiṭattē niṉṟa veṇ ṭāḷiyiṉ kuḷirnta pūvāṟ kaṭṭiya mālaiyai aṇivar. maṭalaiyuṭaiya tāḻayiṉ pūvaic cūṭuvar. mēlē cora corappuṭaiya paṉaiyiliruntu iṟakkiya kaḷḷaiyum nellāṟ camaitta kaḷḷaiyum kuṭittuk kaḷittu viḷaiyāṭuvar. piṉpu ellōrum pulāl nāṟṟattaiyuṭaiya maṇaliṭattē pūkkaḷaiyuṭaiya kaṭaṟkaraiyiṟ kūṭi iruntu, teḷinta nīruṭaiya kaṭaliṭattē kalaṅkiya nīruṭaiya kāviriyāṟu, kāya mēkañ cūḻnta cekkar vāṉam pōlavum 1tāymulaiyait taḻuviya kuḻavi pōlavum kalakkiṉṟa oliyuṭaiya pukār mukattē tamatu tīviṉaipōkumpaṭi nīrāṭuvar. āṭiyapiṉ avvuppu nīṅka āṟṟu nīrilē kuḷittum naṇṭukaḷaip piṭittāṭṭiyum, parantu varukiṉṟa tiraikaḷaik kālāl uḻakkiyum maṇaliṉāṟ pāvaikaḷaic ceytum aimpulaṉkaḷāl nukarappaṭuvaṉavellām nukarntu mayaṅkuvar. avarkaḷ oruvarai oruvar nīṅkāta viruppuṭaiyarāyp pakaṟ poḻutai ivvakai viḷaiyāṭṭukaḷiṟ pōkkuvar.

peṇkaḷ uyarnilai māṭattē makiḻntiruppar
irākkālattē aḻakiya peṇkaḷ cuvarkkattai otta uyarnta māṭaṅkaḷiliruntu pāṭalkaḷaik kēṭṭum nāṭakaṅkaḷaik kaṇṭum, nilāviṭattē peṟum iṉpattai nukarntum kaḷḷuṇṭalaiviṭṭu uyarnta matuvakaikaḷaip parukiyum makiḻntiruppar. paṭṭāṭaiyaik kaḷaintu veṇtukil uṭuttut tam kaṇavaraic cērnta makaḷir kaḷḷiṉ mayakkattāl taṅkaṇavar cūṭiya mālaiyait tamateṉṟeṭuttuc cūṭuvar; kātalar taṅkātaliyar cūṭiya mālaiyait tamateṉṟeṭuttaṇivar. ivvāṟu iṉpam nukarnta āṭavarum makaḷirum kaṇṇuṟaṅkiya kaṭaiyāmattē māṭaṅkaḷil viḷakkukaḷ eriyum. vaikaṟaik kālattē kaṭṭumaraṅkaḷiṟ ceṉṟa paratavar māṭaṅkaḷil erikiṉṟa viḷakkukaḷai eṇṇuvar.

kālaiyil, akaṉṟa kaṭaitteruvilē palaruntoḻun teyvaṅ kaḷukkup pāṭumakaḷiriṉ pāṭalkaḷōṭu pullāṅkuḻal, yāḻ, muḻavu, muracu mutaliya vāttiyaṅkaḷ olikka viḻākkaḷ naṭaipeṟum.

araciṉariṉ kaṭaṟkaraip paṇṭacālai
veḷḷiya pūṅkottukkaḷaiyuṭaiya tāḻaikaḷ niṟkum kaṭaṟkaraiyi luḷḷa paratavar vāḻum akaṉṟa teruviṭattē kāvaluṭaiya paṇṭacālai yuḷḷatu. kappalkaḷil ēṟṟum poruṭṭu uḷnāṭukaḷiliruntu koṇṭu varappaṭṭa mikunta paṇṭaṅkaḷ cuṅkam matittup puli muttiraiyiṭṭup paṇṭacālai muṟṟattē aṭukkik kiṭakkum. marakkalaṅkaḷiliruntu iṟakkap paṭṭa paṇṭaṅkaḷ pulippoṟi iṭappeṟṟaṉavāyc cuṅkam matittaṟ poruṭṭup paṇṭacālai muṟṟattiṉ orupakkattē kuvintu kiṭakkum. ivvāṟu nilattiliruntu kaṭaliṟ parappuvataṟkum, kaṭaliliruntu nilattiṟ parappuvataṟkum kāttuk kiṭakkum paṇṭaṅkaḷ, mēkam tāṉ mukanta nīrai malaiyiṭattē corivataṟkākavum malaiyiṭattē corinta nīrai mīṇṭum kaṭaliṭattē parappuvataṟkākavum peykiṉṟa taṉmaipōl irukkum. araca ṉuṭaiya poruḷaip piṟar koḷḷāmaṟ kākkum paḻaiya pukaḻiṉaiyuṭaiya cuṅkaṅkoḷvōr erikiṉṟa kaṭiya kiraṇaṅkaḷaiyuṭaiya pakalavaṉ tēriṟ pūṭṭiya kutiraikaḷaip pōl oru kālum ōyviṉṟi nāṭōṟum kuṟaiyātu cuṅkaṅkoḷvar. malaiyiṭattē ulāvukiṉṟavaruṭaiya māṉ pōṉṟaṉavum kūriya nakaṅkaḷai uṭaiyaṉavumākiya āṇ nāykaḷum āṭṭuk kaṭākkaḷum paṇṭacālai muṟṟattē kiṭakkum mūṭaikaḷil ēṟik kutikkum.

aḻakiya makaḷir māṭaṅkaḷi liruntu murukaṉ viḻāvaiyum piṟa kaṭavuḷar viḻākkaḷaiyum kāṇpar

aṇukiya paṭikaḷuṭaiya ēṇikaḷ nāṟpuṟattum cārttappaṭṭa vīṭukaḷukkup periya vāyilkaḷum paṭikkaṭṭukaḷum naṭaipātaikaḷum uṇṭu. vāṉait tīṇṭum māṭaṅkaḷil civanta aṭiyiṉaiyum, tūcu pōṉṟa uṭaiyiṉaiyum, pavaḷa niṟattiṉaiyum, neruṅkiya tuṭaiyiṉaiyum, paciya āparaṇaṅkaḷaiyum, periya araiyaiyum, māṉpōl nōkkaiyum, kiḷipōl maḻalaimoḻiyaiyum, mayiliṉ cāyalaiyumuṭaiya aḻakiya iḷam makaḷir ulāvuvar; teṉṟaṟ kāṟṟu vīcum cāḷaraṅkaḷiṉ etirē niṉṟu aṅkāṭit teruviṭattē mātarpāṭa, pullāṅkuḻal ūta, yāḻ vācikka, muḻavu koṭṭa, muracu olippa viḻākkoṇṭāṭappaṭum. veṟiyāṭutaṟkuriya murukakkaṭavuḷaiyum, piṟappaṟuttaṟkuk kāraṇamāṉa palarun toḻun teyvaṅkaḷaiyum vaḷaikaḷ neruṅkiya ceṅkāntaḷ malar pōṉṟa kaikaḷaik kuvittu vaṇaṅkuvar.
aṅkāṭiyil koṭikaḷ nāṭṭappaṭṭirukkum kāṭci

illuṟai teyvaṅkaḷ makiḻumpaṭi vāyilkaḷil koṭikaḷ uyarttappaṭṭirukkum. kaṭaitteruviṉ irumaruṅkukaḷilum eṭutta tukiṟ koṭi kāṭṭāṟṟaṅ karaiyiṉiṉṟa aḻaku poruntiya karumpiṉ pūvaip pōṉṟu viḷaṅkum. tāṉiyaṅkaḷai nirappit tāḻiṭappaṭṭa tāṉiya aṟaikaḷilē nāṭṭiya koṭikaḷum tāṉiya aṟaikku muṉṉē cāṇiyāl meḻuki veḷḷariciyaip paliyākat tūviya kavinta pantariṉ kālkaḷil māṭṭiya koṭikaḷum acaiyum. pala nūlkaḷai muṟṟakkaṟṟa āciriyar piṟarkku meypporuḷ teruṭṭutaṟku nāṭṭiya accantarum koṭikaḷum, acaiyāta kampattai acaikkum yāṉakaḷaip pōlak kāvirippūm paṭṭiṉattuḷḷa tuṟaimukattē niṟkum marakkalaṅkaḷmītu eṭukkappaṭṭa koṭikaḷum viḷaṅkit tōṉṟum
.
vīṭukaḷilum kaṭaikaḷilum piṟaviṭaṅkaḷilum nāṭṭiya koṭikaḷ

kaḷviṟṟaṟku aṭaiyāḷamākak koṭikaḷ uyarttappaṭṭa kaṭkaṭai kaḷiṉ muṟṟattē cilar mīṉkaḷaiyum iṟaicciyaiyum aṟuttup porippar. ikkoṭikaḷōṭu vēṟu vēṟu koṭikaḷum, aḻakāṟ ciṟanta peruṅ koṭi kaḷum niṟaintamaiyāl avaṟṟiṉ niḻalālē ñāyiṟṟiṉ kiraṇaṅkaḷ vīti yil nuḻaiya māṭṭāvāyiṉa.

pala nāṭukaḷiṉiṉṟum koṇṭuvanta paṇṭaṅkaḷ kaṭaivītiyil kuvintu kiṭakkum

kaṭavuḷaratu pātukāvaliṉālē, kāṟṟiṉ utaviyāl ōṭum marakkalaṅkaḷiṟ koṇṭuvanta kutiraikaḷum, miḷaku potikaḷum, mēruvilē piṟanta māṇikkamum poṉṉum, potiyiṉ malaiyiṟ piṟanta cantaṉamum akilum, teṉṟicaik kaṭaliṟ piṟanta muttum, kīḻtticaik kaṭaliṟ piṟanta pavaḷamum, kaṅkaiyāṟṟil uṇṭākiya poruḷum, parmā tēcattiluṇṭāṉa nukarum poruḷkaḷum, cīṉam mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷiṉiṉṟum vanta kaṟpūram paṉinīr mutaliyaṉavum, kāviriyiluṇṭāṉa poruḷkaḷum, ilaṅkaiyiluṇṭāṉa uṇavup poruḷkaḷum ivaiyallāta piṟaporuḷ kaḷum pukārnakarilum tuṟaimukattum kuvikaiyiṉāl terukkaḷ matit taṟiyamuṭiyāta celvamuṭaiyaṉavāy viḷaṅkum. paṟpala nāṭukaḷiṉiṉṟum pōnta palavēṟu moḻikaḷaip pēcum makkaḷ kāvirippūmpaṭṭiṉattē taṅkiyiruppar.

vēḷāṇ makkaḷiṉ aruḷ oḻukkam

valaiñar muṟṟattē mīṉkaḷ pāyntu tiriyum. ūṉ viṟpār kuṭicaiyilē āṭu mutaliya vilaṅkukaḷ tiraṇṭu niṟkum. aṟat toḻil muṭṭuppaṭāta ilvāḻkkaiyuṭaiya vēḷāṇ makkaḷ ivvāṟu mutaṟkaṇ makkaḷiṭaiyē kolait toḻilaip pōkkiṉar. piṉ kaḷavu ceyvārai akkaḷaviṉiṉṟum vilakkiṉar. iṉṉum antaṇarkkuṇṭākum pukaḻiṉai nilai niṟuttiṉar. periya puṇṇiyaṅkaḷaippaṇṇi avaṟṟaic ceyya māṭṭātārkkut tāṉaṅkaḷai īntaṉar; arici, kaṟi mutaliyavaṟṟai avarkku uṇṇa aḷittaṉar.
vaṇikariṉ iyalpukaḷ
vaṇikar, mēḻit toḻiluṭaiya uḻavaratu nukattila taitta pakalāṇi pōla naṭuvunilai yeṉṉuṅ kuṇam nilaipeṟṟa neñciṉaiyuṭaiyavar; tammuṭaiya pala carakkukaḷaiyum piṟa carakkukaḷaiyum koḷmutalukēṟpa ilāpattai ārāyntu pārttut tam kuṭikka vaṭuvāmeṉṟañci mey colli viṟkumiyalpiṉar; tām koḷḷuñ carakkaiyum tām koṭukkum poruṭku mikaiyākak koḷḷār; tām koṭukkuñ carakkaiyum tām vāṅkum poruṭkuk kuṟaiyak koṭāmal meycolli viṟpar. avar kaḷiṭattē koḷḷappaṭṭa paḻaiya paṇṭaṅkaḷ mikkirukkum.
karikālaṉiṉ pukaḻmikka vīram mutaliyaṉa
kūriya nakaṅkaḷaiyuṭaiya puli kūṭṭilē aṭaipaṭṭu niṉṟu vaḷarn tāṟpōlak karikāṟcōḻaṉ pakaivaruṭaiya ciṟaiyiliruntu valimikumpaṭi vaḷarntāṉ. ciṟaiyiṉiṉṟum veḷiyēṟa virumpiya avaṉ, kuḻiyilē akappaṭṭayāṉai cuvariṉ karaikaḷai kompāṟ kuttit tūrttup piṭi yiṭattē ceṉṟāṟpōlak, kāvalāy niṉṟōrai vāḷāṟṟuṇittu av viṭattiṉiṉṟum akaṉṟāṉ. akaṉṟu taṉatu aracurimaiyai muṟaiyāṟ peṟṟāṉ. avaṉ, aṅṅaṉam peṟṟa aracurimaiyiṉāl niṟaivu aṟṟava ṉāy, muṭiyuṭaiya kariyatalaikaḷai uruṭṭukiṉṟa muṉ kālil nakaṅkaḷum, pakai aracariṉ matiṟ katavukaḷai muṟittu araṇai aḻitta kompukaḷum uṭaiya yāṉaikaḷōṭum, maṇikaṭṭiya kutiraikaḷōṭum pōr vēṭṭup pakaip pulañceṉṟāṉ. pācaṟaiyiṭattē pēyiṉ kaṇṇai otta muracu muḻaṅkiṟṟu; vāṉiṭattē paruntu ulāvumpaṭi pakaivariṉ tūcippaṭaiyai aḻittāṉ. mutalaikaḷ neruṅkit tiriyum poykaikaḷuṭaiyatum karumpuṭaṉ nellum kuvaḷaikaḷiṉiṭaiyē neytalum vaḷarappeṟṟatu mākiya maruta nilattēyuḷḷa kuṭikaḷai ōṭṭiṉāṉ. ataṉāl vayalkaḷum vāvikaḷum nīr aṟṟaṉa. avviṭaṅkaḷil kōraiyum aṟukum pūḷaiyum vaḷarntaṉa; āṟṟiṉ karumaṇal paṭinta kompuṭaiya āṇ māṉōṭu peṇ māṉ tuḷḷi viḷaiyāṭiṟṟu.
ampalattil nāṭṭiya aruḷtaṟi
pūkkaḷaic cūṭiya aruṭṭaṟi niṟuttappaṭṭa ampalam, ciṟaiyākap piṭittu vanta pakai nāṭṭu makaḷir nīrāṭun tuṟaiyilē ceṉṟu muḻukivantu meḻukiya meḻukkattiṉaiyum, antik kālattilē aṇaiyātu koḷuttiṉa viḷakkattiṉaiyumuṭaiyatu. ataṉiṭattē piṟanāṭṭavar palaruñ ceṉṟu vaṇaṅkuvar. avvakai ampalattuḷḷa tūṇkaḷ cāyntu kiṭakka avaṟṟil kaḷiṟukaḷum piṭikaḷum tamatu uṭampait tēyttaṉa. terukkaḷil pūkkaḷait tūvi mattaḷaṅ koṭṭi yāḻ olittut tirunāḷ koṇṭāṭappaṭum maṉṟaṅkaḷ viḻākkaḷai maṟantaṉa. avviṭattē ciṟiya pūkkaḷaiyuṭaiya neruñciṟ pūṇṭum aṟukam pullum neruṅki vaḷarntaṉa; akaṉṟa vāyaiyuṭaiya narikaḷ ūḷaiyiṭṭaṉa; aḻuṅkuralāyk kūppiṭum kūkaikaḷōṭu kōṭṭāṉkaḷumiruntu etir kūviṉa; āṇpēyt tokuti yuṭaṉē mayirai aviḻttut toṅkaviṭṭu iruntu piṇattait tiṉṉum vaḷainta kālaiyuṭaiya peṇ pēykaḷ kūṭiyāṭiṉa.
karikālaṉiṉ pakaivar nāṭukaḷ pāḻāyiṉa
aṭukkaḷai, viruntiṉar māṭattē nīṇṭa talaivāyilil kūṭi iruntu piṉpu uḷḷē ceṉṟu uṇṭapiṉ mikkirukkiṉṟa periya cōṟṟai yuṭaiyatu. ivvakai aṭukkaḷaikaḷ poruntappeṟṟa cāntiṭṭa vīṭṭiṉ uyarnta tiṇṇaikaḷilē paciya kiḷikaḷ iruntu iṉiya coṟkaḷaip pēcum. ivvāṟu ceḻumai mikunta nakarattē vaḷainta villaiyuṭaiya vēṭar ceruppiṭṭa aṭiyiṉaiyuṭaiyarāyt tuṭiyolippat tiraṇṭu ceṉṟu koḷḷai koṇṭamaiyāl vaṟitāyppōṉa neṟkūṭukaḷiṉ mēl kūkaikaḷ naṇpakaṟ kālattē iruntu kuḻaṟiṉa. ivvāṟellām ariya kāvalaiyuṭaiya matilaiyuṭaiya pakaivar vīṭukaḷ aḻakaḻiyavum avarkaḷ kulamiṉṟākavum nāṭṭaip pāḻ ceytāṉ. pala aracarkaḷ etirttu variṉum tāṉ oruvaṉāy niṉṟu pōrtoṭukka vallaṉeṉṟu vañciṉaṅ kūṟiya nāṟpaṭaiyaiyuṭaiya pāṇṭiyaṉai veṉṟa koṟṟattālum ivaṉ makiḻcci eytāṉāyiṉāṉ. ivaṉ teyvattaṉmai uṭaiyavaṉātaliṉ, im malaikaḷai ellām akaḻtalaic ceyvaṉ. kaṭalkaḷai yellām tūrt talaic ceyvaṉ; tēvarulakaik kīḻ vīḻac ceyvaṉ; kāṟṟai iyaṅkāmal vilakkuvaṉ; eṉa ulakattār mīk kūṟumpaṭi tāṉ niṉaittavaṟṟai anniṉaittavāṟē tuṟaipōka muṭikkavallavaṉāyiṉāṉ. ākaiyāl oḷi nāṭṭār palarum vantu tāḻntu oṭuṅkavum, aruvāḷa nāṭṭiṉaracar avaṉ ēviya toḻilaic ceytaṟku avaṉ coṟkēṭkavum, vaṭanāṭṭu aracar vāṭi niṟkavum, kuṭanāṭṭār maṉa veḻucci kuṉṟavum, pulliya iṭaiya aracaratu kiḷaimuḻutuṅ keṭṭuppōkavum, iruṅkō vēḷ cuṟṟattār aḻiyavum kāṭṭai ellām aḻittu nāṭākkik kuḷaṅkaḷ akaḻvittup palvaḷaṅkaḷum perukac ceytaṉaṉ. viḷaṅkum māṭaṅkaḷuṭaiya uṟaiyūriṭattuk kōyilkaḷaiyum kuṭikaḷaiyum nilaipeṟac ceytu, ciṟuvāyilum peruvāyilum amaittu matilkaḷil ampeyyum1 ñāyilkaḷil ampuk kaṭṭukaḷai vaittāṉ. ivaṉaip paṇiya vanta vāriṟukkiṉa muḻaviṉaiyuṭaiya maṟṟai aracar tam oḷimaḻuṅkap peṟārā yiṉar. avar ivaṉ aruḷ nōkkattiṟkut takkarākal vēṇṭi ivaṉaip paṇitalāl avar muṭikaḷiṟ patitta maṇikaḷ vīrak kaḻalkaḷ aṇinta ivaṉ kālkaḷil uraiñcum. oḷḷiya pūṇiṉaiyuṭaiyavaṉum ciṅkēṟṟaip pōṉṟu pakaivarkku varuttattaic ceypavaṉumākiya karikāṟ cōḻaṉatu mārpiṭattēyuḷḷa ceñcantaṉak kuḻampu poṟkāp paṇinta tam putalvar ōṭi viḷaiyāṭavum toḻil muṟṟup peṟṟa aṇikalaṉkaḷaṇinta maṉaivimāriṉ koṅkaikaḷ taḻuvavum, kalaintirukkum.
celvamikka kāvirippūmpaṭṭiṉattaip peṟuvatāyiṉum kātaliyaip pirintu cellēṉ
ippeṟṟiyaṉāṉa avvēntaṉ pakaivarmēl ōcciya vēlaik kāṭṭilum yāṉ pōtaṟku eḻunta kāṉam koṭiyatāyiruntatu. ivaṉ akaṉṟa melliya tōḷkaḷōṭu avaṉ ceṅkōliṉuṅ kuḷirntaṉavā yirukkiṉṟaṉa. ātaliṉ ivvakaic ciṟappuṭaiya kāvirippūm paṭṭaṉat taiyē eṉakkurittākap peṟiṉum neñcē! nīṇṭa kariya kūntaliṉaiyum viḷaṅkukiṉṟa pūṇiṉaiyu muṭaiya eṉ kātali īṇṭu taṉiyaḷāyiruppa ivaḷaip pirintu varutaṟku uṭaṉpaṭēṉ.

10. 1malaipaṭukaṭām
kūttariṉ vāttiya vakaikaḷ
ākāyattil muḻaṅkum ōcaipōl olikkum mattaḷam valittu iṟukkiya vārkkaṭṭuṭaiyatu. kañcatāḷam veṇkalattai urukkit takaṭākat taṭṭic ceyyappaṭṭatu. kompu mayiliṟakun taḻaiyuṅ kaṭṭap peṟṟatu. neṭuvaṅkiyam yāṉaiyiṉ tumpikkai pōṉṟu kaṇukkaḷiṟṟuḷai kaḷuḷḷatu. kuṟiya tūmpu iḷi eṉṉum narampiṉ ōcaiyait taṉṉiṭattē tōṟṟuviṟkum iyalpiṉatu. iṉiya kuḻal pāṭṭiṉ curuti kuṉṟāmaṟ mēṟ koṇṭu niṟkum. karaṭikai mattimamākiya ōcaiyaic ceyyum. ellari tāḷattaik koṇṭu olikkum. orukaṇ mākkiṇai māttirai aṟivikkum tāḷattaiyuṭaiyatu. ivaṟṟōṭu ciṟu paṟaiyum iṉṉum kūṟātoḻinta vāttiyaṅkaḷum iṭutaṟkuriya uṟaikaḷiliṭṭuc curukkiṭap peṟṟu, palāk kāyk kottukaḷ pōṉṟa tōṟṟattai uṭaiyaṉa vāyirukkum.
kūttar malaikaḷaik kaṭantucellutal
kūttar ivvakaip pala vāttiyaṅkaḷait tūkkiyavarkaḷāy kāṭṭuvaḻiyē cellā niṉṟaṉar. muṭceṭikaḷ vaḷarnta pakka malaiyiṭattē kaṟkaḷaip patittāṟ pōṉṟa aḻuttamākiya pāṟaikaḷ kiṭantaṉa. ap pāṟai kaḷiṉ pakkattē, nilattē kiṭakkiṉṟa vaḻiyai eṭuttu niṟuttiṉāṟ pōṉṟu malaiyai iṭittu amaitta ariya oṭuṅkiya vaḻiyuṇṭu. villiṭattē toṭutta ampiṉarākiya kāṉavar tamatu maṉaiviyaruṭaṉ aracar āṇai yāl avviṭattē kāttu niṉṟu vaḻiccelvorai āṟalai kaḷvar varuttāmaṟ pātukāttaṉuppuvar. cellutaṟkariya iv vaḻiyiṭattiṟ pōtalaik kūttar añcuvārallar.
pēriyāḻ
periya ōcaiyaiyuṭaiya pēriyāḻ peṇkaḷ kaiyilaṇiyum vaḷaiyal pōṉṟu oṉpatu vārk kaṭṭiṉaiyuṭaiyatu. nūlkaḷiṟ colliya ilakkaṇam amaiyumpaṭi vaṭittu muṟukkiṉa ataṉ narampukaḷ veṇciṟu kaṭukaḷavēṉum. koṭumuṟukku viḻātapaṭi uruvi ōcai ōrntu pārttuk kaṭṭappeṟṟavai. olittalamainta pattal varakiṉ katir oḻukiṉa taṉmai pōla nuṇṇiya tuḷaikaḷ ceytu tuḷaikaḷ nirampumpaṭi cuḷḷāṇikaḷ iṟukattaittu yāṉaikkompāṟ putitākac ceyta 1yāppai valipeṟa iṟukkappaṭṭatu; piciṉōṭu cērnta tōlākiya putiya pōrvai poṉṉiṟa muṭaiyatu. 2unti, taṉatu kūntalilē irunta vaṇṭukku kaliyāṇañ ceyta vaḷuṭaiya maṇattaik koṭukkiṉṟa maṭantaiyiṉ mārpiṭattē ceṉṟu maṟaiyum mayiroḻuṅku peṟṟa aḻakiya vayiṟṟai ottatu. vaḷaintu ēntiya taṇṭu kaḻāppaḻattiṉ niṟamuṭaiyatu. pāṇar, nūlmuṟaiyiṟ ceyyappaṭṭa yāḻai avaikkaḷattē aracar kēṭṭu makiḻumāṟu tuṟaipōkak kaṟṟu viḷaṅkiṉar.
pāṇaṉ kūttaṉai nōkkik kūṟutal
viṟalpaṭap pāṭiyāṭum makaḷiriṉ kalpōṉṟa ciṟṟaṭikaḷ malai yiṭattē ōṭi valikeṭṭa nāyiṉ nākkup pōṉṟaṉa. palavakai vāttiyaṅ kaḷuṭaṉ pāṇarum viṟaliyarum cūḻntiruppat talaimai peṟa virunta kūttar talaivaṉai, naṉṉaṉiṭattē paricil peṟṟu vantāṉoru kūttaṉ etirppaṭṭuk kūṟukiṉṟāṉ: kuḷattilē pukuntālotta kuḷirnta niḻalilē vaḻinaṭanta varuttan tīrntirukkum kūttar cuṟṟattukkut talaivaṉē! malai ucciyiṉiṉṟum iḻinta nalla pūkkaḷ ceṟinta āṟu kaṭalai nōkkic ceṉṟāṟpōla yāṉ naṉṉaṉai nōkkic ceṉṟu cila paricilkaḷaip peṟṟu varukiṉṟēṉ. paḻaṅkaḷaic corikiṉṟa kāṭṭil avaṟṟaic cuṟṟattōṭē ceṉṟu tiṉṉaṟku viraintu paṟakkum paṟavaittiraḷaiyoppa naṉṉaṉ makaṉākiya naṉṉaṉiṭattē nīvirum celvīrāka. avaṉ aḻakiya mālaiyai mārpiṭattē aṇintuḷḷāṉ. avaṉ, aḻakiya taṉaṅkaḷaiyum mūṅkil pōṟṟiraṇṭa tōḷaiyum, pūppōl kuḷirnta kaṇṇiṉaiyumuṭaiya makaḷirkkuk kaṇavaṉ. nāēr uḻavarkaḷākiya pulavarkaḷukku vēṇṭiya kālattup putup perukkāy vanta nīrpōlap poruḷaḷippāṉ; taṉ ākkat tiṉai uṇarum niṉaiviṉaiyum, viṟṟoḻiliṟ payiṉṟa kaiyiṉaiyum pēraṇi kalaṉkaḷaiyumuṭaiyaṉ. avaṉ tarum paricilkaḷ ivai eṉṟu ōrntu apparicilkaḷai virumpi nīvirum avaṉpāl celvīrāka. eṉṉai etirppaṭukaiyiṉālē nīvir puṟappaṭṭa nēram naṉ mukūrtattōṭē naṉṉimittamum uṭaiyatāyiruntatu. avaṉai aṭaitaṟkuc cellum. vaḻiyiṉ naṉmaiyiṉaḷavum, tīmaiyiṉ aḷavum, nīr taṅkum nalla iṭaṅkaḷiṉ taṉmaiyum, piṟanāṭṭārukkuk koṭukkum avaṉ nāṭṭil viḷaiyum uṇavukaḷum, avaṉ nāṭṭiṉ malaikaḷiṉ taṉmaiyum, cōlaikaḷiṉ taṉmaiyum, vilaṅkukaḷ virumpittiriyum kāṭṭiṉ taṉmaiyum kūṟuvēṉ.
naṉṉaṉ koṭaic ciṟappu
iṉṉum pakaivariṭattiruntum koḷḷai koṇṭa pēraṇi kalaṉkaḷai muṟpaṭa aṟivuṭaiyōrkkuk koṭuttup piṉ avarkkuc cori yum poṉmaḻaiyiṉ taṉmaiyaiyum. ikaḻntirukkum pakaivarai aracu koṭāmal curukkum aṟiviṉ valimaiyaiyum naviluvēṉ. cūtar, mākatar, pāṇar, kūttar mutaliyōrukkut tāṉ veṉṟa pakaiva raracaik koṭuppatō ṭamaiyātu attāṇi maṇṭapattē iruntu pala paricilkaḷai aḷippāṉ.
pulavarkaḷiṉ taṇṇaḷi
tām kaṟṟavaṟṟaic collum vaṉmaiyuṭaiya kaṟṟōr kuḻumi yirukkum avaṉatu avaikkaḷattē, vanta aṟivuṭaiyōr tāṅkaṟṟa vaṟṟai eṭuttu viḷakkum vaṉmai ilarāyiṉ akkaṟṟār avvaṟivuṭai yōriṉ māṭṭāmaiyai maṟaittum tām poruḷaic collik kāṭṭi ellōrum ēṟṟukkoḷḷumpaṭi aṟivittu naṭattuvar.
kaṭalcūḻnta ulakattavar añci naṭuṅkum nañcai ūṇāka uṭaiya iṟaivaṉ ‘navira’ meṉṉum malaiyiṭattil iruppāṉ. ñāyiṟu iruḷaik kaṭivatupōla naṉṉaṉ taṉ pakaiyākiya iruḷaik kaṭinta taṉmaiyai yuṭaiyaṉ. avaṉatu muṉṉōr, pakaivar tēyam tūrattiluḷḷatāyiṉum avviṭattē ceṉṟu muṉṉaṇiyiluḷḷa kālāṭkaḷaik koṉṟu yāṉaip paṭaiyiṟ ceṉṟu vēṟpōr purinta vīrarkku nāṭu ūr mutaliyavaṟṟaik koṭuppar. avaṉatu pukaḻ paranta paḻaiya ūr iraiyait tēṭi ulāvum vaḷainta kāliṉaiyuṭaiya mutalaikaḷōṭē tiraiyuṇṭāka āḻnta akaḻiyaiyum, malaiyai otta uyaramuṭaiya vāṉait tīṇṭum matilaiyum uṭaiyatu.
naṉṉaṉatu nāṭṭukkuc cellum vaḻiyiṭattē kāṇappaṭum kāṭcikaḷ
ippoḻutu nīvir naṉṉaṉaik karutic celkiṉṟa ticaiyaik kēṭpīrāka. nilattē iṭṭa vitaikaḷellām viḷaiyavēṇṭumeṉṟu āṇṭuḷḷār virumpiṉāṟ pōla viḷaiyumpaṭi mēkam miṉṉi maḻaiyaip peyyum. maḻaipeyta kollai nilattup pulliya koṭiyiṉaiyuṭaiya mucuṇṭai ākāyattil kārttikai mīṉpōl veḷḷitāka malarntirukkum. vayalkaḷilē muḷaitta pala kiḷaikaḷaiyuṭaiya eḷḷup payirkaḷ nīlamaṇi pōṉṟu viḷaṅkum. nīrccālai otta niṟainta cuṉaikaḷaiyuṭaiya kāṭṭiṭattē mikunta tuḷipaṭukaiyiṉālē eḷḷup payirkaḷiṉ taṭikaḷ muṟṟic civanta niṟattai aṭaiyamāṭṭā. muṟṟikkaṟuttu niṟainta ney yuṭaiya vitaikaḷaiyuṭaiya ēḻukāykaḷ oru piṭiyil aṭaṅkumpaṭi koḻut tirukkum. viḷaiyāṭip porukiṉṟa yāṉaik kaṉṟukaḷiṉ oṉṟōṭoṉṟu cērnta kaikaḷai otta katirkaḷaiyuṭaiya tiṉaimuṟṟi aṟukkum paruvattai aṭaintirukkum. tiṉai arinta tāḷkaḷil arivāḷiṉ vāyai otta kāyaik koṇṭa avaraiyiṉ tayirttuḷipōṉṟa pūkkaḷ utirntu kiṭakkum. erumai kiṭantāṟ pōṉṟa pāṟaikaḷuṭaiya vaḻiyiṭattē, tarkkik kiṉṟavaṉatu iṇaitta kai viralkaḷaiyotta iraṭṭitta katirkaḷ muṟṟi viḷainta varakiṉ tāḷkaḷai aṟuppōr arivāḷāl arivar. malar kaḷaiyuṭaiya kāṭṭiṭattuḷḷa tōrainelmuṟṟi avaliṭikkum paruvattai aṭaintirukkum. uḻātu maṇveṭṭiyāl kottiya tōṭṭaṅkaḷil vitaitta veṇ ciṟukaṭuku neruṅki viḷaiveytiyirukkum. ōviyaṉāl varaiyappaṭāta iyaṟkai ōviyam pōṉṟamainta iñcik kiḻaṅku uṟaippup peṟṟirukkum. koḻuviya koṭikaḷaiyuṭaiya kavalai kuḻi kaḷilē yāṉaiyiṉ maṭitta muḻantāḷaip pōṉṟavum māvuṭaiyavum kiḻaṅkukaḷai vīḻttum. malaikaḷaic cūḻntu neruṅki vaḷarntirukkum vāḻaikaḷiṉ pottikaḷ yāṉaiyaik kuttum piṭiyil iṟukkiya vēl kaḷaippōl malaip pakkaṅkaḷaik kuttum. neruṅkiya kāykaḷaiyuṭaiya vāḻaik kulaikaḷ paḻuttirukkum. nel muṟṟiya periya mūṅkilkaḷ acaintu koṇṭirukkum. maraṅkaḷ kālamaṉṟiyum nilanalattāṟ payaṉaik koṭukkum. kāṟṟu acaitaliṉāl nāval kariya paḻaṅkaḷaip pāṟaiyil utirkkum. viṭāyttakālattu vāy nīrūṟutaṟkuk kāraṇamāṉa uyavaik koṭi nīraip pōlap paṭarum. kūvaikkiḻaṅku nīṟu pūttu muṟṟik kiṭakkum. uṇpārai vēṟoṉṟiṟ cellaviṭāmaṟ taṭuttukkoḷḷum paḻaṅkaḷ māmaraṅkaḷiṟ ṟūṅkum. nīṇṭa aṭiyai uṭaiya palāviṉ paḻam veṭittu uḷḷēyuḷḷa vitaiyaic cintum. malaippakkattē pērāntaip pēṭuñ cēvalum māṟimāṟi alaṟum. acaikiṉṟa kiḷaikaḷaiyuṭaiya palāviṉ kīḻiṭaṅkaḷilum mēliṭaṅkaḷilum, vaḻippōkum kūttaruṭaiya mattaḷam pōṉṟa kāykaḷ tūṅkum. aivaṉa nellum veṇṇellum īṉṟakatirkaḷ pāl kaṭṭik kāṟṟālaṭipaṭṭu naṉṟāy viḷaintirukkum. iṉiya karumpiṉ taṇṭu pātti teriyātapaṭi tiraṇṭirukkum. ilaikaḷ kaviḻntu vēṟ paṭaikaḷ oṉṟōṭoṉṟu cārntāṟpōl kāṟṟaṭittalāṟ cāynta karumpukaḷ ālaiyiliṭuvataṟku aṟukkappaṭum. mikunta celvamuṭaiya putuvaruvāyai yuṭaiya ūrkaḷālē putiya taṉmaiyai aṭainta avvaḻiyiṉ taṉmai ituvākum. neruppai otta viḷaṅkukiṉṟa ceṅkāntaḷiṉ maḻai peyta lāl cerukki vaḷarnta mukaikaḷaik kapila niṟa mutukaiyuṭaiya kaḻuku tacai eṉak karuti eṭukkum; eṭuttu tacai allātapaṭiyāl avaṟṟaip pāṟai yilē cintumpaṭi pōṭum. appāṟaikaḷ neruppai otta pala itaḻkaḷ paranta veṟiyāṭukaḷaṉai okkum. maṇañceyta maṉaipōl parimaḷikkum periya malaip pakkattiṟ ciṟu kuṭiyiluḷḷa kāṉavar tēṉaiyum, kiḻaṅkai yum, tacai niṟainta kūṭaiyaiyum, ciṟiya kaṇṇaiyuṭaiya paṉṟiyiṉ iṟaicciyaiyum, maṟṟum tacaikaḷ niṟainta paṉai ōlaiyāl muṭainta kaṭakaṅkaḷaiyum, tammiṟporutupaṭṭa yāṉaiyiṉ kompaik kāvumara mākak koṇṭu tāviccelvar. vaḷaviya putuvaruvāyiṉaiyuṭaiya avar kaḷatu ciṟiya ūrilē taṅkuvāyāṉāl kariya periya cuṟṟattōṭē av vuṇavukaḷai uṇṇumpaṭi peṟukuvai. avaṟṟaip peṟṟu iḷaippāṟi aṟṟaināḷ irāppoḻutum avarkaḷuṭaṉē porunti avviṭattē taṅkuvīrāka. viṭiyaṟkālattē erikiṉṟa neruppuppōṉṟa oḷḷiya pūṅkottukkaḷaic cūṭic civanta acōka marattaiyuṭaiya nallavaḻiyē celvīrāka. ariya vaḻikaḷ iḻikiṉṟa mūṅkilolikkum cilampiṭattē yuḷḷa cīṟūrkaḷaic cērntu ‘veṟṟiyuṭaiya naṉṉaṉuṭaiya kūttar yām’ eṉṟu kūṟuvīrkaḷāka. malaikaḷiluḷḷōr nummai uḷḷē aḻaittut tūrattil niṉṟum vanta varuttan tīrumpaṭi iṉiya moḻikaḷ kūṟi neyyiṟ poritta iṟaicciyuṭaṉ tiṉaic cōṟṟai uṇṇumpaṭi taruvar.

malaiyiṉ ucciyilēyuḷḷa naṉṉaṉuṭaiya nāṭu yāṉ kūṟiya taṉmait taṉṟu; mikka ciṟappamaintatu. āṇṭup peṟuvaṉavaṟṟaik kēṭpīrāka. avviṭattē peṇnāy ōṭik kaṭitta uṭumpiṉ iṟaicciyōṭu kaṭamāṉiṉ koḻuviya tacaikaḷaiyuṅkalantu tiṉṟu iṭaiyē mūṅkiṟ kuḻāykkuḷ iruntu muṟṟiya tēṉāṟ ceyta kaṭṭeḷivaik kuṟaiviṉṟi nirampa uṇṇap peṟukuvir. piṉṉar nellāṟ camaitta kaḷḷaiyum uṇṭu kaḷikkumpaṭi peṟukuvir.

malaināṭṭār varavēṟṟu iṉiya cōṟaḷippar
naṟiya malaraic cūṭiṉa nāṟukiṉṟa kariya mayir muṭiyiṉaiyuṭaiya kuṟamakaḷ cuṉainīr koṇṭuvanta palāppaḻattiṉ veḷḷiya vitaiyiṉ māvaiyum puḷiyam paḻattiṉ puḷiyaiyum mōrukku aḷavākak kalappāḷ; kalantu mūṅkilariciyaic curapuṉṉai neruṅkiya maraccāral ellām kamaḻumpaṭi tuḻāvi ākkuvāḷ; ākkiya veḷḷiya cōṟākiya uṇavai, neñcu niṟainta makiḻcciyuṭaiyarāy viruntiṉaraip peṟṟēmeṉṉum ācaiyōṭē neñcu kalantu tattam piḷḷaikaḷaik koṇṭu muṟai collit taṭuttuttara, kaḷḷuṇṭu makiḻcciyuṟṟa nummuṭaiya matam tīrumpaṭi viṭiyaṟ kālattup peṟukuvir. iṉṉum uccimalaiyilē ēṟikkoṇṭu vanta viḷaṅkukiṉṟa paṇṭaṅkaḷaiyum peṟukuvir. aṅṅaṉam peṟṟatālē pōraic ceykiṉṟa valimaiyuṭaiya naṉṉaṉiṭattup peṟak karutiya paricilai maṟantu nīṭittiruppiṉum iruppir. niṟaitta itaḻaiyiṭaiya kuvaḷaiyait teyvam virumputalāl naṟumaṇam poruntiya pūkkaḷaik kiṭṭiṉum, āṇṭuvaraiyara makaḷiruṭaiya iruppaik kāṇiṉum vetumpi naṭuṅkavum peṟuvir. ākaiyāl pala nāḷ taṅkātu avviṭattaiviṭṭuc celvīrāka.

muṟṟiṉa tiṉaippuṉattaip paṉṟi aḻikkaiyiṉāl ataṟkañci vaikkappaṭṭa kaṟpoṟikaḷ vaḻikaḷiluṇṭu. ākaiyiṉāl ceṟinta iruṭ kālattē avviṭattiṟṟaṅkiyiruntu viṭiyaṟkālattē pōvīrāka. peritum palarum pōkāta vaḻiyē pōkat tuṇivīrāyiṉ mēṭṭu nilattēyuḷḷa kaṟ kaḷuṭaiya paḷḷattiṟ veṭippukaḷil pāmpu maṟaintu kiṭakkum kuḻikaḷuḷa. appaḷḷattaik kavaṉittu, maraṅkaḷilēṟi avaṟṟil aṭittu vilaṅku kaḷaip pārppīrāka. viṟaliyar pāmpu makiḻumpaṭi kaiyāṟṟoḻutu vāḻtta vilaṅkukaḷ kiṭakkum vaḻiyai akaṉṟu valappakkattē uḷḷa vaḻiyai numakku neṟiyākak koṇṭu celvīrāka. yāṉaikaḷāl tiṉaippuṉattai aḻiyāta paṭi kuṟavar uyarnta paraṇil ēṟi iruntu kāval kāppar; kāttu kuṟiya kāṭṭiṭattē niṟkum yāṉaikaḷ nilaikulaiyumpaṭi kavaṇāṟ kaṟkaḷai eṟivar. akkaṟkaḷ kūṟṟuvaṉaippōl uyir pōkkun taṉmaiyaṉa. avai kuraṅkum ataṉatu kuṭṭiyum ōcai aṭaṅkumpaṭi periya mūṅkiliṉ kōlaik kaṭantu vicaikeṭātu varum. avaṟṟukku maraṅkaḷiṉ piṉṉē otuṅki niṉṟu celvīrāka. kāṭṭāṟu valiya yāṉaiyai viḻuṅkum valiya mutalaikaḷ taṅkum irākkālattai otta iruḷ ceṟinta kāvaṟ kāṭṭaiyum, āḻnta nīraṟāta kiṭaṅkukaḷaiyumuṭaiyatu. avvāṟu akaḻiyilē iṟaṅki ṉāṟpōṉṟu ēṟutaṟkum iṟakkutaṟkum ariya taṉmaiyuṭaiyatu. avvāṟṟu vaḻi vaḻukkum karaiyuṭaiyatu. avvaḻiyiṟ cellum pōtu cemmaṟi yāṭṭai yotta cempaṭṭai mayiruṭaiya num piḷḷaikaḷōṭē maraṅ kaḷiṟ cuṟṟik kiṭakkum koṭikaḷaip piṭittuk kāl vaḻukkātapaṭi oruvarai oruvar pātukāttuc celvīrāka. acaiyum cevverukku vaḷarum pakka malaiyil vīḻntōraik kollum āḻnta kuḷaṅkaḷukku arukē nilattai maṟaittup pāciyirukkum. ataṉiṭattē mitikkiṉ ūṉṟiya aṭiyaic caṟukkappaṇṇum. ataṉāl avviṭam pōtaṟku arumaiyuṭaiyatu. ataṟkup parikāramāka vaḻi muḻuvatum piṉṉi vaḷarnta nuṇṇiya kōl kaḷaiyuṭaiya ciṟu mūṅkilōṭē koṟukkaip pullaiyum paṟṟuk kōṭākap piṭittup pōvīrāka.

mukapaṭāttaiyum, neṭumaiyaiyumuṭaiya malaipōlum pōrk kaḷiṟṟiṉaiyum, maḻaipōl villiṭattiṉiṉṟum puṟappaṭum ampiṉai yuṭaiya tūcippaṭaiyaiyum peṟṟup polivu eytiya paḻaiya matil uṭaiya ūriṭattup pūkkaḷaiyuṭaiya maṭuvuṭaiya āṟṟaṅkaraiyil ōr vaḻiyuṇṭu. ataṉiṭattē tutittaṟ kariya muṟaimaiyiṉaiyuṭaiya kaṭavuḷaik kaṇṭīrāyiṉ nīvir vaṇaṅkip pōtalallatu vācciyaṅkaḷai oliyātirup pīrāka. ataṟkuk kāraṇam yāteṉil, avaṉatu vaḷaviya navira meṉṉum malai mayaṅkiya tuḷiyaiyuṭaiya maḻaiyai iṭaiviṭāmaṟ peyyum. ataṉāl vācciyaṅkaḷ naṉaintu numakkup pōtal aritākum
navira malaiyiṭattē kāṭṭuvaḷam

akkāṭṭiṭattē mayil kalāpattiṉatu pārattālē āṭi yiḷaittu niṟkum. neṭiya mūṅkiliṉ kavaṭukaḷilē kūttaruṭaiya piḷḷaikaḷai otta kaṭuvaṉ pāyum. teyvam virumpum neṭiya pakkamalaiyilē tēṉ kūṭukaḷ kāṇappaṭum. avaṟṟai viraintu pārātu vaḻiyē celvīrāka. avvāṟu ceyyāviṭiṉ nīvir vaḻitappi viṭutalkūṭum. malaiyaic cērnta vaḻiyiṉai yuṭaiya kāṭṭiṭattē cellumiṭattu, kāṉavar yāṉai mutaliyavaṟṟukku eṭṭāta paraṇiliruntu eytataṉāl mārpilē puṇpaṭṭu ampuṭaṉ vaḻiyaik kaṭantu ceṉṟu vīḻnta tēynta kompiṉaiyum veḷḷiya niṇattaiyum coracorappuḷḷa kaḻuttaiyum, kariya niṟattaiyumuṭaiya paṉṟiyaik kāṇpīrkaḷ. kaṇṭu mūṅkiliṭattup piṟanta tīyiṉāl ataivāṭṭi, mayir pōkac cīvittiṉṟu kuvaḷaivaḷarum paciya cuṉaiyil paḷiṅku pōṉṟa teḷinta nīrai vaḻiyiṭattu iḷaipput tīrumpaṭi uṇpīrāka. paruttatacai muḻuvataiyum uṇṇāmal mītiyaip potiyākak kaṭṭik koṇṭu 1vaḷai kaiyiṉiṉṟum nīṅkutaṟkuk kāraṇamāyirunta piḷḷai kaḷuṭaṉē avviṭattiṉiṟum pōkātu nummillilē pukkā lotta malai muḻaiñcukaḷil irākkālattut taṅkuvīrāka. irākkālattē ellōruṅ kūṭi iḷaippāṟi viṭiyaṟkālattē tuyileḻuntu nalla kāṭṭuvaḻiyē celvīrāka. irākkālattē ellōruṅ kūṭi iḷaippāṟi viṭiyaṟkālattē tuyileḻuntu nalla kāṭṭuvaḻiyē celvīrāka. akaṉṟa paṭattiṉaiyum, aḻakiya kaṇṇiṉaiyum, valimikunta ciṉamuṭaiya yāṉaiyai viḻuṅkat takka perumaiyaiyum perumaraṅkaḷai otta vaṭivaiyumuṭaiya perum pāmpukaḷ kiṭakkum vaḻiyai vilakic celvīrāka. tūrattē maṇaṅkamaḻ kiṉṟa pūvaiyum, uṇṭavar maṟantu uyirvāḻtalāṟṟāta paḻaṅkaḷaiyum teyvaṅkaḷaṉṟi māṉiṭar nukarār. ākaiyiṉālē nīṇṭa kāmpuṭaiya ap pūkkaḷaiyum paḻattaiyum kuḷattaik kaṇṭāṟpōlum kuḷircciyuṭaiya marak kūṭṭattaiyum, yāṉ kūṟiyataṉai niṉaittu avaṟṟiṉ kuṟikaḷai aṟintu iṭattiṉum valattiṉum pārttu avaṟṟai aṇukāmaṟ pōvīrāka. paḻaṅkaḷaiyuṭai kiḷaikaḷil palavācciyaṅkaḷoppa oli ceyyum paṟavaik kūṭṭattaiyuṭaiya ālamaraṅkaḷ niṟkum nāṭukaḷai mella aṭai vīrkaḷ. appāl niḻalmaraṅkaḷ neruṅkiṉa kuṟuṅkāṭiruppatāl veyil nuḻaiyātatum, irākkālattē vēṭṭaiyāṭuṅ kuṟavarum ticai teriyāmal mayaṅkukiṉṟa kuṉṟiṭattē celluṅkaḷ. ceṉṟu vaḻiyiṭattē pōkātu akaṉṟa pāṟaiyilē iruntu kalleṉṉum ōcaipaṭa nummuṭaiya vācciyaṅ kaḷai olippīrkaḷāka. iṉiya ōcaiyuṭaiya aruvikaḷuṭaiya ucci malai yilē kāṭṭik kāttirukkum kāṉavar palaruḷar. avarkaḷ, tuṟaitappi āḻat tilē ceṉṟu valiyaḻintu puṉalilē aḻuntukiṉṟavaraik kaṇṭu viraintu eṭuppāraip pōla nīvir ticaiteriyāta mayakkattāl aṟivaḻinta varuttam tīrumpaṭi ōṭivantu cērntu uṇṭaṟkiṉiya paḻaṅkaḷaiyum cūṭutaṟkiṉiya pūkkaḷaiyum taruvar. piṉṉar iṭaiyūṟu mikka vaḻiyiṭattu numakku muṉṉē nummuṭaiya cuṟṟattōṭē maṉamakiḻcci eytuvīrāka. pakkamalai putiyamāntar kaṇṇāṟ kāṇiṉum talaimayakkum taṉmaiyatu. ticai kāṭṭuvār kūṟiya ticaiyai aṟintu kuṟiyavum neṭiyavumākiya pakka malaikaḷai muṟaiyē kaṭantu, pūpparanta pala varikaḷaiyuṭaiya niḻalilē iḷaittiruppīrkaḷāyiṉ palavakai ōcaikaḷaik kēṭpīrkaḷ.

malaiyiṭattē āravāram

malaiyiṭattē kuraṅkukaḷ palāp paḻattait tōṇṭutalāl malaimuḻutum tēṉ nāṟum. ammalaiyiṉ ucciyiluḷḷa paṇṭaṅkaḷai vāṅkikkoṇṭu vicaiyuṭaṉ āravārañ ceytu varukiṉṟa aruviyil āṭum teyva makaḷir kaiyālnīr kuṭaiyuntōṟum nummuṭaiya vācciyaṅkaḷaip pōlat tāḷan terikiṉṟa iṉiya ōcai piṟakkum. puṉattup pukuntu tiṉaiyait tiṉṉum viḷaṅkukiṉṟa ēntiṉa kompaiyuṭaiyatum taṉṉiṉattaip pirintatum talaimaiyuṭaiya āṇ yāṉaiyai malaiyiṉ mēliṭṭa paraṇil iruntu kāval ceyyum kāṉavar piṭittaṟku virumpi vaḷaittalāl āravāramuṇṭākum. nīṇṭa aḷaiyil taṅkum muṭpaṉṟi taṉatu kūriya muḷḷāl eytu koṉṟavar poruṭṭu aḻukiṉṟavarkaḷiṉ āravāram orupāl eḻum. tam kaṇavar mārpilē puli pāyntu uṇṭākkiya neṭiya piḷanta puṇṇai āṟṟutaṟkuk kāvaleṉṟu karuti kariya kūnta luṭaiya koṭicciyar pāṭi oliyaic ceyyā niṟpar. kālaiyiṟ pūtta poṉpōluṅ kottuṭaiya vēṅkaip pūvaic cūṭutaṟku makaḷir pulipuli eṉṟu accamillāta āravārañ ceyvar. karuppamuṭaiya piṭikkuk kaḷiṟu kavaḷan tēṭik koṭuttup pātukākkaiyāl, ataṉ pōkkiṉaik kavaṉittu oḷintirunta puli pāyntatāka, appiṭi taṉ cuṟṟattōṭē malaiyiṭattil cattamiṭum. kuṭṭiyait taṉatu kaiyāl taḻuva maṟanta manti ak kuṭṭi eṭuttaṟkariya malaikkukaiyil vīḻntiṟantatākat taṉatu cuṟṟattōṭu ataṉai neruṅkiyiruntu tuṉpattāl āravārañ ceyyum. kuraṅkukaḷ ēṟamuṭiyāta malaiyiṭattil ēṇiyākak kūṭṭi naṭṭa mūṅkiṟ ṟaṭikaḷiṉ cuṇukkaḷil mitittēṟit tēṉeṭuppōr āravārañ ceyvar. aḻakiya vēliṉaiyuṭaiya naṉṉaṉukkuk koṭuttaṟku icciṟ ṟaraṇkaḷiṟ kaik koṇṭa poruḷ pōtumeṉak karuti aḻittaṟkuriya pakaivar ciṟṟaraṇkaḷai aḻikkum kāṉavar āravārañ ceyyā niṟpar. viṭiyaṟ kālattē kaḷḷiṉaiyuṇṭa kuṟavar māṉtōl pōrtta paṟai kalleṉṉumpaṭi olikka vāṉait tīṇṭum uccimalaiyiṭattē tam peṇṭirōṭu kūṭik kuravaiyāṭuvar. aḻakiya uyarnta tērkaḷ vaḻiyiṭattē ōṭi vanta taṉmai pōl kalliṉmēl varum āṟukaḷ muḻaiñcukaḷilē vīḻntu iṭaiviṭātu ōcaiyaic ceyyum. kāṭṭāṟṟiṉ nīṇṭa cuḻiyil akappaṭṭa koṭiya yāṉaiyiṉ kōpattait taṇiyap paṇṇip periya kampattilē ārkkum pākaruṭaiya āravāram orupāl eḻum. orupāl taṭṭaiyaip puṭaittup puṉaṅkaḷtōṟum kiḷikaḷai ōṭṭukiṉṟa makaḷir cattamiṭum āravāram eḻum. orupāl niraiyiṉiṉṟum peyarnta acaiyum imiliṉaiyuṭaiya iṭapamum, pōrttoḻilkaivanta marai āṉiṉ ēṟum, mullai nilakkōvalarum kuṟiñci nilak kuṟavarum cērakkūṭi veṉṟa tam veṟṟi tōṉṟa mutukiṭātu puṇ uṇṭākumpaṭi muṭṭip porum āravāram eḻum. erumaik kaṭākkaḷ kāṭṭu mallikaiyum, kuṟiñciyum vāṭumpaṭi porutu āravārañceyyum. palāp paḻaṅkaḷiṉ cuḷaiyai virumpiṉōr tiṉṟu kīḻē iṭṭa eñciya paḻattiṉ vitaiyaip payaṉpeṟutaṟkuc ciṟuvar kaṉṟukaḷaip piṇaittut tuṭuppup pōlum kāntaḷiṉatu kamaḻukiṉṟa maṭalālē avaṟṟai aṭittu āravārañ ceytu kaṭāviṭuvar. karuppañcāṟṟaip piḻintu cāṟeṭuttalāl ālaiyiṭattil ōcai eḻum. tiṉaiyaik kuttum makaḷiruṭaiya icaimikunta vaḷḷaip pāṭṭālum, cēmpaiyum mañcaḷaiyum naṭṭu vaḷartta piṉpu paṉṟi kiḻaṅkai akaḻāmaṟ kāppōr koṭṭum paṟaiyālum āravāra muṇṭākum.
yāṉ kūṟiya ivvōcaikaḷum, tiraṇṭa tāḻvaraiyilum uccimalai yiluḷḷa āravāramum, ivaiyallāta pala olikaḷum cērntu neruṅkukai yiṉālē malaiyākiya yāṉaiyiṭat tuṇṭākiṉṟa oli ticaikaḷeṅkum oliyā niṟkum. palaniṟam poruntiya mālaiyaṇinta makaḷiriṉ āṭaṟku ēṟṟa muḻaviṉ ōcaiyiṉāl ūriṭattuḷḷōr tuyilaṟiyār. ivviyal puṭaiya ūriṭattē koṇṭa tirunāḷaippōṉṟa kāṇṭaṟkiṉiyavaṟṟaik kaṇkuḷirak kaṇṭum, kēṭkappaṭuvaṉavaṟṟaic cevikuḷirak kēṭṭum uṇṭaṟkiṉiya pala uṇavukaḷaik koṇṭāṭi uṇṭum, makiḻvīrāka. makiḻntu namakku mēlum innukarcci uṇṭāvatāka veṉṟu virumpip paḻaiya uṟaviṉaraip pōlum muṟaimaiyiṉaiyuṭaiyayīrāy avviṭattē cilanāḷ taṅkuvīrkaḷāka. pōrttoḻil mikku naṭattalāl ulakam pukaḻum tirumakaḷ taṅkum mārpaiyuṭaiya naṉṉaṉatu iṭiyēṟu muḻaṅkum malaikaḷ tōṉṟum. avaṟṟaikkaṭantu celvīrāka. pakkamalaiyiṭattē viyappu mikka iṉiya yāḻaiyuṭaiya viṟaliyar kuṟiñciyeṉṉum paṇṇaippāṭa nīvirum āṇṭuṟaiyum teyvaṅkaḷaik kaiyālē toḻutu, ‘eṅkuṟai muṭittāl numakku ivai tarutum’ eṉṟu paravuk kaṭaṉ pūṇṭu vāḻttic celvīrāka.

naṭukal
piṉṉiya pañcupōlac ceṉṟu kārkālam varumaḷavum malaiyiṟ kiṭainta mēkam cūl koṇṭu kaiyiṉāṟ piṭikkattakka aṇmaiyil karumai peṟṟu viḷaṅkum. atu iyalpākat tūviṉālotta tuḷikaḷait tūvāniṟkum, ataṉāl viraivāka naṭantu cella muṭiyāta umatu cuṟṟat tōṭē kāvic cellum vāttiyaṅkaḷ naṉaiyātapaṭi kiṇaṟukaḷ pōṉṟa kukaikaḷiṭattil nuḻaintu pukuvīrkaḷāka. periya pāṟaikaḷ muṟintu tōṉṟu kiṉṟa malaikaḷai aṭaiyātīr. eṭṭātē niṉṟu kaṇṇiṉāṟ pārkkiṉum, avai kaṇṇiṉoḷiyai aḻakālē vāṅkikkoḷḷum. ipperiya malaiyil varuttattaic ceyyum kuḻikaḷiṭattup piṟakkum iṭaiyūṟukaḷ pala. maṇ ceṟinta muḻavait tūkkic cellum kāmarattai numakku ūṉṟu kōlākak koṇṭu akkuḻikaḷil viḻāmaṟ pōvīrāka. veyiluḷḷa vaḻiyiṭattē cella viruppiṉ veyiltāḻnta antippoḻutiṟ pōvīrāka. avviṭattē naṉṉaṉatu yāṉaip paṭaiyai uṭaiyavum porutuvanta aracar valiyaik keṭuppavumākiya araṇkaḷuṇṭu. avvaraṇkaḷiṟ piṉṉi vaittālotta koṭikaḷaṭarntatum ciṟu kāṭukaḷuṭaiyatumāki ciṟu vaḻikaḷ tōṉṟum. muṉṉē pōkiṉṟavaṉ etirē kiṭakkum tiraṇṭakōl mukattil aṭiyāmal muṉṉē taḷḷi vilakic celvāṉāka. avvicaiyuṭaiya kōl yāḻiṉ pattaraiyum muḻaviṉ kaṇkaḷaiyum keṭātapaṭi kāttu ataṉaik kaiyāṟ piṭittuk koṇṭu mella mellac celvīrāka. yāṉaikaḷ oṉṟuṭaṉ oṉṟu cērntu porutiṉāṟpōla neruṅkiṉa kuṉṟukaḷiṭattu maḻaiyiṉāṟ ceḻumaiyuṟṟa pala kāṭukaḷ uṇṭu. ēval kēḷātapakaivar pōril mutukiṭṭaṉar. ataṉaik kaṇṭa vañciyār paṭai veṟṟitōṉṟa āravārañ ceytatu. ataṉaik kaṇṭu poṟāta puṟaṅkoṭutta maṟavar uyir koṭuttaṟku itu nalla kāla meṉṟu nāṇattiṉāl mīṇṭu vantu uyiraik koṭuttaṉar. avarkaḷ pukaḻiṉai eḻuti naṭṭa pala kaṟkaḷaiyuṭaiya mutukiṭṭōṭiṉōrai ikaḻum pala vaḻikaḷ akkāṭṭiṭattē uṇṭu. annaṭukaṟkaḷiluḷḷa teyvaṅ kaḷukku viruppamuṇṭākumpaṭi numatu yāḻai vācittu viraintu celvīr kaḷāka. vaḻiyaṟiyāmaṟ tīya nilattē pōyt tirumpi vantavarkaḷum, iṉi varukiṉṟavarkaḷum avvāṟu pōy mīḷātapaṭi pala vaḻi kūṭiya cantiyil kaiyālē kūṭṭi muṭinta ūkampullai aṭaiyāḷamāka vaippīrkaḷāka. ivvāṟu porutu iṟanta iṉṉāṉ. eṉṟu avaṉ peyarai ulakam aṟiyum paṭi eḻuti marāmara niḻalilē naṭṭa vīrak kaṟkaḷiṉ teyvattaṉmai mikunta koṭumaiyāl akkāṭu piṟanāṭukaḷai ikaḻvatu pōṉṟatu.

ataṉiṭattuḷḷa palavaḻikaḷil naṉṉaṉatu pakaivaruṟaiyum koṭiya iṭaṅkaḷ palavuṇṭu. tam mūrkaḷiṉiṉṟu niṉaippiṉum talai naṭuṅkum curattu, tēṉ coriyum mālaiyaiyum koṭuttuk kavinta kaiyiṉaiyum taṉakkeṉṟu oruporuḷaiyum pēṇāta iyalpiṉaiyumuṭaiya naṉṉaṉai nāṭic celkiṉṟē meṉṟu kūṟuvīrkaḷāka. kūṟuvīrkaḷāyiṉ appakaippulam nammai otta paricilarkku, celvamikka naṉṉaṉuṭaiya uyarnta oḻukkat tiṉaiyuṭaiya paḻaiya ūrkaḷai okkum. ātaliṉ accamiṉṟi av viṭattē iḷaippāṟip pōvīrāka.

kāṭṭiṭattē puli pāyntu koṉṟa peṇmāṉai niṉaittuk kalai māṉ cattamiṭum; villiṉ ōcaikku veruviṉa civanta kaṇṇaiyuṭaiya maraiyēṟu kuṟuṅkāṭṭilē viraintōṭum. vēṟṟup pulaṅkaḷil ceṉṟu mēynta ēṟṟaiyuṭaiya niraiyiṭattuḷḷa caṅkupōl veḷḷiya pacukkaḷiṉ iṉiya pālai vaḷaiyaṇinta makaḷir maṉa makiḻumpaṭi niraiyaic cūḻntu kāval ceyyum iṭaiyar koṇṭuvantu corivar. avviṭattē celliṉ nīvir avarkaḷiṉ viruntiṉarākuvir. paṇṭam viṟṟaliṉāṟ peṟṟapala iṉa nelliṉaik kuṟṟiya ariciyaippōla kiṭāyuṭaiya cemmaṟittiraḷ veḷḷāṭṭuṭaṉē kalakkum; kalantu kalleṉṟa ōcaiyuṭaiya kāṭṭiṭattē kaṭalpōlolikkum. ivvakaiyāṉa āṭṭuttiraḷ niṟkiṉṟa kāṭṭiṭattē irākkālattē celvīrāyiṉ, iṭaiyar numakkeṉṟu camaiyātu tamakkeṉṟu camaitta pāṟcōṟṟaiyum pālaiyum peṟukuvir. iṭaiyar neruppai erittu, melliya talaimayirai aṭaitta mettaiyaip pōṉṟu, āṭṭut tōlait taittuc ceyta paṭukkaiyilē tuyil koḷvar. annerup piṭattē tuṇaiyāka irāttaṅkip pōvīrāka. aracaṉ āṇaiyāl nāṭṭaik kāttu niṟkum vēṭar kūṭṭattaik kāṇpīrāyiṉ naṉṉaṉai niṉaittup pōkiṉṟōmeṉṟu kūṟuvīrāka. avar kaḷḷaiyum kiḻaṅkukaḷai yum uṇṇumpaṭi numakku valintu taruvar. avaṟṟai nukarntu avviṭattē avar pōkac coṉṉa vaḻiyē celvīrāka. akkāṭṭiṉ taṉmai ivvakaiyiṉatākum. tēṉtuḷikkum melliya malark kottukkaḷaiyum yāṉai muṟitta taḷirkaḷaiyumuṭaiya āccāviṉ pūkkaḷaiyum maral nāriṉālē mālaiyākak kaṭṭic cūṭuvīrāka. parukkaik kaṟkaḷuṭaiya mēṭṭu nilattil ciṟu vaḻi maḻai peytu kuḷirukaiyāl avviṭattē taṅki niṉṟa teḷinta nīraik kuṭittu vaḻikkum mukantukoṇṭu pōvīrkaḷāka. ceṉṟu taṭikaḷāl teṟṟip paṇṇip pullāl vēynta kuṭilkaḷirukkum kuṭiyiruppiṉaiyuṭaiya akaṉṟa ūrai aṭaivīrkaḷ. avviṭattut taṅkuvīrāyiṉ vēṅkaip pūviṉ niṟattaiyuṭaiya avarai vitaiyaiyum mūṅkilariciyaiyum puḷi karaitta ulaiyilē corintu ākkiṉa kuḻainta puḷiyaṅkūḻaik kāṭṭiṭattup pakal vaḻi naṭanta varuttan tīrumpaṭi irākkālattē peṟukuvir. avviṭattē cila nāḷ taṅkuvīrkaḷāyiṉ iṟaicci kalanta neyc cōṟṟaiyum tiṉaimāvaiyum peṟukuvir. avaṟṟai uṇṭu kuṟaṅkoḷḷiyai erittup paṉi varuttātu nittirai koṇṭu viṭiyaṟkālattē nimittam pārttup pōvīrkaḷāka.

naṉṉaṉiṉ marutavaḷanāṭu
naṉṉaṉuṭaiya kuḷirnta maruta marattaiyuṭaiya nāṭu kāñci marattaiyum, nīrvantu porukiṉṟa āṟukaḷaiyum, melliya viḷainilaṅ kaḷaiyum, poḻilkaḷaiyum, iṭaiccērikaḷaiyum uṭaiyatu. ivvakai yāṉa ūrkaḷil oru nāḷ taṅkip pōkiṉum palanāḷ niṟpiṉum yāḻiṉ paṇkaḷai māṟi vācikkumiṭattu evvāṟu iṉitāyirukkumō avvakai yāṉa nalla pala poruḷkaḷai māṟi māṟi uṇṇumpaṭi peṟukuvir. naṇṭukaḷ āṭittiriyum vayalukku arukē uḷḷa mēṭṭu nilattē malai otta pōrkaḷaik kīḻē taḷḷik kaṭāviṭum uḻavar nellai aḷḷit taruvar. pakaṉṟaip pūvāṟ ceyta mālaiyaṇinta kaḷ viṟkum valaiyaruṭaiya makaḷir, valaivīcuvār koṇṭu vanta vāḷai mīṉṟacaiyuṭaṉ tūṇṭiliṟ piṭitta piṭiyiṉkaiyai otta civanta kaṇṇaiyuṭaiya varāliṉatu uṭukkiṉ kaṇ pōṉṟa tacaiyaik kalappar; kalantu acaikiṉṟa miṭāviṉiṉṟum vārtta nel muḷaiyālaṭṭa kaṭṭeḷivuṭaṉ viṭiyaṟkālattē kaḷaṅkaḷ tōṟum amaiyumeṉṟu kaṇṭōr maruḷumpaṭi muḷḷaik kaḻittu ākkiṉa koḻuppāl veḷḷiya niṟattaiyuṭaiya tacaiyuṭaiya veḷḷiya cōṟṟai num cuṟṟattuṭaṉē uṇpīrāka. uṇṭu eruttai aṭikkiṉṟa uḻavar ōcai yōṭē kūṭumpaṭi marutappaṇṇai vācittu iḷaippāṟip pōvīrāka. nellaṟuppōr koṭṭum paṟai yōcaikku añci erumait tiraḷaip pirinta kaṭā num mēl varuvataip pātukāttuk kuyavaṉ vaṉaikiṉṟa maṭkalattuc cakkarampōlak kumiḻi cuḻaṉṟutōṉṟum vāyttalaiyil oḻivillāmal ōṭum cēyāṟṟaṅkaraiyai vaḻiyākak koṇṭu celvīrāka.

naṉṉaṉatu vaḷamikka nakar
naṉṉatu paḻaiya mūtūr pala vaṇṭukaḷ olikkum kuḷirnta poḻilkaḷaiyum uyarnta matiliṉaiyum, tirunāḷeṉṉumpaṭi māntar poruntik kaṭaleṉak kāreṉa olikkum āravārattōṭu tiriyum periya māṭaṅkaḷ ōṅkiya aṅkāṭit teruviṉaiyum, pala kuṟukkut terukkaḷai yum uṭaiyatu. ivvakaittākiya avaṉatu periyanakar cēymaik kaṇṇaṉṟu. pala piṉṉiya vaḻikaḷaiyuṭaiya kōpuravāyilil pakaivariṉ kariya talaikaḷaip paruntukaḷ paṭintu uṇṇumpaṭi pōrai vellum vāḷaiyuṭaiya maṟavar kariya kāmpiṉaiyuṭaiya vēlaic cārtti vaittuk kāttu niṟpar. avvariya kōpura vāyilai aiyuṟāmaṟ pukuvīrāka. tūra tēcaṅ kaḷiṉiṉṟum vantu ampalaṅkaḷilē taṅkukiṉṟa kūttar mutaliyō rellām nummaik kāṇpar. kaṇṭu vellukiṉṟa pōraiyuṭaiya murukaṉaippōṉṟa naṉṉaṉiṭattup paricil peṟa vantāreṉṟu aṉpākap pārppar. pārttu nīvir taṉimaiyāy irukkum varuttam nīṅkumpaṭi tamatu viruntiṉarāy uṅkaḷai etir koḷavar. nīvir avviṭattē taṅkiyirup pīrāka. 

eṭṭu kāluṭaiya varuṭai
aracaṉatu talaivāyiliṉiṭattē marāmarattiṉ kīḻk kiṭanta āmāṉiṉ kuḻaviyum, yāṉaik kaṉṟum, vaḷainta aṭiyiṉaiyuṭaiya karaṭiyiṉ vāy tiṟavāk kuṭṭiyum, vaḷainta kāliṉaiyuṭaiya eṭṭuk kāluṭaiya varuṭaiyiṉ kiṭāyum, kīriyum, puli pāyntu koṉṟa maraiyāṉiṉ periya ceviyuṭaiya kuṭṭiyum, civanta nilattiṟ cukkāṉ kalliṉmēl tavaḻum āṇ uṭumpum, malaiyiṭattē āṭum mayilum, kōḻip pēṭṭai aḻaikkum kuraluṭaiya cēvalum; muḻavu pōṉṟa palāppaḻamum, iṉiya māmpaḻaṅkaḷum, maṇam poruntiya naṟaik koṭiyum, kāvik koṇṭu vanta nukampōṉṟa nūṟaikkiḻaṅkum, karuppūramum, māṇikkamum, puliyiṉāṟ kollappaṭṭa yāṉaikaḷiṉ muttuṭaiya kompukaḷum, kāntaṭ pūvum, tilakap pūvum, paciyamiḷakuk kottum, mūṅkiṟ kuḻāyil muṟṟiya kaṭṭeḷivum, mūṅkiṟ kuḻāyilūṟṟiya erumait tayirum, tēṉ coriyum iṟāṟkūṭukaḷaiyuṭaiya āciṉip palākkaḷ niṟkiṉṟa malaikaḷiṉiṉṟum piṟanta kāviri kaṭalilē mikkuc celkiṉṟa pukār mukattai yoppa īṇṭik kiṭakkum. vāṉait toṭum yāṉaikaḷiṉ ilatti kiṭakkum muṟṟattē aṇukuvīrāka. avviṭattē, maḻai muḻakkam pōṉṟa kaṇṇaiyuṭaiya muḻavum pullāṅkuḻalum olippa, viṟaliyar paḻaiya muṟaimaiyiṟ ṟappātu kaṭavuḷai mutalil vāḻttā niṟpar. piṉpu putiya pāṭum pāṭṭukaḷaip pāṭi ‘periya pukaḻuṭaiya niṉatu muṉṉōr marapil vanta pala upakārikaḷ iṟantārkaḷ. avarkaḷaip pōlāvatu niṉpēr ikkālattu ivvulakil naṭantuviṭātu ulakuḷḷaḷavum niṟkumpaṭi koṭaikkaṭaṉai muṭittalai yuṭaiyavaṉē’ eṉṟu kūṟuvīrāka. kūṟi avaṉatu veṟṟippukaḻai avaṉ aimpulaṉum taṉ vacamākumpaṭi pukaḻntu kūṟuvīrāka. nummaṉattiṟ ceṉṟa ēṉaip pukaḻkaḷai muṟṟakkūṟavum viṭāṉāy, “eṉmēluḷḷa viruppaṅ koṇṭu varukaiyiṉālē eṉṉiṭattē vantatupōtum, paḻivanta varuttam periyatu” eṉak kūṟuvāṉ piṉṉar paṭaittalaivarōṭē polivu peṟṟa celvattaiyuṭaiya taṉ maṉaiyiṉ muṟṟattē uṅkaḷai aḻaittuc celvāṉ. ceṉṟu 1camayattirukku maṇṭapattē yiruppāṉ. iruntu “uyarnta aracu urimaiyaiyum koṭumaiyillāta amaiccar mutaliyō raiyum, akaṉṟa nāṭṭiṉaiyum, uṭaiyarāyiruntum curuṅkiṉa aṟiviṉai yuṭaiyarāy irantu vantōrkku illai eṉṟu kūṟi maṟitta kaiyiṉarāyt tam peyarai niṟuttāmal iṟantupōṉa aracar malaiyiṉiṉṟum peruki ōṭukiṉṟa cēyāṟṟaṅ karai maṇalilum palarāvar. ākaiyiṉāl namakkut teyvam ittuṇai kālamiruveṉa ellaiyiṭṭa kālam pukaḻōṭē kaḻintu pōvatāka” eṉṟu niṉaivāṉ. piṉṉar koṭukka virumpiṉavaṉāy nummai iṉitāka nōkki, iḻaipōṉa viṭamaṟiyāta nuṇṇiya nūlāṟ ceyta āṭaikaḷai aḻukkēṟiya tuṇikaḷ kiṭakkum araiyilē uṭukkum paṭi taruvāṉ. avviṭattē neṭunāḷ taṅkuvīrāyiṉum nellaricic cōṟṟuṭaṉ peṇṇāy vēṭṭaiyāṭik koṇṭu vanta koḻuppuṭaiya iṟaicciyaiyum orunāṭ pōlap pala nāḷum taruvāṉ. malaiyiṉiṉṟum vīḻum aruvi veṟṟikkoṭi pōlat tōṉṟum malaināṭṭuk kuriyavaṉum maḻai pōṉṟa koṭait toḻilai yuṭaiyavaṉumākiya naṉṉaṉ, koṭukkak kuṟaivupaṭāta pala poruḷkaḷaiyum, tēraiyum, vēḻattaiyum, maṇi kaṭṭiya pacuttiraḷaiyum, poṉṉāṟ ceyta cēṇattaiyuṭaiya kutiraikaḷai yum nalkuvāṉ. nalkik kūttar talaivaṉākiya nī poṟṟāmarai cūṭavum viṟali poṉṉāparaṇaṅkaḷaṇiyavum ceytu mutal nāḷilē paricil tantu aṭutta nāḷ pōkumpaṭi moḻivāṉ.

ārāycci

caṅka nūlkaḷ
caṅka nūlkaḷ eṉa vaḻaṅkuvaṉa eṭṭuttokai pattuppāṭṭu eṉpaṉa. caṅkattiṉ iṟutik kālam ki. pi. mūṉṟām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ naṭuppakuti eṉa ārāycciyāḷar kūṟuvar. ikkāla ellaikkumuṉ ceyyappaṭṭa tiruk kuṟaḷ, cilappatikāram, maṇimēkalai mutaliyaṉavum caṅkakālat taṉavākum. eṭṭuttokai, pattuppāṭṭu mutaliya tokai nūlkaḷiṟkāṇappaṭum taṉinilaic ceyyuṭkaḷ tamiḻ nāṭṭiṉ vevvēṟu iṭaṅkaḷil vevvēṟu kālattum orē kālattum vāḻnta paṟpala pulavarkaḷāṟ pāṭappaṭṭavai. caṅkattiṉ iṟutik kālattum ataṉai aṭuttum aracar pulavarkaḷ nūl nilaiyaṅkaḷil akappaṭṭa taṉi nilaic ceyyuṭkaḷ poruḷ, aḷavu, ceyyuḷ eṉṉum vakaikaḷāl vevvēṟu nūlkaḷākat tokukkappaṭṭaṉa. tolkāppiyattukku ilakkiyaṅkaḷāka irunta paḻaiya nūlkaḷ iṟantupōṉamaiyiṉ piṟpaṭṭa ittokai nūlkaḷē ataṟku ilakkiyaṅ kaḷāka iruntu varukiṉṟaṉa. caṅka ilakkiyaṅkaḷ iṟantu pōkātu piḻaittamaikkuriya kāraṇaṅkaḷuḷ oṉṟu ituvākum.

pattuppāṭṭiṉ tokuppu
iṟaiyaṉār kaḷaviyalurai nakkīraṉārāṟ ceyyappaṭṭup pattut talaimuṟaikaḷākak kēḷvi vaḻakkil vantu piṉpu evarō oruvarāl eḻutivaikkappaṭṭa teṉpatu avvuraiyālaṟiyavarukiṉṟatu. ki. pi. 7ām nūṟṟāṇṭil vāḻnta aracakēcari pārāṅkucaṉ mītu pāṭappaṭṭa pāṇṭik kōvaiyiliruntu 329 pāṭalkaḷ ivvuraiyil mēṟkōḷāka āḷap paṭṭuḷḷamaiyiṉ ivvurai ki. pi. ēḻām nūṟṟāṇṭil allatu ataṟkup piṉ eḻutappaṭṭateṉat tuṇiyappaṭukiṉṟatu. ivvurai eṭṭut tokai nūlkaḷaik kuṟippiṭukiṉṟatu. pattup pāṭṭaik kuṟippiṭavillai. ākavē pattup pāṭṭu eṉṉum tokuppu ēḻām nūṟṟāṇṭukkuppiṉ ceyyappaṭṭa teṉak karutappaṭum.
paṉṉirupāṭṭiyal eṉṉum nūl ēṟakkuṟaiya ki. pi. 12ām nūṟṟāṇṭil ceyyappaṭṭateṉak karutappaṭukiṉṟatu. itil,

“nūṟaṭic ciṟumai nūṟṟuppat taḷavē
ēṟiya aṭiyiṉ īraim pāṭṭut
tokuppatu pattup pāṭ ṭeṉappaṭum” (384)

eṉṉum cūttiram kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. itaṉāl pattuppāṭṭu eṉṉum tokuppu ki. pi. ēḻām nūṟṟāṇṭukkup piṉṉum 12ām nūṟṟāṇṭukku muṉṉum ceyyappaṭṭa teṉṉum muṭivu uṇṭākiṟatu.
caṅka ilakkiyaṅkaḷil aintukkup pāratam pāṭiya peruntēvaṉār kaṭavuḷ vāḻttup pāṭiyuḷḷār. patiṟṟuppattu paripāṭal mutaliya nūlkaḷ muṟṟum kiṭaiyāmaiyāl avaṟṟukkumivar kaṭavuḷ vāḻttup pāṭiṉārō illaiyō eṉa aṟiyamuṭiyavillai. pattuppāṭṭukkuk kaṭavuḷ vāḻttuk kāṇappaṭavillai. peruntēvaṉār kālattup pattuppāṭṭu eṉṉum tokuppu iruntatāyiṉ avar itaṟkum kaṭavuḷ vāḻttup pāṭi yirukkamāṭṭārā? eṉṉum kēḷvi eḻalām. itaṟku pāṭiyiruppār eṉṉum viṭaiyē ēṟṟatākat tōṉṟukiṉṟatu. peruntēvaṉār ki. pi. 9-m nūṟṟāṇṭukkum 12ām nūṟṟāṇṭukkum iṭaiyil tokukkappaṭṭa nūleṉak kūṟalām. pattup pāṭṭu, eṭṭuttokai nūlkaḷukkuk kaṭavuḷ vāḻttu iruppataikkaṇṭa pulava roruvar tirumurukāṟṟuppaṭaiyē kaṭavuḷ vāḻttāka amaiyumeṉak koṇṭu ataṉai mutalāka vaittup pattuppāṭṭait tokuttārākalām.

pattuppāṭṭilaṭaṅkiya ceyyuṭkaḷaip pāṭiyavarkaḷiṉ kālam
caṅkakālap pulavar aracarkaḷiṉ kālaṅkaḷaik kaṇittuk kūṟuva taṟkuk kalleḻuttukkaḷō piṟa cāṉṟukaḷō kiṭaikkavillai. cilappati kāram cēraṉ ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉiṉ tampiyākiya iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷāṟ ceyyap paṭṭatu. kaṇṇakikkuk kal naṭṭu viḻā veṭuttapōtu ilaṅkaik kayavāku vēntaṉ cēranāṭu ceṉṟiruntāṉ. itu cilappatikārattil kūṟappaṭukiṉṟatu. kayavāku teṉṉintiyāviṉiṉṟum pattiṉi vaḻipāṭṭai ilaṅkaikkuk koṇṭuvantāṉeṉpatai ciṅkaḷa nūlkaḷum kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. ākavē cēraṉ ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉum ilaṅkaik kayavākuvum oru kālattavarkaḷākak koṇṭu ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ kālattum avaṉukku muṉṉum piṉṉum vāḻnta aracar pulavarkaḷuṭaiya kālaṅkaḷ kaṇikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivvāṟu koṇṭu kālaṅkaḷaik kaṇippatilum taṭumāṟṟaṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. orē peyaruṭaṉ vāḻnta pulavarkaḷum aracarkaḷum palarāvar. mutalil caṅkakālap pulavar aracarkaḷiṉ kālakkaṇippuc ceytavar āyirattu eṇṇūṟu āṇṭukku muntiya tamiḻar eṉṉum nūliṉ āciriyarākiya vi. kaṉakacapaip piḷḷai avarkaḷāvar. ivarait toṭarntu palar palavāṟu kālakkaṇippuc ceytirukkiṉṟaṉar. ik kālakkaṇippukaḷai yellām oruṅkē vaittu nōkkiṉ kaṉakacapaip piḷḷai avarkaḷiṉ kaṇippē ēṟakkuṟaiyac cariyāyiruppatākat tōṉṟum.

ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ kālam
ilakkiya varalāṟṟu āciriyar pērāciriyar kā. cuppiramaṇiya piḷḷai avarkaḷ cēraṉ ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ kālamum ilaṅkaik kayavākuviṉ kālamum oṉṟeṉak koṇṭu kālaṅkaṇittatilum pala cikkalkaḷ eḻukiṉṟaṉa. ākavē avar paraṇaril iruvar, peruṅkuṉṟūr kiḻāril iruvar, kaṇakkāyaril iruvar, iḷanākaṉāril iruvar iruntaṉar eṉak koḷḷavēṇṭiya iṭarppāṭu uṇṭāyiṟṟu.

kaṉakacapaippiḷḷai avarkaḷ kālaṅ kaṇitta pōtu ilaṅkaik kayavākuviṉ āṭcikkālam ki. pi. 113 mutal 135 eṉak koḷḷappaṭṭatu. itil cila tavaṟu iruppatākak koḷḷappaṭṭu mēṟpaṭi āṭcik kālam 171 mutal 195 eṉat tiruttappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ākavē kaṉakacapaip piḷḷai avarkaḷ kaṇittuḷḷa kālaṅkaḷ aṟupatu āṇṭukaḷ kīḻē varukiṉṟaṉa. kaṉakacapaip piḷḷai avarkaḷ kaṇittuḷḷa kālakkuṟippaiyē nām ovvoru pāṭṭiṉ aṭikkuṟippilum kāṭṭiyuḷḷōm. kīḻ varuvatu (tiruttappaṭṭa kayavākuviṉ kālappaṭi) kaṉakacapaip piḷḷai avarkaḷatum ilakkiya varalāṟu kāraratum kālak kaṇippukaḷākum.

pāṭalkaḷum-pāṭṭuṭait kaṉakacapaip cuppiramaṇiya
talaivarum piḷḷai piḷḷai

nakkīrariṉ kālam ki. pi. 225-ai aṭuttateṉak kūṟuvar pērāciriyar es. vaiyāpurip piḷḷai avarkaḷ.

cilappatikāram karikāṟ cōḻaṉaik kuṟippiṭuvatālum, cēraṉ ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉiṉ tāy karikāṟ peruvaḷattāṉiṉ makaḷ cōṇai eṉa mēṟpaṭi nūliṟ kāṇappaṭuvatālum, ilaṅkaik kayavāku vēntaṉiṉ tantai kālattuk kāvēri āṟṟiṉ aṇai kaṭṭuvataṟkuc ciṟaipiṭittuc cōḻa nāṭṭukkuk koṇṭu pōkappaṭṭa īḻanāṭṭavaraik kayavāku mīṭṭukkoṇṭu vantāṉeṉṟum, avaṉ teṉṉintiyāviṉiṉṟum pattiṉic cilampaiyum kaṇṇaki vaḻipāṭṭaiyum ilaṅkaikkuk koṇṭu vantāṉeṉṟum ciṅkaḷa nūlkaḷ kūṟuvatālum karikāṟ cōḻaṉiṉ kālam cilappatikāra kālattai aṭuttateṉak kaṉakacapaip piḷḷai avarkaḷāl koḷḷappaṭuvatāyiṟṟu. pattuppāṭṭiluḷḷa ceyyuṭkaḷiṉ pāṭṭuṭait talaivaruḷ karikāṟ peru vaḷattāṉē kālattāl muṟpaṭṭavaṉāvāṉ. ākavē pattuppāṭṭiluḷḷa ceyyuṭkaḷ muṉpiṉ oru nūṟāṇṭakaḷukkiṭaiyil tōṉṟiyaṉavākak karutak kiṭakkiṉṟaṉa.

kaṭiyalūr uruttiraṅkaṇṇaṉār karikālaṉaiyum toṇṭaimāṉ iḷantiraiyaṉaiyum pāṭiyuḷḷamaiyiṉ ivviru aracarum orē kālattava rāvar. ākavē perumpāṇāṟṟup paṭai, paṭṭiṉappālai, porunarāṟṟup paṭai mutaliya mūṉṟum orēkālattaṉavākum. nakkīrar pāṭiya neṭunalvāṭai yum māṅkuṭi marutaṉār pāṭiya maturaik kāñciyum talaiyālaṅkāṉattuc ceruveṉṟa pāṇṭiyaṉaip pāṭṭuṭait talaivaṉākak koṇṭaṉa. maturaik kāñci malaipaṭukaṭāttiṉ pāṭṭuṭait talaivaṉākiya naṉṉaṉ cēy naṉṉaṉaik kuṟippiṭukiṉṟatu. (618-9). ākavē neṭunalvāṭai, tirumuru kāṟṟuppaṭai, maturaikkāñci, malaipaṭukaṭām mutaliya nāṉkum oru kālattaṉa. nakkīrar kapilaraik kuṟippiṭṭiruttalālum (akam 78) piṟa kāraṇaṅ kaḷālum kapilar nakkīrar kālattukku muṟpaṭṭavarākak kāṇappaṭutaliṉ kuṟiñcippāṭṭu im mūṉṟiṟkum muṟpaṭṭatākum. mullaippāṭṭiṉ pāṭṭuṭait talaivaṉ yāreṉap pulappaṭāviṭṭālum ataṉ talaivaṉ talaiyālaṅkāṉattuc ceruveṉṟa neṭuñceḻiyaṉ eṉac cilar karutuvar. neṭunal vāṭaiyaiyum mullaip pāṭṭaiyum oppiṭṭu nōkkum pōtu ik karuttu valipeṟukiṉṟatu. avvāṟāyiṉ mullaippāṭṭum neṭunal vāṭaiyum orukālattaṉavākum. ciṟupāṇāṟṟuppaṭai ēḻu vaḷḷalkaḷaik kuṟippiṭukiṉṟatu. kaṭai ēḻu vaḷḷalkaḷiṉ kālam ki. pi. 100-kkum ki. pi. 300-kku miṭaiyil eṉṟu es. kiruṣṇacāmi aiyaṅkāravarkaḷ ‘kaṭai vaḷḷalār kālam’ eṉṉum nūlil kūṟiyirukkiṉṟār. ākavē ciṟu pāṇāṟṟuppaṭai iṟutiyiṟ ceyyappaṭṭateṉak koḷḷappaṭum.

tirumurukāṟṟuppaṭai piṟkāla nūl eṉṉum karuttu
tirumurukāṟṟuppaṭai caṅkakālattu vāḻnta nakkīrarāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa teṉavē vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. itaṉ naṭai caṅkac ceyyuḷ naṭaiyākavē kāṇap paṭukiṉṟatu. caṅkac ceyyuṭkaḷil vaṭa coṟkaḷ arukik kāṇappaṭum. caṅka kālattiliruntu kīḻnōkkic cellap piṟkāla nūlkaḷil kāṇappaṭum vaṭacolliṉ viḻukkāṭu atikamākum. itaṉaik koṇṭum pāṭalkaḷiṉ paḻamai oruvāṟu aṟiyappaṭukiṉṟatu. pērāciriyar cuntaram piḷḷaiyavarkaḷ tirumurukāṟṟuppaṭaiyil kāṇappaṭum vaṭacoṟkaḷaik kuṟittuk kūṟiyiruppatu varumāṟu: “camakkirutam tamiḻ eṉṉum iru moḻikaḷukkum potuvākiya tāmarai, maṉam, mīṉ, muttu, ulakam mutaliya coṟkaḷaiyum camakkirutac coṟkaḷ eṉak koṇṭu kaṇakkuc ceytatil, 317 aṭika ḷuṭaiya tirumurukāṟṟuppaṭaiyil aṭikku aintu col uṇṭu eṉa vaittuk koṇṭu pārttāl nūṟu tamiḻc collukku iraṇṭu camakkirutac col viḻukkāṭum kāṇappaṭavillai.”1

patiṉōrām tirumuṟaiyil nakkīrar ceytaṉavāka pala ciṟu nūlkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. in nūlkaḷaic ceytavar nakkīratēva nāyaṉār eṉavum aṟiyappaṭuvar. caṅka kāla nakkīrarum patiṉorān tirumuṟaiyiṟ kāṇappaṭum nūlkaḷaic ceyta nakkīrarum oruvarē eṉṉum karuttu mika aṇmai varaiyil iruntu vantatu. ākavē caṅkakāla nakkīraraiyum piṟkāla nakkīrarai yum toṭarpupaṭuttum purāṇak kataikaḷ eḻuntaṉa. ārāycciyāḷar caṅkakāla nakkīrarum patiṉōrān tirumuṟaiyiluḷḷa nūlkaḷaic ceyta nakkīrarum iruvēṟu kālattil viḷaṅkiyavarkaḷ eṉa avarkaḷiṉ ceyyuḷ naṭaikaḷaik koṇṭum avaṟṟil vantuḷḷa vaṭa col viḻukkāṭu kaḷaik koṇṭum kaṇittuḷḷaṉar. ārāycciyāḷarum oruvar tirumurukāṟṟup paṭai piṟkāla nakkīrarāṟ ceyyappaṭṭateṉa eḻutiyirukkiṉṟār. ik koḷkaikkut takka ātāram etuvum kāṇappaṭavillai. tirumuṟaiyiṟ kāṇappaṭum pāṭalkaḷaic ceytavarē tirumurukāṟṟup paṭaiyaiyum ceytavarāvar eṉṉum mayakkam uṇṭāyirunta kālattil evarō oruva rāl tirumurukāṟṟuppaṭai tirumuṟaiyil cērkkappaṭṭiruttal vēṇṭum. tirumurukāṟṟuppaṭai tirumaṟaiyuḷ cērkkappaṭutaṟku atiluḷḷa karuttukkaḷ iṭaṅkoṭā. intiraṉ, viṭṭuṇu, civaṉ eṉṉum kaṭavuḷar piramaṉaic ciṟai viṭuvikka murukak kaṭavuḷiṭam ceṉṟaṉar eṉṉum kūṟṟaic civa vaḻipāṭṭiṉar ēṟṟukkoḷḷa maṟuppar. tirumurukāṟṟup paṭaikku urai kaṇṭa nacciṉārkkiṉiyar mutaliyōr atu tirumuṟaiyi luḷḷateṉak kuṟippiṭavumillai. tirumuṟaikaḷ nampiyāṇṭār nampiyārāl vakukkappaṭavillai eṉṉum karuttum nilavukiṉṟatu.

tirumurukāṟṟuppaṭaiyil purāṇa campantamāṉa paḻaṅkataikaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟamaiyiṉ atu piṟkālattu nūl eṉak karutattakkatākiṉṟa teṉak kūṟuvar oru cārār. paripāṭal, kalittokai eṉṉum caṅka nūlkaḷil purāṇak kataikaḷ palavaṟṟaik kāṇalām. mēṟpaṭi nūlkaḷaiyē nōkkuvārukkuc caṅka kālattilēyē purāṇa campantamāṉa paḻaṅkataikaḷ tamiḻ nāṭṭil aṟiyappaṭṭiruntaṉa veṉpatu naṉku viḷaṅkum. purāṇak kataikaḷ kāṇappaṭutaliṉ tirumurukāṟṟuppaṭai piṟkālattatu eṉak koḷḷutal mēlum ārāyccikkuriyatu1.

kaḷaviyalurai nakkīrarāl ceyyappaṭṭateṉa avvuraiyiṉāl viḷaṅkukiṉṟatu. itu 7ām nūṟṟāṇṭil viḷaṅkiya oru nakkīrarāl ceyyappaṭṭateṉṟum avar camaṇa matattiṉar eṉṟum oru ārāycci yāḷar kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār. ikkaruttu ēṟṟukkoḷḷat takkataṉṟu. nakkīrar ceyta kaḷaviyaluraiyai uruttiracaṉmar kēṭṭār eṉappaṭutalālum, akkālattu marutaṉiḷanākaṉār mutaliya pulavarkaḷ iruntaṉar eṉac collappaṭutalālum, avvurai pala talaimuṟaikaḷākak kēḷvi vaḻakkil iruntu vantateṉappaṭutalālum ataṉaip piṟkālattirunta oruvar ceytār eṉpatu nampattakkataṉṟu eṉat tōṉṟukiṟatu.








As a rule however, the Aryas in adopting a Dravidian word changed it considerably in order to suit to their tongue, and whenever such a word was imperfectly understood of negligently reproduced, the change naturally became still greater. It is more than probablo that Samskrita borrowed a number of words also from Dravida tribes the dialects of which are unknown to its, so that in such a case it becomes very kifficult and even impossible to trace their origin-Dravidal Elements in Samskrita Dictionaries-Rev. F. Kettel, Kannada-English Dictionary.-P XV.

But most Sanskrit assume that every Tamil word which looks like a Sanskrit one must have been borrowed from Sanskrit by the Tamils. When the speakers of two different languages come in to touch with each other, the probablities are that each language will borrow words from the other. Thus the names of articles produced only in South India, such as pearls, pepper, cardamoms must certainly have been borrowed by Sanskrit from Tamil. Hence Sanskrit maricha, muktala are derived from Tamil miriyal or milagu, muttu, elam; there are other Sanskrit words borrowed from Tamil wantonly which Sanskrit scholars wrongly claim to belong to Sanskrit. i.e. nitram evidently derived from nir, min, for we cannot imagine that the Tamils were drinking water and eating fish for ages without names for these objects and deferred naming them till Sanskrit speakers presented them with names for them......Many such words can be rescued for Tamil from the hands of the Sanskrit scholars. Pre-Aryan Tamil Culture-P. 16. P.T.S. Aiyengar.

The greater number of the Sanskrit and Pracrita words in the Dravidian language were introduced by the Jaina writers. Some tatsamas however were introduced by the three comparatively modern philosophic schools: the Shaiva Siddhanta, the School of Sankaracharya and the School of Ramanucharya. Sanskrit words are said to have been introduced even before the time of Jains, but it is doubtful whether these are not ancient words common to both Aryan and Dravidian languages-Manual of Administration of the Madras Presidency: Vol. 1. p. 41-2.

paḻamai poruntiya cīṟiyāḻ pēriyāḻilē ‘māṭakam’ eṉappiṟkālattu vaḻaṅkiya muṟukkāṇi amaikkappaṭavillai. narampukaḷait tuvakkum vārkkaṭṭākiya tivavavu avai tammai valittal melittal ceytaṟkum payaṉpaṭṭatu.......kōṭṭiṉ mītu kaṭṭappaṭṭa tivavāṉatu kariya niṟamuṭaiya peṇṇiṉ kaiyilaṇiyappaṭṭa vaḷaiyal pōlavum vaṭivu peṟṟiruntateṉa aṟikiṉṟōm. kavaikkaṭai eṉpatu pēriḻayaḻiṉai niṟuttuvataṟkut takkavāṟu ataṉpiṉṉē amaikkappaṭuvatōruṟuppu. ciṟu pāṇāṟṟuppaṭaiyil aḷakam eṉṟatu pattariṉai.” (yāḻnūl)

1. Turning to the vocabulary of the poem we find but thirty Sanskrit words even counting such common words tāmarai, maṉam, mīṉ, muttu maṟṟum ulakam. Taking each line to have an average of five words the 317 lines of the poem do not show two per cent of Sanskrit words.
“The work of having compiled the eleven Thirumurais or Saivite works like Thevaram is attributed to him on the basis of Thirumuraikanda Puranam wrongly considered to be from the pen of Umapathi Sivam...The Puranam is itself in two different meters; the verses in the second kind of meter were Probably a later day addition. The first Part stops with the narration of the Thevaram compilation wheareas the second part mentions the eleven Thirumurais......It is very doubtful vhether these were compiled at any time....From the Poems of Nambiandar Nambi, one could easily conclude that whether he compiled the Thirumurai or not.....Tamil Pultruch P. 68-notes by Prof. T. P. Meenakshisundaram.
1. It is now generally admitted that a great deal of the ancient and mediaeval myth and legend enshrined in the Sanskrit epics and Puranas is of Non-Aryan origin and that even in Vedic mythology certain Pre-Aryan elements are present. Puranic myths of the gods and legends of kings, heroes, and sages, in the form in which we find them in the Sanskrit works represent undoubted by a considerable amount of modification from other original forms, whether Aryan or Non-Aryan......The Aryan speaking masses of Northern India became Aryanized in language and their tales and legends were retold as a matter of course in the Aryan language of their adoption. -Purana Legends and the Prakrit tradition in the new Indo-Aryan-S. K. Chatterji-Bulletin of the school of Oriental studies: VOL. VIII (1935-1937).

16
patiṟṟuppattu vacaṉam
mukavurai
caṅkat tokai nūlkaḷuḷ oṉṟākiya patiṟṟup pattuc cēra aracariṉ kīrttip piratāpaṅkaḷaik kūṟukiṉṟatu. cēra aracar palariṉ carittiraṅkaḷai aṟitaṟku innūl tuṇaipurikiṉṟatu. innūlaik kaṟpatiṉāl iraṇṭāyiram āṇṭukaḷukkumuṉ tamiḻ nāṭu evvakaiyāṉa nākarīkam aṭaintirunta teṉpataip paṭṭappakal pōla aṟintu koḷḷutal kūṭum. uḷḷatai uḷḷavāṟē kūṟum meymmoḻip pulavarkaḷ, āṟu malai cōlai kāṭu mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ vaḷaṅkaḷum, makkaḷiṉ naṭai uṭai pāvaṉaikaḷum, vēntariṉ māṭa māḷikai kūṭakōpuram, nāṭu nakaram, muṭi koṭi kuṭai āla vaṭṭam, tēr yāṉai kutirai kālāḷ, pōrkkaḷam pācaṟai ātiyaṉavum piṟavum namatu maṉak kaṇṇukku iṉitu pulappaṭumāṟu, tamatu pāṭalkaḷākiya tiraiyil, aḻakupeṟac cittirittirukkiṉṟaṉar. namatu paḻaiya tamiḻakattiṉ oru pakutiyākiya cērar ulakam paṇṭu irunta taṉmaiyai iṉṟu nam maṉak kaṇkaḷāṟkaṇṭu kaḷikūrtal evvaḷavu makiḻccikkuriya tākiṉṟatu.
patiṟṟuppattiṟ palavaḻakku vīḻnta coṟkaḷ kāṇappaṭutaliṉ annūlai eḷitiṟ poruḷ viḷaṅkip payiltal aritākiṉṟatu. 1innūl, uyartarap parīṭcaikaḷukkup pāṭamāka vaikkappeṟṟu varukiṉṟamaiyiṉ itaṉaip payilvōr mikacciramam uṟukiṉṟaṉar. innūl, parīṭcaikkup payilvōr ceyyuṭkaḷaip payilvataṟkut tuṇaiyāyirukkum poruṭṭu mūlattiluḷḷa oru collēṉum viṭupaṭāmal vacaṉamāka eḻutappeṟṟa toṉṟātaliṉ, vacaṉaṅkaḷaik kuṟukkuvatu iyalātāyiṟṟu. āyiṉum itaṉaip payilvōr patiṟṟup pattiṟ kūṟum ceytikaḷai aṟintu makiḻvāreṉpatil aiyamillai.
“kuṇanāṭik kuṟṟamumnāṭi avaṟṟiṉ
mikaināṭi mikka koḷal.”
tamiḻ nilaiyam
navāliyūr
1-8-1937. na.ci. kantaiyā.
ārāycci urai
caṅkat tokainūl eṭṭaṉuḷ patiṟṟuppattu nāṉkāvatāyuḷḷatu. innūliṉ mutaṟpattum iṟutip pattum kiṭaittila. kiṭaittuḷḷa eṭṭup pattukaḷum eṭṭup pulavarkaḷāl vevvēṟu eṭṭuc cēra aracariṉ vīram koṭai ākiyavaṟṟaip pukaḻntu pāṭappaṭṭaṉavākum. ippāṭalkaḷaip pāṭiya pulavarkaḷukku ac cēra maṉṉar mikunta poṉṉaiyum nāṭukaḷai yum paricilāka vaḻaṅkiṉar. patiṟṟup pattaip pāṭiya pulavarkaḷ ki. pi. iraṇ ṭām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ piṟpakuti allatu mūṉṟām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ muṟpakutiyil viḷaṅkiṉārkaḷ. tamiḻ ilakkaṇa nūvār kuṟippiṭuvatupōlac cēra nāṭu koṭuntamiḻ nāṭukaḷuḷ oṉṟu. innūlakattu ikkālam vaḻakku vīḻnta coṟkaḷum, col vaḻakkum, paḻaiya ilakkaṇa muṭipukaḷum taṟkāla malaiyāḷariṉ muṉṉōrākiya mēṟkuk karait tamiḻariṉ paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
kumaṭṭūrk kaṇṇaṉār, imayavarampaṉ neṭuñcēralātaṉ mītu innūliṉ iraṇṭām pattaip pāṭiṉār. iv varacaṉ utiyaṉ cēralātaṟku vēḷmāṉ nalliṉiyiṭam piṟantāṉ. ivaṉ kaṭuñcolluṭaiya yavaṉaraiveṉṟāṉ. ivaṉ ilaṅkai vēntaṉākiya mutalām kayavākuviṉ ki. pi. (169-191) kālattavaṉum ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉiṉ tantaiyumāvaṉ. ivaṉ 58 āṇṭu aracāṇṭāṉ. intac cēra aracaṉum kaṇṇaṉār eṉṉum pulavarum 2ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ, mattiya kālattavarkaḷāvar. ivaṉ veṇṇip paṟantalaiyil karikālaṉōṭu pōr ceytu puṇṇuṟṟu, (akam 55.) nāṇit tāṉkoṇṭa vāḷōṭu vaṭakkiruntāṉ. ivaṉ kaṭaliṟceṉṟu pakaivaratu kaṭampiṉai aṟuttu ataṉāl vīra muracam ceytāṉ. maṟṟorumuṟai vaṭanāṭu ceṉṟu āriyarai alaṟattākki, avar aracaraic ciṟaiceytu imayavaraiyil taṉatu viṟpoṟiyaip poṟittāṉ. icciṟanta vīraṉ pakaivarpāl ciṟanta poṉṉāparaṇaṅkaḷaiyum vayiram mutaliya irattiṉaṅ kaḷaiyum eṇṇiṟanta aḷavu tiṟaiyākappeṟṟatōṭu, oru poṟpāvai yaiyum peṟṟāṉeṉat terikiṟatu (akam 127). ivaṟṟai ivaṉ māntai muṟṟattiṟ kuvittu viṭṭup puṇṇukku nāṇit tuṟavu pūṇṭu vaṭak kiruntāṉ (akam 127).
mūṉṟām pattāl pālaik kautamaṉār palyāṉaic celkeḻu kuṭṭuvaṉaip pāṭiṉār. ippulavarai iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ ‘taṇṭamiḻ maṟaiyōṉ’ eṉakkūṟuvar. imayavarampaṉ tampi palyāṉaic celkeḻu kuṭṭuvaṉ teyva pakti niṟaintavaṉātaliṉ irupattain tāṇṭu āṭci purintapiṉ tuṟavu pūṇṭāṉ. ivaṉ kautamaṉār poruṭṭu neṭumpāratāya ṉār eṉṉum purōkitar vāyilāka pattu vēḷvikaḷ vēṭṭāṉ. pattu vēḷviyiṉ piṉ kautamaṉār maṉaiviyuṭaṉ maṟaintu tēvarulakam eytiṉar eṉṟu collappaṭukiṉṟatu. cilappatikāramum iccampavattaik kūṟum. ayiraimalaiyiluḷḷa kulateyvattai avaṉ alaṅkarittāṉ eṉṟuñ collappaṭukiṉṟatu.
nālām pattāl kāppiyāṟṟuk kāppiyaṉār kaḷaṅkāykkaṇṇi nārmuṭiccēralaip pāṭiṉār. iccēraṉ neṭuñcēralātaṟku vēḷāvik kōmāṉ makaḷ patumaṉ tēviyiṭam piṟantavaṉ. ivaṉ pūḻi nāṭṭai veṉṟu naṉṉaṉait tōṟkaṭittāṉ. ivaṉ cēralātaṉukkup piṉ cimmācaṉa mēṟiṉāṉ. ivaṉukkuriya iyaṟpeyar viḷaṅkavillai. ivaṉ cūṭutaṟkuriya muṭiyaip pakaivar kavarntamaiyiṉ kaḻaṅkāyālum nārālum ceyta muṭiyaic cūṭiṉāṉ; akkāraṇattāl kaḻaṅkāykkaṇṇi nār muṭiccēral eṉṉum peyar peṟṟāṉ. ivaṉ 25 āṇṭu aracu vīṟṟiruntāṉ.
aintām pattup pāṭiyavar paraṇar. ippattukkuriyavaṉ ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ. ivaṉ ilaṅkai aracaṉākiya mutalām kayavākuviṉ kālattavaṉ. akkālattirunta cōḻa aracar uruvap paḵṟēriḷañcēṭceṉṉiyum vēṟ paḵṟaṭakkaip perunaṟkiḷḷiyum; pāṇṭiya aracar: neṭumāṟaṉum veṟṟivēṟ ceḻiyaṉum. ivaṉ āriya aracarākiya kaṉṉa vicayaraik kaṅkaiyiṉ vaṭakaraiyil veṉṟāṉ; oṉpatu cōḻa aracarai uṟaiyūrukku aṇittiluḷḷa nērivāyilil tōṟkaṭittāṉ; mōkūr maṉṉaṉākiya paḻaiyaṉaip paṇivittāṉ. ivaṉ iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷiṉ tamaiyaṉum cilappatikārattuḷḷa vañcik kāṇṭattiṉ katānāyakaṉumāvaṉ. ivaṉ 55 āṇṭu aracu vīṟṟiruntāṉ. ivaṉ kālam ki. pi. 150-225.
āṟām pattāl kākkai pāṭiṉiyār nacceḷḷaiyār āṭu kōṭpāṭṭuc cēralātaṉaip pāṭiṉār. ivaṉ kaḷaṅkāyk kaṇṇi nārmuṭic cēraliṉ tampiyākiya kuṭakkō neṭuñcēralātaṉukku vēḷāvikkōmakaṉ makaḷ patumaṉ tēviyiṭam piṟantavaṉ. ivaṉ, taṇṭāraṇiyattiluḷḷavar kaḷāṟ kavarappaṭṭa pacu niraikaḷai mīṭṭu toṇṭiyil koṇarntu cērppit tamai paṟṟi ippeyar peṟṟāṉ. ivaṉ antaṇarukku pacukkaḷaiyum nilaṅkaḷaiyum tāṉañceytāṉ. ivaṉukkut talai nakaram toṇṭi. ivaṉ kālam ki. pi. mūṉṟām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ muṟpakutiyākum. ivaṉ 38 āṇṭu aracu vīṟṟiruntāṉ eṉpa.
ēḻām pattāl kapilar celvak kaṭuṅkōvāḻi ātaṉaic ciṟappittār. ivaṉ antuvañcēralirum poṟaikku oru tantai īṉṟa makaḷākiya peruntēviyiṭattiṟ piṟantavaṉ. yāṉaikkaṭcēy eṉpatu ivaṉukku maṟu peyar. ivaṉ peruṅkoṭaiyālum aruṅkuṇaṅkaḷālum peyar peṟṟavaṉ; tirumāl pattiyiṟ ciṟantu viḷaṅkiyōṉ. kapilar tam uyirttuṇaivaṉāka viḷaṅkiya vēḷpāri uyir nīttatum, appāriyiṉ uttamakuṇaṅkaḷ palavum iccelvakkaṭuṅkōviṭam uḷḷaṉavākak kēḷviyuṟṟu ivaṉaik kāṇacceṉṟu ēḻām pattai ic cēraṉ muṉ pāṭiṉar. avar pāṭalaikkēṭṭa celvakkaṭuṅkō, av vantaṇap periyārkku nūṟāyiram kāṇam aḷittatōṭu naṟṟā eṉṉuṅ kuṉṟil tāṉum avarum ēṟi niṉṟu taṉ kaṇṇiṟ kaṇṭa nāṭellām kāṭṭi appulavarkku aḷittāṉ. ivaṉ maṉaivi neṭuñcēralātaṟku makaṭ koṭutta vēḷāvikkōmāṉuṭaiya maṟṟoru makaḷāvaḷ. ākavē neṭuñcēralātaṉum celvak kaṭuṅkōvum cakalamuṟaiyiṉar. ivvēntar pirāṉ irupattaintāṇṭu vīṟṟiruntāṉ. ivaṉ cikkaṟpaḷḷi eṉṉu miṭattiṟ tuñciṉāṉ.
kapilar pāṇṭiyanāṭṭil tiruvātavūril piṟanta antaṇaṉ. tāṉ piṟanta nāṭṭukkuriya pāṇṭiya revaraiyāvatu ivar pāṭavillai. ivar iḷamaiyilēyē pāṇṭiya tēcam viṭṭuc cēranāṭṭiṟ ceṉṟu vāḻntatē itaṟkuk kāraṇamāyirukkalām. ivar perumpālum malaivaḷaṅkaḷaiyum malaināṭṭu aracarkaḷaiyumē ciṟappittirukkiṉṟaṉar. ivar ceyta nūlkaḷ: aiṅkuṟu nūṟṟil oru nūṟu, kuṟiñcippāṭṭu, iṉṉā nāṟpatu, naṟṟiṇai kuṟuntokai, akanāṉūṟu, puṟanāṉūṟu mutaliyavaṟṟiṟ cila ceyyuṭkaḷ ātiyaṉavākum ivar palatēvaṉ viṣṇu civaṉ mutaliya kaṭavuḷarait tamatu pāṭalkaḷiṟ ciṟappittirukkiṉṟaṉar. patiṉōrāntirumuṟaiyiṟ kāṇappaṭum pāṭalkaḷ piṟitoruvar ceytaṉavāyirukkalām. ivar kālattaiya pulavarkaḷ ivaraip ‘poyyānāviṟ kapilaṉ’ eṉṟu ciṟappit tirukkiṉṟaṉar.
eṭṭām pattāl aricilkiḻār peruñ cēralirumpoṟaiyaic ciṟappittār. ivaṉ celvakkaṭuṅkōvukku vēḷāvik kōmāṉ makaḷ 1patumaṉ tēviyiṭam piṟanta makaṉ. ivaṉ atikamāṉatu takaṭūr mēṟpaṭai yeṭuttuc ceṉṟu perumpōr purintu, avvūraiyum atikamāṉaiyum aḻittaṉaṉ. ippōrc ceyalē takaṭūr yāttirai eṉṉum paṇṭainūliṟ ciṟappittuk kūṟappaṭuvatu. iv veṟṟi paṟṟi ‘takaṭū reṟinta’ eṉṉum aṭaiyuṭaṉ ivaṉ vaḻaṅkappaṭuvaṉ. aricil kiḻār pāṭalkaḷaik kēṭṭu makiḻnta iccēraṉ, tāṉum taṉ maṉaiviyum veḷiyē vantu niṉṟu, taṉ kōyililuḷḷa vellām koḷka eṉṟu oṉpatu nūṟāyiram kāṇattōṭu taṉ aracu kaṭṭilaiyum pulavarkkuk koṭuppa, avaṉ, ‘yāṉirappa nīyāḷka’, eṉṟu avaṟṟait tirumpak koṭuttu avvaracaṉukku amaiccuppūṇṭār. iccēraṉ patiṉēḻāṇṭu vīṟṟiruntāṉ. ip peruñ cērarkku maṉaivi attuvañceḷḷai. ivaṉ ukkirapāṇṭiyaṉ kālattavaṉ.
oṉpatām pattāl peruṅkuṉṟūr kiḻār iḷañcēralirum poṟaiyaic ciṟappittār. ivaṉ kuṭṭuvaṉ irumpoṟaikku maiyūr kiḻāṉ vēṇmāḷantuvañ ceḷḷaiyiṭam piṟantavaṉ. ivaṉ kaṭal aṭaṅkavēlōṭṭiyavarum, ayiraimalaitteyvattai alaṅkarittavaru mākiyavarkaḷiṉ cantatiyil vantavaṉ eṉṟu collappaṭukiṉṟatu.
caṅka nūlkaḷ tokuttu muṭintatu ki. pi. aintām nūṟṟāṇṭu varai yil eṉṟu karutappaṭukiṉṟatu. iṟaiyaṉārakap poruḷuraiyil tokai nūlkaḷaik kuṟippiṭumiṭattup patiṟṟuppattum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. ākavē iṟaiyaṉārakap poruḷurai eḻutappaṭuvataṉ muṉ paḻantamiḻc ceyyuṭkaḷ tokukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa veṉpatu tēṟṟam. iṟaiyaṉārakap poruḷ nīlakaṇṭaṉārāl ki. pi. āṟām nūṟṟāṇṭu varaiyil eḻutappaṭṭirukka vēṇṭumeṉpatu pallōr karuttu.
in nūl tokuttavarālēyē patikaṅkaḷum pāṭappaṭṭiruttal vēṇṭum. āciriyar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar aṭiyārkku nallār mutaliya uraiyāciriyarkaḷ ip patikaṅkaḷait tamatu uraikaḷil eṭuttāṇṭiruk kiṉṟaṉar. ivarkaḷiṉ kālam ki. pi. 12ām nūṟṟāṇṭu varaiyil.
iṉic cēra aracar kapilar, paraṇar, pālaik kautamaṉār peruṅkuṉ ṟūrkiḻār aricilkiḻār mutaliya pulavarkaḷatum kālattai ārāyvōm. patiṟṟup pattiluḷḷa aracarkaḷiṉ paramparai iruvakaippaṭum.
ivvaracarkaḷukkum talainakaram toṇṭi.
ivvaracarkaḷuḷ ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ kālam māttiram tīrmāṉamākak kaṇikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. atu ki. pi. 175-225 ākum. ākavē cilappatikāram iyaṟṟappaṭṭa kālam ki. pi. 200kkum 225-kkumiṭaiyil irukkalām. mēlē kuṟikkappaṭṭa 1ām aṭṭavaṇaiyiṉ 1ām 2ām 3ām aracarkaḷatum 2ām aṭṭavaṇaiyiṟ kuṟikkappaṭṭa 3ām aracaṉatum kīrttippiratāpaṅ kaḷaic cilappatikāraṅ kūṟukiṉṟatu. ākavē ivviraṇṭu paramparaikaḷilu muḷḷa aracarkaḷ ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉukku muṟpaṭṭa kālattavarkaḷāka vēṇṭum. paraṇar, (4) ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉaiyum (1) cēralātaṉaiyum pāṭiyirukkiṉṟār. iṉṉum pukārilirunta uruvappaḵṟēr iḷañcēṭceṉṉi (karikālaṉiṉ tantai) uṟaiyūrilirunta vēṟpaḵṟaṭakkaip perunaṟkiḷḷi mutaliyōrai yum pāṭiṉar. 1ām aṭṭavaṇaiyiṟ kāṭṭappaṭṭa 1 mutal 4 varaiyumuḷḷa aracarkaḷiṉ āṭcik kālattaik kaṇakkiṭiṉ oru nūṟṟāṇṭukku mēlākiṉṟatu. itu orupōtum paraṇaruṭaiya vayatāyirukkamuṭiyātu. ākavē ovvoru aracariṉ āṭcik kālameṉṟatu muṭicūṭuvataṉmuṉ cēra nāṭṭiṉ cila pakutikaḷukku atipatikaḷā(Viceroys)yiruntu avarkaḷ āṭcipurinta kālamum cērntatēyākumeṉka. inta iraṇṭu aṭṭavaṇai yilum kāṭṭiya aracarkaḷ ki.pi. 125-kkum 225-kkum iṭaiyil āṇṭavarka ḷātal vēṇṭum.
cilappatikārattil īriṭaṅkaḷil kayavākuviṉ peyarvantuḷḷatu. cilappatikārattiluḷḷa vañcikkāṇṭattiṉ pāṭṭuṭaittalaivaṉ ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ. ākavē ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ kayavākuviṉ kālattavaṉ eṉpatu taṭaiyiṉṟit tuṇiyappaṭum. immuṭipiṉaiyē ṭākṭar es. kiruṣṇacāmi aiyaṅkār mutaliya ārāycciyāḷar kaikkoṇṭaṉar. iṅkilīṣ moḻiyil ‘tamiḻar carittiram’ eṉa nūl eḻutiya pi.ti. srīṉivāca aiyaṅkāra varkaḷ ikkoḷkaiyai utaṟittaḷḷi cilappatikāram kayavāku kālattukkup piṟpaṭṭateṉac cātikka muyaṉṟu kayavāku eṉpatu eḻutuvōrāl piḻai yāka eḻutappaṭṭirukkalāmeṉak kūṟiṉar. kāla ārāycciyaip paṟṟiya akkaṟai ēṟpaṭāta kālattu ēṭṭup piratikaḷaip pirati ceyyum pōtu ‘kayavāku’ eṉṉum peyarai yārāvatu vēṇṭumeṉṟu nūliṟ pukutti yiruppārkaḷā? appaṭip pukuttiṉum pērā. cuntarampiḷḷai avar kālattu allatu ataṟkup piṉ itu nikaḻntirukkavēṇṭum. vāṉmīki irāmā yaṇam teṉṉintiyāvaik kuṟippiṭum pakuti iṭaic cerukal eṉak kūṟuvataṉ muṭipum ippaṭiyē. tamiḻ nūlkaḷukkuk kūṭiya vayataik koṭukkakkūṭāteṉpatu cila carittirakārar kōṭpāṭu. ākavē avarkaḷ taṅkaḷ koḷkaikkēṟpa niyāyaṅkaḷaik kaṇṭu piṭippatil talaiyai uṭaikkiṉṟaṉar.
patiṟṟuppattu vacaṉam
iraṇṭām pattu
11 civanta vāyuṭaiya ampu pakaivariṉ uṭalaip pōḻntu tiṟanta puṇ kaḷiṉiṉṟum irattam koppaḷikkum. ataṉāl periya akaḻiluḷḷa paḷiṅku pōṉṟa nīr kuṅkumak kalavai pōṟcivakkum. ivvāṟu (pakaivar) araṇai aḻittu mikunta valimaiyaiyum uyarnta ciṟappaiyum ūkkattaiyum eytiya vēntē!
palar moyttu niṉṟu kātta pūṅkottukaḷuṭaiya kaṭampai veṭṭip peṟṟa veṟṟik kaṟikuṟiyāka muḻaṅkum (vīra) muraciṉai (akkaṭampiṉāl) ceytavaṉum, tāṉ velkiṉṟa pōrum, paṉṉāṭaiyāl vaṭikkappaṭum tēṉ oḻukukiṉṟa pūmālai acaiyum mārpum, pōr virumpum tāṉaiyu muṭaiyavaṉumākiya cēralāta!
pirakācikkiṉṟa niṟainta nīruṭaiyatum, kāṟṟu mōtutalāl malaipōṉṟa tiraikaḷ vāṉil tuḷikaḷaic citaṟukiṉṟatumākiya kuḷirnta kaṭaliṭattē (māvaṭivāka) niṉṟa, accantarum avuṇar kāvalpuriyum cūrapaṉmāvai aḻittaṟporuṭṭu murukak kaṭavuḷ kaḷiṟūrntār. at tōṟṟattai oppa, mārpiṭattē polikiṉṟa paciya pū mālaiyum neṟṟip paṭṭamum uyarnta maruppumuṭaiya yāṉaiyiṉ mattakattil vīṟṟiruttalāl palarālum pukaḻappaṭum niṉcelvattai yām kaṇṭēm.
12 vīrar porutu vīḻkiṉṟa vāṭpōriṟ kalantu periya cuṟṟamuṭaiya aracar talai naṭuṅkak kaṭampai veṭṭiya kaṭiya kōpattiṉaiyum, tāraṇinta eruttiṉaiyu muṭaiya vēntē!
vārukiṉṟa vaḷaviya nakamuṭaiya ciṅkam cañcarikkiṉṟa malaiccāralil ataṉaikkaṇṭa māṉ kūṭṭam neñcu tiṭukkiṭutal pōl uṉatu pōrmuracu muḻaṅkuvataik kēṭṭa periya nakariṭattuḷḷa vēntar tuyil koḷḷār; pala ticaikaḷilumuḷḷōr naṭuṅkuvar. ivvakaiyāṉa niṉ celvamum maṟam mikunta pukaḻum kēṭṭaṟku iṉitu.
vāṭāta paciyamayirum paiya acaikiṉṟa naṭaiyum iḷamaiyumuṭaiya kaḷiṟṟuyāṉai, kaṉṟōṭu kūṭiya piṭikku variyuṭaiya vaṇṭai maṇa muṭaiya malai mallikaiyāl ōccukiṉṟa kuṉṟukaḷ palavaṟṟaik kaṭantu niṉ cuṟṟattiṉar uṉṉaik kāṇṭal viruppāl vantu cērvar. añ ñāṉṟu avarkaḷatu paḻaiya pacivaruttam nīṅkumpaṭi arivāḷiṉāl aṟutta veḷḷiya niṇak kuṟaiyaiyum, āṭṭiṟaicci kalanta veḷḷiya cōṟṟaiyum kuḷirntakaḷḷuṭaṉ uṇṇumpaṭi koṭuppai; nīriṟ paṭṭa paruntiṉ ciṟaku pōṉṟa maṇ tiṉṟa kantai uṭaikaḷaik kaḷaiyumpaṭi ceytu paṭṭāṭaikaḷai uṭukkumpaṭi ceykuvai. curuṇṭakariya kūnta lum vaḷainta melliya tōḷumuṭaiya kuṟṟamillāta peṇkaḷ aṇi yumpaṭi pirakācikkiṉṟa āparaṇaṅkaḷai nalkuvai; ivvāṟu aṇintu amarum meymmai niṟainta cuṟṟattōṭu niṉperiya āravārattai yuṭaiya olakkattuc celkiṉṟa vinōta makiḻcci aṉupavittaṟkiṉitu.
