(pakaip) pulattiṟ koṭiyacai yum kāvaluṭaiya eyilkaḷ eṇṇiṉ ellai aṟiyā; pala kutiraikaḷ parantaṉa; (ākaiyāl pakaippulam namakku vellaṟku ariya toṉ ṟeṉṟu uṇarāy) nī avvāṟu valluṉai āṉapaṭiyai muṉpu aṟiyātu, iṉṟu pōrceytu aṟintaṉarāyiṉum, avar niṉṉōṭu kōpittu eḻuntu vantu ataṉaip piṉṉumaṟivatallatu (niṉṉai) vaṇaṅkal aṟikiṉṟilar. iṉi avar, mukilpōl ārttumuḻaṅkum iḷam yāṉaiyiṉ kīḻakappaṭṭa mūṅkil muḷaiyiṉ kiḷai aḻivatu pōla aḻivatallatu uyyakkarutuvatu yātu?
kōpittu eḻuntuceṉṟu pōrkkappaṭṭa muracam, āravāram eḻuntu caṇṭaiyai aṟivikkap pōrttoḻil pūṇṭa māṟṟāratu vēṟpaṭai niṟainta parappil, kālattāṟpeyyum māripeytu taṉatu toḻilāṟṟi ākāyattiṟ ceṉṟapuyal neṭuṅkālam malaiyiṭattē ceṉṟu maḻai tōṉṟiyaviṭattu, palakuraluṭaiya puḷḷiṉ oli eḻuntāṟpōlap pōraṟai kūvum cerukkuṭaiya vil nīṅkāta vīrarōṭu iṭi iṭikkum malaipōṟ kaḷiṟu nilattē uruḷa nilaiyāmai amainta pala pōraic ceytu niṉ paṭaikkūṭṭam āravārittirukkiṉṟa iruppiṉaiyām iṉitu kaṇṭēm.
85 (iḷañcēralirumpoṟai) poṉṉiṉāl oḷiviṭumpaṭi vēlaippāṭuka ḷamaittuc ceytatum piṟar pūṇutaṟkariyatumākiya viḷaṅkum periya āparaṇaṅkaḷai aṇinta ceṉṉiyar perumāṉuṭaiya (=cōḻaṉatu) (payaṉtarum) nalla maraṅkaḷ ceṟinta pala nāṭukaḷaik kavarntu emakkut tantāṉ; acceṉṉiyar perumāṉai em muṉṉē piṭittuk koṇṭuvantu tammiṉeṉat tam paṭaittalaivarai ēvac, ceṉṉiyar perumāṉiṉ paṭaiyāḷar porutu tōṟṟup pōṭṭu ōṭiya veḷḷiya vēluṭaiya celvak kaṭuṅkōvāḻiyātaṉeṉpavaṉ, tīñcuṉai kaḷuṭaiya aḻakiya pakkaṅkaḷil pala kōṭukaḷ parantatum (taṉ mēl) ēṟi nāṭu muḻuvataiyum pārttaṟku ētuvākiyatumākiya malaiyiṭattu kaḷḷuvārkkappaṭummakiḻccikkuriya kālai olakka irukkaiyiṉkaṇ taṉ muṉṉōrkaḷākiya aracarkaḷaip pōl aracavai paṇiyumpaṭi aṟattai nāṭṭuvataṟku maṟampuriyum taṉmaiyai otta koḷkai yuṭaiyai! avaṉ tantanāṭukaḷ, pirakācikkiṉṟa civanta nāviṉāl meymaiyākap pāṭiya pukaḻuṭaiya kapilaṉ peṟṟa nāṭukaḷilum palavākum.
86 nilattilē irattam perukip, pōrkkaḷam pulāl nāṟṟam vīcumpaṭi koṉṟu pōraiveṉṟa periya valiya vicālitta kaiyil vellum vēluṭaiya poṟaiyaṉ eṉṟu (ellōrum) kūṟukaiyāl yāṉ avaṉai accantarum kōpamuṭaiyāṉ oru makaṉeṉṟu (muṉpu karuti ṉēṉ); avveṇṇam ippoḻutu kaḻintatu. appoṟaiyaṉākiya, nalla pukaḻnilai peṟṟa periya ulakil illātavarkaḷiṉ varuttam tīra nalkum ārāycci mikunta aruḷ uṭaiya neñciṉaṉākiya pāṭuvōr vēntaṉum, acaintu naṭakkiṉṟa talaivaṉum ākiya avaṉ, puṉalilē makaḷir pāyntu āṭa mitantu poṉṉāṟ ceyta aḻakiya kuḻaimītu tōṉṟiya cāntu mitantuvarum vāṉi āṟṟu nīriṉum niccayamāka iṉiya kuḷirnta cāyaluṭaiyaṉ.
87 pala vēṟpaṭaiyuṭaiya cēraṉ, cantaṉamum akilum, poṅkum nuraiyiṭattuc cumantu, teḷinta kaṭaliṭattē cellum veḷḷiya talai yuṭaiya civanta nīraiyuṭaiya āṟṟiṉ nīr vaḻic celuttum, karumpāṟ ceyta teppattiṉum aruḷceyya vallaṉ; (ātalāl avaṉ pāl) pāṭiṉi! celvāyāka; (celliṉ) nalla āparaṇaṅkaḷaip peṟukuvai.
88 ulakiṟ paranta aracaratu toḻiliṉ muṟai tavaṟātu, koṟṟavaiyiṉ peya ruṭaiya kāṭṭōṭu 1(vintāṭavi) malaikaḷ uyarntu kaṭalāṟcūḻappaṭṭa akaṉṟa iṭattaiyuṭaiya ulakattut tamatu pukaḻparanta pakaivar tēya, alaiyuṭaiya periya kaṭal piṟaṅkiṭumpaṭi vēl eṟintum, aḻakiya kaṭampai veṭṭiyum, porumporuṭṭu māṟupaṭṭa kaḻuvuḷaip puṟaṅkaṇṭum, pukaḻpeṟṟa maṉṉarai veṭṭikkoṉṟum, katiyuṭaiya kutiraiyaip pakaivariṭam kavarntum, cuṭarviṭukiṉṟa vākaiyuṭaiya naṉṉaṉai aḻittum, irattam cintiya kuvinta cōṟṟumalaiyōṭu aḻakiya ayiraimalait teyvamākiya koṟṟavaiyaip paraviyum, aracarum vēḷirum piṉceṉṟu paṇiya veṟṟi koṇṭōratu marapil vantōṉē! nīṇṭa piṭarimayiruṭaiya ciṅkampōṉṟu vīram poruntiya kuricil! muracu parantolikkiṉṟatum, paricaikaḷ varicaiyāka (niṟuttap peṟṟatu) mākiya iṭattil valiya kaḷiṟṟiṉmītu veṟṟikkoṭi acaikiṉṟa pācaṟaiyum, kāvaluṭaiya matilai aḻikkum kaṟkaḷai iṭai viṭātu eṟiyum kavaṇaiyum, paṉṉāṭaiyāl vaṭikkum tēṉaiyu muṭaiya koṅkar talaiva! kōpittōraik koṉṟa poṉtēruṭaiya kuricil! vaḷainta kaṭaluṭaiya toṇṭiyōr poruna! peruma! collappaṭṭa māṭcimaiyuṭaiya uttama ilakkaṇaṅkaḷōṭum, polinta cāntōṭum kuḷirnta maṇam vīcukiṉṟa mālai cūṭippūṇ kaḷaic cumappavaḷum, kariya mukiṟkūṭṭaṅkaḷ periyamaḻai peytu vēṅkai pūttuḷḷa ākāyattai muṭṭum uyarnta koṭumuṭikaḷi liruntu aruviōṭum malaiyai ottamārpuṭaiyavaḷum, tūrattē naṟumaṇam vīcappeṟukiṉṟa, nalla pukaḻamainta aḻakiya āparaṇaṅkaḷai uṭaiyavaḷumākiyavaḷatu kaṇava! kāṟṟaṭittalāl uyarntu varum tirai aṭaikkappaṭṭa karaiyai uṭaikkum taṇṇiya kaṭaṟkaraic cōlai yuṭaiya nāṭukiḻavōy! yāṉ īṇṭu niṉṉaik kāṇavantēṉ. nī nīṭuvāḻvāyāka; ellārālum pukaḻntu collappaṭṭa tumpaippōrai niṉakku veṟṟi taraṟku meymaiyāka viṉavum valiyaittarum koṟṟavai kūṭi uṟaikiṉṟa (ayirai) malaiyil ūṟṟeṭuttup pāy kiṉṟa pērāṟupōla, varaivillātu varuvōr ceḻitta pala arum paṇṭaṅkaḷaik koḷḷak koḷḷak kuṟaiyātu, mēlum mēlum niṉnakar ciṟappaṭaiyum. (ivvāṟu celvamamainta) cittirampōṉṟa aḻakiya nīṇṭa nakaril pāvai otta makaḷiriṉ naṭuvē tēvarulakam oḷipeṟa periya vicumpiṉ kaṇ uyarntu cellum ñāyiṟṟaippōlap paṉṉāḷ viḷaṅkuka.
89 pala vēṟpaṭaiyuṭaiya perumpoṟaiya! cemmaiyāṉa vāṉam poḻu toṭu viḷaṅka, kāṭṭiṟ kūṭṭamākiya peṇmāṉ āṇ māṉōṭu cērntu tiriya, periya marakkiḷaikaḷil paṭcikaḷum vaṇṭum iruntu ārppa paḻamuṅ kiḻaṅkum uṇṇak kuṟaivu paṭātiruppa, pala pacu nirai pul aruntittuḷḷa, celvamkuṟai talaṟiyāta vaḷam poruntiya ciṟappuṭaiya periya pala putuvaruvāyākiya kūlam niṟaiya, nalla pala ūḻikālam naṭuvu nilaimai niṉṟu oḻuka, tiṉantiṉam nāṭṭavar toḻutu ētta, viṇ ulakattut tēvarkaḷ ētta, araciyal piḻaiyātoḻiyap pōril mēmpaṭṭut tōṉṟi nī, kaṉavilum nīṅkāta pōrvaiyōṭu niṉṉiṭattu aṭaṅkiya neñcu mācupaṭutal aṟiyātu, kuḷirnta mayirc cāntu pūciya īrampularāta kūntaluṭaiya kaliyāṇamakaḷir ceṉṟu nōkki tam vāḻnāḷai aṟiyum pirakācikkiṉṟa oḷiyuṭaiya aruntatiyōṭu otta kaṟpuṭaiya, oḷḷiya neṟṟiyuṭaiya niṉ arivaiyōṭu kāṇumpaṭi polintu nōyillaiyākuka.
90 veḷḷi niṟkum muṟaimaiyil niṟka vāṉam maḻai peytaliṉ, accam tīrntu pātukāppākit, tuṉpan tīrntu, iṉpam mikavuntōṉṟip (peruka), (pārppār mutalāyiṉār tattamakku aḷavāṉa) tuṟai nūlkaḷai añcātu niṟaiyakkaṟka, valiyiṉ mikkōr kōpikkum kaṭiya vali yiṉai añcāta oḷḷiya veṟṟivēntar yāṉaikaḷōṭu āparaṇaṅ kaḷait tantu, niṉ paḻamaiyaip pukaḻntu ēviyataic ceyya, akaṉṟa ulakattavarkaḷukku naṭuvu nilaimaiyaic ceytu, māyāta palapukaḻ vāṉil parakka vāḷvali purintu ciṟappup pūṇṭu, aṟakkaṭavuḷ vāḻtta naṉku āṭci purinta veṟṟiyuṭaiya māntaraṉuṭaiya marapiluḷḷōy! aḻakiya muṟaimaiyil ayiraimalaitteyvattai vaḻipaṭṭu, kaṭal aṭaṅka vēleṟintu, malai iṭattavum, nilattiṭattavumākiya araṇaik kaippaṟṟic ciṉanta pakaivariṉ valiyaic cāyttup peṟṟa perum pukaḻaip palariṭattiṉiṉṟum pōkkiya veṟṟit tiṟamaiyaip peṟṟa uravōr umpal! kaṭṭiyōṭukūṭiya avaraip paruppup puḻukku mutaliya puḻukkiṉaiyuṭaiya koṅkar kōvē! kaḷḷuṭaiya kuṭṭuvar ēṟē! pōril mutukiṭṭār mēṟcellāta valiyatōḷuṭaiya pūḻiyar meymmaṟai! ilaṅkukiṉṟa nīrpparappuṭaiya māntaiyōr poruna! vēḷaiyiṉ veḷḷiya pūvoṭu curaip pū māṟit toṭuttāṟ pōlap pācaṟaiyil, kalanta (piṟapala) moḻikaḷaippēcum vīrariṉ vēntē! paranta kaṭalpōṉṟa tāṅkutaṟkariya tāṉaiyōṭu periya pōr ceyyum villai mārpil muṭṭumpaṭi vaḷaittaliṉ nāṇ urōñcutalāl viḷaṅkiya, vēntar tiraṇṭālotta valiyuṭaiya, vārntu alaṅkarikkap paṭṭatu pōṉṟa vicālittakaiyaiyum, naṭcattiraṅkaḷ pūttāṟpōṉṟu viḷakkuṭaiya cēṇamum āyntamayirāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa kavariyumuṭaiya kutiraimītu ēṟi, piṭiyuṭaiya vēlaippiṭittu eṟintu, pakaivarai iṭumpai ceytalai virumpum vīraraiyumuṭaiya peruma! uyarnta peruñciṟappiṉāl ōṅkiya pukaḻōy! kuḷirnta puṉaliṭattu āṭuvōriṉ āravārattil kalakka vevviya pōrceyyum maḷḷar teḷḷiya ōcaiyuṭaiya kiṇaippaṟaiyaikkoṭṭa, kūlaṅkaḷuṭaiya nallavīṭukaḷil ēṟukaḷ oṉṟukkoṉṟu māṟāka ārppa, ceḻiya palākkaḷ uṭaiya koḻuviya kuḷirnta vayaliliṭattuḷḷa kāviri cūḻnta cōlaiyuṭaiya vayaliluḷḷa uḻavar, mattaḷaṅkoṭṭa vayaliluḷḷa mayilkaḷ maḻaiyaikkaṇṭāṟ pōl āṭum nalla nāṭṭaippōla vaḷamporuntiya, cilampum aṭaṅkiya kuṇamum, āṟiyakaṟpum, tēṟiya nalla kuralum, vaṇṭukaḷ olikkiṉṟa kūntalumuṭaiya oṇṭoṭiyiṉ kaṇava! iṭipōl muḻaṅkum muracum periya nalla yāṉaiyumuṭaiya iṟaikiḻavōy; īram uṭaimaiyiṉ nīril vāḻvōrai oppai; aḷattaṟkarumaiyiṉ periyavāṉam aṉaiyai; koḷḷakkuṟaivu paṭāmaiyiṉ kaṭal alaiyai; paṉmīṉkaḷiṉ naṭuvē tiṅkaḷaippōla malarnta cuṟṟattōṭu polintu tōṉṟutalaiyuṭaiyai; (ātalāl) niṉakku aṭaitta nāṭkaḷ ulakattiṟ tiṅkaḷ aṉaiyavākaveṉṟum, niṉṉuṭaiya tiṅkaḷ yāṇṭu aṉaiyākaveṉṟum, niṉṉuṭaiya āṇṭu ūḻiyaṉaiyavāka veṉṟum, niṉyāṇṭiṟku oppākiya appallūḻitam aḷaviṟpaṭṭa palavāy nillātu veḷḷavarampiṉavāka veṉṟum niṉaintu niṉṉaikkāṇpēṉ vantēṉ.
oṉpatām patikam
kuṭṭuvaṉ irumpoṟaikku maiyūr kiḻāṉ makaḷ vēḷmāḷ attuvañ ceḷḷai īṉṟa makaṉ, accantarum tāṉaiyōṭu ciṉantu ceṉṟu iru peruvēntarum vicciyum vīḻa ariya kāvaṟkāṭuṭaiya kaṟkōṭṭaiyi ṉakattu aintu matilkaḷai aḻittu, pottiyai āṇṭa periya cōḻa ṉaiyum vittai āṇṭa iḷam paḻaiyaṉ māṟaṉaiyum (tāṉ) kūṟiya vañciṉam palikkumpaṭi veṉṟu, vañci mūtūriṟkaippaṟṟiya poruḷ kaḷaip piṟarkku aḷittu, mantira muṟaimaiyil (ayirait) teyvattai vaḻipaṭṭu, taṉ mantiriyākiya maiyūr kiḻāṉaip purōkitaṉiṉum aṟaneṟi aṟivāṉākap paṇṇi, ariya valiya muṟaimaiyil nāṟcantiyi lamarnta koṭiya valiyuṭaiya pūtaraik koṇarntu ivviṭattu niṟutti, terinta muṟaimaiyiṟ pūcaiceytu, ulakattu uyirkaḷaik kātta kuṟṟan tīrnta ceṅkōlum, iṉṉicai muracu muṭaiya iḷañcēralirum poṟaiyaip peruṅ kuṉṟūrkiḻār pattup pāṭṭup pāṭiṉār.
pāṭip peṟṟa paricil: mayakkam illārkku mayaṅkak koṭukka veṉṟu uvakaiyiṉ muppattīrāyiraṅ kāṇaṅkoṭuttu, avar aṟiyāmai ūrum maṉaiyum vaḷamikap paṭaittu, ērum iṉpamum iyalvarapparappi, eṇṇaṟku ākā ariya paṇṭaṅkaḷākiya celvattōṭu paṉṉūṟāyiram pala paṭak koṭuttu aṉuppiṉāṉ akkō. kuṭakkō iḷañ cēralirum poṟai patiṉāṟāṇṭu vīṟṟiruntāṉ.
1. puṟattiraṭṭut tolkāppiya urai mutaliyavaṟṟiṟ kāṇap paṭṭa “iruṅkaṇyāṉai” eṉṉum ceyyuḷ.
iṭi iṭittu muḻaṅkaliṉ vicumpu atirvatu pōla kaṇ atirntu muḻaṅkum kaṭuṅ kuraluṭaiya muracōṭum kāṟṟuppōṉṟu vēkamākiya ūrtiyōṭum, kāṭṭuttīyaippōṉṟa niṟainta (taṭuttaṟkariya) kōpat tōṭu, periya iṭamuṭaiya periya kaṭalai nāṭi ōṭi nilantiraikkum āṟṟaip pōṉṟa vēkamākiya celaviṉaiyuṭaiya tāṉaiyōy! niṉakku.
tolkāppiya uraiyiṟ kāṇappaṭṭa
“ilaṅku toṭi” eṉṉum ceyyuḷ
ilaṅkukiṉṟa pūṇ iṟukkiya maruppuṭaiyamatam poḻintunilam atirum paṭi tiriyum veṟṟiyuṭaiyakaḷiṟum, neruppu malarntāṟ pōṉṟu virinta talaiyāṭṭattaic cūṭṭiyakatiyuṭaiya kutiraiyum, pōrkkaḷattu koṭikaḷ oṉṟōṭu oṉṟu kalakkac cūtāṭumiṭaṅkaḷōṭu ūṉviṟpā riṭaṅkaḷaiyum ottu (=koṭikaḷ kalantu acaitaliṉ) uyarntu aruviyaip pōṉṟu olikkum variyuṭaiya alaṅkarikkappaṭṭa tērkaḷum varutalaikkaṇṭu, (pakaivar) muracaṅkoṭṭiṉar; periya ticaikaḷ kalleṉṟu olikkumpaṭi kāṟṟāṭiyaippōṟṟa ōcaiyuṭaiya kompōṭu valampuric caṅku ārppa neṭiya matilum niraitta ñāyilum kāvaṟkāṭum āḻnta akaḻum uṭaiya, mēlē akaṉṟa āravārikkum araṇ kāvalaiyuṭaiyatāyiruntatu. ceñcu vīram aḻintu nilai taḷarntu nīṅkiya pakai aracar naṭuṅka ivviṭam vanta periya celavu naṉmaiyai aḷippatākuka.
tolkāppiya uraiyiṟ kāṇappaṭṭa “vantaṉeṉ” eṉṉuñ ceyyuḷ
māriyeṉṟu pārātum, paṉi eṉac cōrātum, pakaivemmaiyiṉāl acaiyāta ūkkattai uṭaiyōy! pakaip pulattut taṅkiya veṟṟi yuṭaiya koṭiya tāṉaiyōṭu pakai māṟāta māṟṟār pakaitēya valimai mikunta kaṭṭuttaṟiyaip piḷantu kaṭāyāṉai muḻaṅkum muḷvēli iṭāta pācaṟai yiṭattu, yāṉaiyaiyum, katiyuṭaiya kutiraiyaiyum tērōṭu iravalarkku aḷittu irukkum irukkaiyai! peruma! vantu kaṇṭupōka vantēṉ.

pattuppāṭṭu urainaṭai
muṉṉurai
caṅka nūlkaḷiṉ poruḷ pulavarkaḷukkum eḷitil viḷaṅkuvataṉṟu. ataṟkuk kāraṇam annūlkaḷ ceyyappaṭukiṉṟa kālattu vaḻaṅkiya coṟkaḷiṟ perumpālāṉa iṉṟu vaḻakku vīḻntu viṭṭamaiyēyākum. poruḷ viḷaṅkutaṟ karumaiyuṭaiya innūlkaḷ vittuvāṉ, paṇṭitar, pulavarkaḷāl māttiram payilappaṭṭu varukiṉṟaṉa. potumakkaḷ caṅka nūlkaḷil collappaṭṭirukkum poruḷkaḷ evai eṉpatai aṟiyamāṭṭārkaḷ. avarkaḷ caṅka nūṟporuḷkaḷai eḷitil aṟintu koḷḷum muṟaiyil avaṟṟai urainaṭaippaṭutta vēṇṭumeṉṉum uṇarcci iṟṟaikkup patiṉaintu āṇṭukaḷukkumuṉ namatu uḷḷat teḻuntatu. ākavē caṅka nūṟkaḷiṟ palavaṟṟai urainaṭaip paṭuttalāyi ṉēm. av vurainaṭaikaḷ peritum payaṉuṭaiyaṉa veṉṟu tamiḻ aṟiñar tamatu karuttait terivittārkaḷ; māṇavar palarum avvāṟē aṟivitta ṉar. ippatippil kūṭiyaḷavu vacaṉaṅkaḷaik kuṟukkiyum kaṭiya coṟkaḷai nīkkiyum uḷḷōm. namatu nūl kaḷait tamiḻ ulaku aṟiyumāṟu innūliṉ mutaṟpatippaiyum piṟa nūlkaḷaiyum veḷiyiṭṭa oṟṟumai nilaiyat talaivar tiru em. ī. vīravāku piḷḷai avarkaḷukku eṉatu naṉṟiyum kaṭappāṭum eṉṟum uriyaṉa.
ceṉṉai
10-3-1949 na. ci. kantaiyā.
1. tirumurukāṟṟuppaṭai
tōṟṟuvāy
murukakkaṭavuḷ vīṟṟirukkum iṭaṅkaḷiṟ ceṉṟu avar aruḷaip peṟṟu mīḷkiṉṟa murukaṉaṭiyāṉ oruvaṉ murukaṉ aruḷaip peṟutaṟkup pakkuvam vāynta oruvaṉai vaḻiyiṭattē etirppaṭṭut, tāṉ ceṉṟu aruḷpeṟṟa varalāṟṟai avaṉukku eṭuttukkūṟi avaṉaiyum murukaṉ pāṟ ceṉṟu avaṉ aruḷaip peṟumāṟu celuttuvatāka amaintuḷḷatu ippāṭal.
“akavaṟ pāvāl viṟalipāṇar
kūttar porunar nālvaru ḷoruvar
pariciṟkup pōvāraip paricu
peṟṟu varuvā rāṟṟiṭaik kaṇṭu
talaivaṉ kīrttiyuṅ koṭaiyuṅ koṟṟamum
aṟaiva tāṟṟup paṭaiyā kummē”
tirumurukāṟṟuppaṭai eṉṉum pāṭalaic ceytavar kaṭaiccaṅkat tiṟutikkālattil caṅkattukkut talaimai tāṅkiyirunta āciriyar nakkīraṉārāvār. avar kālam ki. pi. 100-130. ikkālak kaṇakkum piṉṉāl varuvaṉavum āyirattu eṇṇūṟu āṇṭukaḷukku muntiya tamiḻar eṉṉum nūlāciriyar kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷapaṭi uḷḷaṉa.
tirupparaṅkuṉṟam
(murukaṉ aruḷpeṟṟumīḷum aṭiyavaṉ akkaṭavuḷiṉ aruḷaip peṟa viḻaivuṟṟirukkum maṟṟoru aṭiyavaṉaip pārttuk kūṟukiṉṟāṉ:)
murukak kaṭavuḷiṉ tōṟṟam
murukakkaṭavuḷ kuṟṟamillāta kaṟpiṉaiyuṭaiya teyvayāṉaiyāriṉ kaṇavar. ulakiluḷḷa uyirkaḷ ellām makiḻumpaṭi ñāyiṟu mēruvai valañceyyum. murukakkaṭavuḷ aññāyiṟu kaṭaliṭattē tōṉṟiṉāṟ pōṉṟu iṭaiviṭātu oḷi ceykiṉṟa tirumēṉiyaiyuṭaiyar; tammai aṭaintāratu tīviṉaiyaip pōkkum valiya tāḷkaḷuṭaiyavar; iṭiyai otta valiyum pakaivaraiaḻitta āṟṟalumuṭaiya kaikaḷaiyuṭaiyar; kār kālattil kaṭal nīrai vayiṟu nirampumpaṭi kuṭitta mukil ñāyiṟum tiṅkaḷum kiraṇaṅkaḷai vīcum ākāyattil tuḷikaḷaic citaṟik kāṭṭil mutal maḻaiyaip peyyum. ataṉāl kāṭu kuḷircciyaṭaiyum. akkuḷirnta kāṭṭil ceṅkaṭampu iruṇṭu taḻaittu vaḷarum. ataṉ tēruruḷ pōṉṟa malarkaḷāl toṭutta mālaikaḷ murukak kaṭavuḷiṉ tirumārpiṟ kiṭantu acaiyum.
teyvamakaḷir
mūṅkil tēvarulakait toṭumpaṭi malaiyiṭattē vaḷarum. aṅkut teyvamakaḷir uṟaivar. avarkaḷ viḷaṅkukiṉṟa tamatu ciṟiya pātaṅkaḷil ciṟiya cataṅkaikaḷai aṇintiruppar. avarkaḷiṉ kālkaḷ tiraṭciyuṭaiyaṉa; iṭai vaḷaintu acaiyum taṉmaiyatu; tōḷkaḷ perumaiyuṭaiyaṉa. avarkaḷ uṭuttirukkum paṭṭāṭai tampalap pūcci pōṉṟu civanta iyaṟkai niṟamuṭaiyatu. avarkaḷ maṇikaḷ kōtta vaṭaṅkaḷai arai yiṭattē aṇintiruppar. avarkaḷiṉ aḻaku kaiyiṉāl aḻaku ceyyap paṭātu iyaṟkai vaṉappu amaintuḷḷatu. avarkaḷ aṇintirukkum aṇi kalaṉkaḷ cāmpūnatameṉṉum uyar cempoṉṉiṉāl ceyyap paṭṭavai. avarkaḷiṉ tūya niṟam tūrattē viḷaṅkit tōṉṟum oḷiyuṭaiyatu. ivviyalpiṉarākiya teyvamakaḷir cōlaiyiṭattē kūṭuvārkaḷ; neypūcap peṟṟa tamatu mayiril civanta kāmpai yuṭaiya veṭcippūvai viṭu pūvākat tūvuvārkaḷ; avaṟṟiṉ naṭuvē kuvaḷai malariṉ itaḻkaḷaik kiḷḷi viṭu pūvākat tūvuvārkaḷ; poṭṭiṭṭa tamatu neṟṟiyil cuṟāviṉ tiṟanta vāypōṟ ceyta talaikkōlattai aṇivārkaḷ; 1cītēviyār eṉṉum talaik kōlattuṭaṉē valampuric caṅkiṉ vaṭivākac ceyta talaikkōlattaiyum vaittaṟkuriya iṭattē vaippārkaḷ; muṭiya vēṇṭiya vakaiyil muṭiyappaṭṭuḷḷa koṇṭaiyil caṇpakappūvaic cerukuvārkaḷ; ataṉmēl viḷaṅkukiṉṟa pūṅkottukkaḷai vaip pārkaḷ; nīriṉ kīḻ niṉṟu mēlē eḻum kiḷaikaḷ nīṭṭiya arumpiṉāl toṭutta mālaiyaik koṇṭaiyil vaḷaiyamākac cuṟṟuvārkaḷ; iru kātukaḷilum acōkan taḷiraic cerukuvārkaḷ; avai, aṇikalaṉka ḷaṇinta avarkaḷ mārpiṭattē kiṭantu acaiyum. ivvāṟu tammai aḻaku ceytu koṇṭa teyvamakaḷir kōṅkiṉ moṭṭai otta tamatu taṉaṅ kaḷiṭattē cantaṉak kuḻampai appuvārkaḷ. accantaṉappūccu, maṇamuḷḷa marutam pūvai appiyatu pōlat tōṉṟum. cantaṉak kuḻampiṉ īram ular vataṉ muṉ vēṅkaip pūviṉ tātaiyum avarkaḷ ataṉmēl appu vārkaḷ; viḷāmarattiṉ ciṟiya taḷiraik kiḷḷit tappāmal oruvarmēl oruvar eṟintu “kōḻiuyarnta veṟṟikkoṭi neṭitu vāḻvatāka” eṉṟu vāḻtti malai iṭaṅkaḷ ellām etiroli ceyyumpaṭi pāṭi āṭuvārkaḷ
murukak kaṭavuḷ tirumuṭiyil cūṭiya ceṅkāntaḷ mālai
atteyvamakaḷir ivvāṟu āṭal pāṭal puriyum cōlaiyai yuṭaiya malaiyil maraṅkaḷ neruṅki vaḷarntirukkum; ākavē mantikaḷum ammaraṅkaḷil ēṟi aṟiyā. immara nerukkam uṭaiya malaiyiṭattuḷḷa ceṅkāntaḷ malarāl toṭutta mālaiyai murukak kaṭavuḷ tirumuṭiyil cūṭiyiruppār.
pēymakaḷ
murukak kaṭavuḷ kutirai mukattaiyum makkaḷ yākkaiyaiyumuṭaiya cūrapaṉmāvōṭu pōr ceytār. appoḻutu cūrapaṉmā kīḻ nōkkip pūtta kottukaḷaiyuṭaiya māmaravaṭivāka māyaiyiṉāl vaṭivukoṇṭu niṉṟāṉ. appoḻutu murukakkaṭavuḷiṉ cevvēl viraintu ceṉṟu avaṉai avaṉuṭaiya 1aṟuvakaip paṭaikaḷum añcumpaṭi aḻittatu. appoḻutu pēymakaḷ veṟṟikkaḷattaip pāṭit tōḷai acaittut 2tuṇaṅkaikkūttu āṭiṉāḷ. pēymakaḷ pōrkkaḷattil accantōṉṟum paṭi ulāvavum virumpiṉāḷ. avaḷatu kuṟukiya mayirkaḷ ularntavai; paṟkaḷ teṟṟiyavai; paciyakaṇkaḷ kōpattāṟ cuḻalvaṉa; pārvai accam viḷaippatu; kātukaḷ mārpu aḷavum nīṇṭu acaivaṉa; avai accantarum pāmpum pituṅkiya kaṇkaḷaiyuṭaiya āntaiyumākiya kuṇṭalaṅkaḷaip pūṇṭavai; uṭal coracorappuṭaiyatu; naṭai kaṇṭōrāl añcattakkatu; kūriya nakaṅkaḷuṭaiya vaḷainta viralkaḷ irattam aḷaitalāl civappu ēṟap peṟṟavai; kaikaḷ, muṭaināṟṟamuṭaiyavum kaṇkaḷait tōṇṭi uṇṇappaṭṭaṉavumākiya kariya talaikaḷai ēntuvaṉa; periya vāykaḷ niṇantiṉpaṉa.
(ivvāṟu pēymakaḷ veṟṟikkaḷattē āṭiṉāḷ.) avuṇar kaḷuṭaiya veṟṟi aṭaṅkiṟṟu, kaṭal nilai kulaintatu.
tirupparaṅkuṉṟam
pāṭavalla pulavaṉē! ivvāṟu perumai peṟṟa murukak kaṭavuḷiṉ tiruvaruḷaippeṟa nī mika virumpukiṉṟāyāyiṉ, nī ataṉai ippoḻutē tappāmal peṟukuvai. avar eṅku uṟaivār eṉa nī kēṭpāyāyiṉ. ataṉai yāṉ kūṟuvēṉ kēṭpāyāka. maturaimā nakariṉ kōpuravāyilil veṟṟikkoṭi acaiyum; varintu kaṭṭappaṭṭa pantumpāvaiyum aṟuppāriṉṟit toṅkum. nakaril pala māṭaṅkaḷuṭaiya terukkaḷuṇṭu. ivvakai aḻaku vāynta maturaikku 3mēṟkē periya nelvayalkaḷ uṇṭu. kariya cēṟṟiṉaiyuṭaiya av vayalkaḷil muṟukkaviḻnta tāmarai malarkaḷmītu vaṇṭukkūṭṭaṅkaḷ irāppoḻutil tuyilkoḷḷum; viṭiyaṟkālattil tēṉ maṇakkiṉṟa neytaṟpūvai ūtum; ñāyiṟu tōṉ ṟiyapiṉ cuṉai malaril iruntu āravārañ ceyyum ivvakai aḻakiya viṭattēyuḷḷa tirupparaṅkuṉṟam eṉṉum kōyilil murukakkaṭavuḷ eḻuntaruḷiyiruppār. (itu avar irukkum vīṭukaḷuḷ oṉṟākum)
2. tiruccīralaivāy1
murukak kaṭavuḷ yāṉaimītu irukkum tōṟṟam
murukakkaṭavuḷ piṇimukam eṉṉum yāṉaimītu eḻuntaruḷuvār. piṇimukam eṉṉum yāṉaiyiṉ neṟṟi aṅkucam veṭṭutalāl taḻumpu ēṟappeṟṟatu. neṟṟiyil poṉṉarimālaiyum neṟṟippaṭṭamum kiṭantu acaiyum; irupakkaṅkaḷilum maṇikaḷ kiṭantu olikkum. ataṉ naṭaipiṟa rāl taṭuttaṟ kariya kaṭumaiyuṭaiya tātaliṉ kūṟṟuvaṉai okkum. murukak kaṭavuḷ aṇintirukkum 2aintu vēṟu vaṭivuṭaiya muṭikaḷil oḷiviṭum irattiṉaṅkaḷ patikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. avar kātilaṇintirukkum poṟ kuḻaikaḷ matiyaic cūḻntu nīṅkāta 3viṇmīṉkaḷaippōl viḷaṅkum. avaratu tirumukam tavattai muṭippavarkaḷatu uḷḷattil viḷaṅkum oḷipōl viḷaṅkum.
āṟu mukaṅkaḷiṉ toḻilkaḷ 
murukak kaṭavuḷiṉ oru mukam iruṇṭa periya ulakam kuṟṟamiṉṟi viḷaṅkum poruṭṭup pala katirkaḷait tōṟṟuvikkum; oru mukam, aṉparkaḷ tutikka ataṟku makiḻntu avarkaḷ vēṇṭum karumaṅkaḷai muṭittukkoṭukkum; oru mukam antaṇaratu yākaṅkaḷil tīmai varāmaṟ kākkum; oru mukam mantiramuṭaiya vētamuṟaiyil oḻukum antaṇaratu yākaṅkaḷil tīmaivarāmaṟ kākkum; orumukam vētattiṉ maṟai poruḷ kaḷai ārāynta iruṭikaḷ makiḻumpaṭi avaṟṟai uṇarttit tiṅkaḷ pōlat ticaikaḷai ellām viḷakkuvikkum. oru mukam māṟupaṭṭup pōrk keḻunta pakaivaraik koṉṟu kōpitta uḷḷattōṭu pōhk kaḷattai virumpum; oru mukam kuṟavariṉ iḷamaiyuṭaiya makaḷākiya vaḷḷi yuṭaṉ makiḻcciyaip poruntum.
paṉṉirukaraṅkaḷ puriyum toḻilkaḷ
murukak kaṭavuḷiṉ tirumārpiṉiṉṟu tōḷaḷavum uttama ilakkaṇa mākiya mūṉṟu varaikaḷ nīṇṭu kiṭakkum. avaratu valiya tōḷkaḷ pakaivar mēl paṭaikkalaṅkaḷai eṟintu mārpaippiḷantu vāṅkum perumai yuṭaiyavai. avar tirukkaraṅkaḷiloṉṟu ākāyattē iyaṅkukiṉṟa iruṭikaḷukku apayaṅkoṭukkum; uyarttiya ak kaiyiṉ iṇaikkai pakkattē kiṭakkum; oru kai toṭaimītu āṭaimēṟ kiṭakkum; orukai yāṉaiyai aṅkucattāl celuttum;iru kaikaḷ kariya kēṭakattaiyum vēlaiyum valamākac cuḻaṟṟum; orukai muṉivarkaḷukkut tattuvaṅkaḷaik kūṟi uraiyiṟanta poruḷai uṇarttum kālattu mārpiṭattu viḷaṅkum; orukai tirumālaiyait tiruttum; oru kai pāravaḷaiyōṭu cuḻalum; orukai iṉiya ōcaiyuṭaṉ āṭukiṉṟa maṇiyai olikkum. oru kai nīla ākāyattil mikka maḻaiyaip poḻiyum. orukai intiraṉ makaḷākiya teyvayāṉaikku maṇamālai cūṭṭum. ivvakai paṉṉirukaikaḷum āṟu mukaṅkaḷukkup poruntum vakai toḻilpuriyā niṟkum.
cīralaivāyil murukak kaṭavuḷ eḻuntaruḷum tōṟṟam
ākāyattil tuntumi olikkum. vayiramuṭaiya kariyakompu vāttiyaṅkaḷum veṇcaṅkum muḻaṅkum. iṭiyēṟṟiṉ iṭippuppōṉṟa ōcaiyuṭaiya muracu atirum. pala puḷḷikaḷ uḷḷa iṟakukaḷuṭaiya mayil veṟṟik koṭiyiliruntu olikkum. murukak kaṭavuḷ ivvakaip pala ārppuṭaṉ ākāyamē vaḻiyāka viraintuceṉṟu mikka aḻakuṭaiya cīralai vāy eṉṉum tiruppatiyil eḻuntaruḷiyiruppār. ituvē avaratu nilai peṟṟa kuṇamākum.
3. tiru āviṉaṉkuṭi1
muṉivar tōṟṟamum iyalpum
tiru āviṉaṉkuṭiyilē muṉivōr murukak kaṭavuḷai vaḻipaṭuvar. avarkaḷ taittup poruntiya maravuri uṭaiyiṉar; avarkaḷiṉ naraitta muṭikaḷ, vaṭivu niṟam aḻaku mutaliyavaṟṟāl valampuric caṅkai ottavai. avarkaḷiṉ uṭampu nīrāṭutalāl aḻukkiṉṟi viḷaṅkuvatu. paṭṭiṉiyiruntu virataṅ kāttalāl avarkaḷ mārpiṭattu eṉpuk kōvaikaḷ tōṉṟum; avarkaḷiṉ pōrvai poruttittaitta māṉtōlākum; avarkaḷ pala nāṭkaḷ paṭṭiṉiyiruppar; veṟuppum ciṉamum avariṭam tōṉṟuvatillai. avarkaḷiṉ aṟivu evvakaip poruḷaikkaṟṟa vallunarum aṟiyamuṭiyā tatu. palavaṟṟaik kaṟṟārkkum avar talaimaiyāyirukkun taṉmaiyar; kāmam, vekuḷi mutaliyavai avinta tōṟṟattiṉar; meyvaruttam uḷavēṉum maṉavaruttam ciṟitum aṟiyāta iyalpiṉar.
nāṟperuṅkaṭavuḷar
veḷḷiya naccup paṟkaḷaiyum aḻalpōṉṟa mūccaiyumuṭaiya pāmpaic cāka aṭikkum karuṭaṉai ūrtiyākavum koṭiyākavum uṭaiya tirumāl kāttaṟṟoḻil purivar. muppurattai aḻitta veṟṟiyaiyum, mūṉṟu kaṇkaḷaiyum, iṭapakkoṭiyaiyum, pākattē umātēviyāraiyu muṭaiya uruttiraṉ aḻittaṟṟoḻil purivar. nilattil tōykiṉṟa vaḷainta tutikkaiyiṉaiyum nāṉku maruppiṉaiyumuṭaiya airāvatamākiya vākaṉamum, āyiram kaṇkaḷumuṭaiya intiraṉ nūṟu vēḷvikaḷ vēṭṭu ataṉ payaṉāl pakaivarai aṭakkiya veṟṟiyumuṭaiyaṉ. muppattumūvar eṉṉumtēvarkuḻuviṉar nāṉkupiriviṉarāvar. kantaruvar melliya vārttaiyaiyum, muṟukkaviḻnta malar mālaicūṭiya mārpaiyum, pukaiyaik kaiyiṉālaḷḷiṉāṟ pōṉṟa mācēṟāta uṭaiyaiyum yāḻvācikkum tiṟamaiyaiyu muṭaiyar. kāntaruvamakaḷir makkaḷukkuriya nōyillāta uṭampum, poṉṉurai viḷaṅkiṉāṟ pōṉṟa aḻakiya tēmalum māntaḷir pōṉṟu viḷaṅkum niṟamum uṭaiyar.
murukak kaṭavuḷ āviṉaṉ kuṭiyil vīṟṟirukkum tōṟṟam.
oru kālattil cerukkuṟṟirunta piramaṉai murukak kaṭavuḷ ciṟaiyi liṭṭār. akkālattup paṭaipput toḻil illai āyiṟṟu. ataṉāl tirumāl uruttiraṉ mutalāṉōrkkuriya kāttal aḻittaṟ ṟoḻilkaḷum illaiyāyiṉa. tamakkuriya kāttal aḻittaṟ ṟoḻilkaḷaip paḻamaipōl peṟavirumpiya tirumālum uruttiraṉum murukak kaṭavuḷiṉ kōpattait taṇittup piramāvaic ciṟaiyiṉiṉṟum mīṭka eṇṇiṉārkaḷ; eṇṇi intiraṉ patiṉeṇkaṇaṅkaḷ muppattumūvar mutaliya tēvarkaḷuṭaṉ, viṇmīṉkaḷ pōṉṟa tōṟṟamum, kāṟṟil nerupputtōṉṟiṉālotta valiyum, iṭi otta kuralum uṭaiyarāy antarattē cuḻaṉṟu tirintaṉar. kantaruvar tamatu makaḷirōṭu yāḻvācittup piṉṉē cella muṉivarkaḷ muṉṉē ceṉṟaṉar. ivvakait tōṟṟattuṭaṉ murukakkaṭavuḷ teyva yāṉaiyāruṭaṉ āviṉaṉ kuṭiyil eḻuntaruḷuvar.
4. tiru ērakam1
antaṇar iyalpu
antaṇar ōtal, ōtuvittal, vēṭṭal, vēṭpittal, ītal, ēṟṟal eṉṉum āṟu toḻilkaḷum uṭaiyar; tūya marapil utittōr; nāṟpat teṭṭu āṇṭu piramacāriyaṅ kākkum iyalpiṉar; tarumattaiyē ennāḷum kūṟum koḷkaiyar, nāṟcaturam, muccaturam, vilvaṭivamākiya ākavaṉīyam, takkaṇākkiṉi, kārukapattiyam eṉṉum muttī ōmpum celvamuṭaiyar; nūlaṇitaṟkumuṉ oṉṟum aṇintapiṉ oṉṟumākiya irupiṟappuṭaiyar; nīrāṭuṅkāl tōytta āṭaiyaip pularumpaṭi uṭukkum iyalpiṉar. ivvakaip puṉitamuṭaiya antaṇar naṟiya pūvaik kaiyilēnti, “namōkuma rāyā” eṉṉum mantirattai nāppuṭaiyapeyara uccarittu murukak kaṭavuḷai vaḻipaṭuvar. ataṟku makiḻntu avar tiru ērakattil eḻuntaruḷuvar.
5. kuṉṟutōṟāṭal
murukak kaṭavuḷ kuṟamātarōṭu āṭum kāṭci
kuṉṟiṭaṅkaḷil vāḻum vēṭar villāṟ kolait toḻil puriyum iyalpiṉar; tēṉaip puḷikkaviṭṭuc ceyta kaḷḷai mūṅkiṟkuḻāyiliṭṭu muṟṟumpaṭi vaippar; akkaṭṭeḷivai malai ucciyiluḷḷa ciṟukuṭic cuṟṟattuṭaṉ uṇṭu kaḷippar; toṇṭakappaṟai koṭṭik kuravai āṭuvar. ataṉaikkaṇṭa peṇmāṉ pōṉṟa cāyalaiyuṭaiya peṇkaḷ tāmum 1kuravaiyāṭuvar. ataṉaik kaṇṇuṟum, kacciṉaiyum, taṇṭaiyaiyum, ciṟuvāttiyaṅkaḷaiyum, kiṭāyaiyum, mayilaiyum, kōḻik koṭiyaiyum, neṭumaiyaiyum, tōḷvaḷaiyaiyum, tukiliṉaiyumuṭaiya murukakkaṭavuḷ tāmum āṭalai virumpuvar; virumpi mārpilē cantaṉam pūciya vēlaṉ eṉṉum veṟiyāṭṭāḷaṉ paccilaik koṭiyaiyum, cātikkāyaiyum iṭai yiṭṭut takkōlakkāy, kāṭṭu mallikai, veṇṭāḷi mutaliyavaṟṟaik kalantu kaṭṭiya mālaiyai aṇivar; civanta āṭaiyai uṭuttu, acōkan taḷiraik kātiṟceruki yāḻōcaipōluṅ kuraluṭaiya pāṭumakaḷirōṭu kuṉṟukaḷtōṟum ceṉṟu 1kuravai āṭum kuṟattiyar kaikaḷait taḻuvi, ēnti, muṉ kaikoṭuttu viḷaiyāṭuvar. itu murukak kaṭavuḷukku nilai peṟṟa kuṇamākum.
6. paḻamutir cōlai2
murukak kaṭavuḷ eḻuntaruḷum iṭaṅkaḷ
murukak kaṭavuḷ taṅkiyirukkum iṭaṅkaḷāvaṉa: ciṟutiṉai ariciyaip pūvuṭaṉē kalantu vaittu āṭṭu maṟiyai aṟuttuk, kōḻik koṭiyai niṟutti ūrkaḷ tōṟum koṇṭāṭukiṉṟa viḻākkaḷ, aṉparkaḷ tutittu maṉam porunti irukkiṉṟa iṭaṅkaḷ, vēlaṉ veṟiyāṭukaḷam, kāṭu, cōlai, āṟṟiṭaikkuṟai, pala ūrkaḷ, nāṟcanti, muccanti, putuppūviṉaiyuṭaiya kaṭampu, ūrukku naṭuvē irukkum marattaṭi, ātīṇṭu kuṟṟi mutaliyaṉa.
kuṟamātu muruka pūcai ceyyum vakai
kuṟamātu akaṉṟa nakariṭattē kōḻik koṭiyai niṟuttuvāḷ; neyyum veṇkaṭukum appuvāḷ; vaḻipaṭum mantiraṅkaḷai uccarittu aḻakiya malarkaḷait tūvuvāḷ; māṟupaṭṭa niṟamuṭaiya iraṇṭu paṭṭāṭai kaḷai uḷḷoṉṟum puṟam oṉṟamāka uṭuppāḷ; civanta nūlai kaiyiṟ kāppākak kaṭṭuvāḷ; veṇporiyait tūvuvāḷ. irattattōṭu picainta veḷḷariciyaic ciṟupaliyākat tūvuvāḷ; paccai mañcaḷōṭu vācaṉai mikunta cantaṉam mutaliyavaṟṟait teḷippāḷ; cevvalari mālaikaḷaiyum piṟamālaikaḷaiyum orēmaṭṭamāka aṟuttu acaiyumpaṭi tūkki, “malaip pakkattuḷḷa ūrkaḷaip paciyum piṇiyum pakaiyum varuttā toḻika” eṉṟu vāḻttuvāḷ; naṟiya pukai kāṭṭik kuṟiñcip paṇpāṭi malai aruviyōṭu pala vāttiyaṅkaḷ olikkac civanta malarkaḷait tūvi, irattamaḷainta tiṉai ariciyaip parappi murukakkaṭavuḷ makiḻumpaṭi vāttiyaṅkaḷai olikkac ceytu vaḻipaṭuvāḷ. ivvāṟu accantarum murukapūcai ceyyappaṭum akaṉṟa nakariṭattum murukak kaṭavuḷ uṟaivār. 
veṟiyāṭuvōṉ veṟiyāṭukaḷam olikkumpaṭi pāṭip, pala kompu kaḷai ūti maṇiyai olittup piṇimukam eṉṉum yāṉaiyai vāḻtti vēṇṭiṉār vēṇṭiya varaṅkaḷaip peṟavēṇṭumeṉṟu vaḻipaṭa murukak kaṭavuḷ avviṭattil eḻuntaruḷuvār.
paḻamutir cōlaiyiṉ iyaṟkai vaḷam
malaiyiṭattiṉiṉṟum koṭic cīlaikaḷaippōṉṟu āravārat tuṭaṉ aruvikaḷ kutittōṭum. avai akil marattai aṭittuk koṇṭu varum; cantaṉa marattait taḷḷum; kiḷaikaḷ antarikkumpaṭi mūṅkiliṉ vērkaḷaik kiḷaṟum; tēṉkūṭukaḷaik keṭuttup palāccuḷaikaḷai vāric curapuṉṉai malarai utirttup peṇ kuraṅkaiyum āṇ kuraṅkaiyum naṭuṅkac ceyyum; peṇyāṉaikaḷ kuḷirāl varuntumpaṭi nīraivīci, poṉṉum irattiṉamum oḷiviṭṭu ilaṅkumpaṭi kutittu, poṉ poṭiyaik karaiyil vīci, vāḻai muṟiyavum teṅkiṉ iḷanīrk kulaikaḷ utiravum mōtum; miḷakuk koṭiyiṉ kariya pūṅkottukaḷaic cāyttu, mayilkaḷum, pēṭṭuk kōḻikaḷum payantōṭac ceytu, āṇ paṉṟikaḷum karaṭikaḷum kalaḷaikaḷil nuḻaiyavum, kompuṭaiya kāṭṭumāṭu muḻaṅkavuñceytu āravārikkum. ivvakai aruvikaḷ uṭaiya paḻamutir cōlaiyil murukak kaṭavuḷ amarntiruppār.
murukakkaṭavuḷ kāṭci aḷittu aruḷ vaḻaṅkum tōṟṟam
yāṉ kūṟiya avvavviṭaṅkaḷilāyiṉum piṟaviṭaṅkaḷilāyiṉum murukakkaṭavuḷ vīṟṟiruppār. nī avaraik kāṇumpōtu kaikūppi vaṇaṅku. piṉpu piṉvarumāṟu tuti: imayamalaiyiṉucciyil taruppai vaḷarnta cuṉai yil aṅkiyaṅkaṭavuḷ taṉ uḷḷaṅkaiyil vāṅki viṭa aruntati allāta aṟuvarāṟ peṟappaṭṭa āṟu vaṭivu poruntiya celvaṉē! kallāliṉ kīḻirukkum kaṭavuḷiṉ putalvaṉē! periya malai araiyaṉ makaṉē! pakaivar kūṟṟē! veṟṟi aḷippavaḷum aṇikalaṉkaḷaṇintavaḷumākiya kāṭu kiḻāḷ kuḻaviyē! vaḷainta villaiyuṭaiya tēvar paṭaittalaivaṉē! ellā nūlkaḷaiyum aṟinta aṟivuṭaiyōṉē! pōrt toḻilil nikarillātavaṉē! veṟṟiyuṭaiya iḷamaip paruvat tōṉē! arumaṟai yuṇarnta antaṇar celvaṉē! cāṉṟōr pukaḻntu collum coṟkaḷiṉ poruḷāyiruppavaṉē! teyvayāṉaiyārukkum vaḷḷiyammaiyārukkum kaṇavaṉē! vīrarkku iṭapamē! vēl tāṅkiya kaiyaiyuṭaiya celvaṉē! kirauñca malaiyaip piḷanta veṟṟi yuṭaiyavaṉē! amararulakait tīṇṭum uyarnta malaikaḷai yuṭaiya kuṟiñci nilattuk kurimai uṭaiya vaṉē! kalviyil matayāṉai pōṉṟavarkkuc ciṅka ēṟu pōṉṟavaṉē! piṟarkkup peṟaṟkariya vīṭṭiṉaiyuṭaiya murukaṉē! vīṭupēṟṟiṉaippeṟa virumpi vantavarkku ataṉai nukarvittalāl periya pukaḻai āḷuta luṭaiyavaṉē! piṟarāl varuttamuṟṟu vantōrkku aruḷ ceyyum cēyē! periya pōrkaḷai muṭittu veṉṟu pakaivaraik kolkiṉṟa niṉmār piṭattup poṉṉāparaṇaṅkaḷai aṇinta cēyē! irantu vantōrai vēṇṭuvaṉa koṭuttup pātukākkum neṭiya vēḷē! tēvarum muṉivarum ēttum tiruppeyaru yuṭaiya talaivaṉē! pōr valiyāl cūrapaṉmāviṉ kulattai aḻitta matavali eṉṉum peyaruṭaiyavaṉē! ivvāṟu nī aṟinta tutikaḷaik kūṟip pukaḻntu pāṭi, oppillāta meyññāṉattai yuṭaiyavaṉē! umatu taṉmaikaḷai ellām muṟṟa aḷaviṭutal palluyir kaḷukkellām aritu ātaliṉ niṉ pātatāmaraikaḷaip peṟa eṇṇi vantēṉ, eṉṟu nī karutiya vīṭupēṟṟai viṇṇappañ ceyvāyāka. uṭaṉē murukak kaṭavuḷaic cēvittu niṟkiṉṟa vēṟu vēṟu vaṭiviṉaiyuṭaiya kuṟukiya pala pūtaṅkaḷ tōṉṟip, “perumāṉē! nī aruḷattakkavaṉākiya aṟivu mutirnta pulavaṉoruvaṉ niṉ pukaḻiṉai virumpik kēṭpōrkku uṟuti payakkum iṉiya pala vāḻtturaikaḷaik kūṟi vantāṉ” eṉṟu kūṟum. appōtu paḻamutir cōlaiyil eḻuntaruḷiyirukkum murukakkaṭavuḷ accantarum teyvat taṉmaiyai uḷ aṭakkikkoṇṭu taṉatu iḷaiya vaṭivaik kāṭṭi, “nī vīṭupeṟa niṉaintu vanta varavai nāṉ muṉṉamē aṟivēṉ; ataṉai niṉaintu eytutal aritu eṉṟu añca vēṇṭām” eṉṟu uṉmēl aṉpuṭaiya nalvārttaikaḷaip palakālum aruḷic ceyvār. piṉ iv vulakil nī oruvaṉumē piṟarkku vīṭaḷittaṟku uriyava ṉākat tōṉṟumpaṭi tiruvaruḷākiya paricilait tantaruḷuvār.
2. 1porunarāṟṟuppaṭai
pāṇaṉ oruvaṉ koṭai vaḻaṅkum vaḷḷalkaḷaittēṭi alaintu tirikiṉṟāṉ. avaṉ piṉṉē avaṉ maṉaiviyākiya viṟaliyum kariya periya cuṟṟattiṉarākiya ciṟuvarum celkiṉṟaṉar. avarkaḷ celkiṉṟa iṭam kāṭṭuvaḻi.
pāṭiṉi ciṟanta aḻaki. avaḷ kūntal āṟṟiṉ karumaṇalpōṉṟu iruṇṭu neḷintatu; neṟṟi piṟaipōṉṟatu; puruvam kolaittoḻi luṭaiya villai oppa vaḷaintatu; aḻakiya kaṭaikaḷuṭaiya kaṇkaḷ kuḷirnta nīrmaiyiṉa; iṉiya coṟkaḷai miḻaṟṟum civantavāyō ilavitaḻ pōṉṟatu; paṟkaḷ muttai niraipaṭa vaittāṟ pōṉṟavai; makarakkuḻaikaḷ acaintu ūcalāṭum kātukaḷ kattarikaiyiṉ kuḻaiccuppōl amaintu aḻakuṟuvaṉa; kaḻuttō nāṇam varuttutalāl piṟarai nōkkātu muṉ vaḷaintirukkum iyalpiṉatu; tōḷkaḷ paruttut tiraṇṭa mūṅkilpōlat tiraṇṭavai; avar muṉkaiyil aitamayiruṇṭu; avaḷ viralkaḷ uyarnta malaikaḷil vaḷarum kāntaṭpūp pōṉṟavai; nakaṅkaḷ kiḷiyiṉ alaku pōl civantu viḷaṅkuvaṉa; taṉaṅkaḷ cuṇaṅkaṇintu īrkkiṭai pōkāta eḻucciyum aḻakumuṭaiyaṉa; koppūḻ nīrccuḻi pōṉṟatu; iṭai uṇṭu eṉṟu piṟarāl aṟiya muṭiyātu oṭuṅki acaiyum iyalpiṉatu; arai pala maṇikaḷ kōtta mēkalāparaṇam aṇiyap peṟṟatu; tuṭaikaḷ peṇyāṉai yiṉ tumpikkaipōṉṟu tiraṭciyuṭaiyaṉa; aṭikaḷ ōṭi iḷaitta nāyiṉ nākkup pōṉṟaṉa; vāy mayir oḻuṅku peṟṟa kaṇaikkālukkup porutta māṉavai.
pāṭiṉi yāḻ vācittut teyvattait tutittal
ivvāṟu pēraḻaku vāynta pāṭiṉi, cātiliṅkam urukki vārttāṟ pōṉṟu civanta nilattilē naṇpakal veyyilil naṭantu celkiṉṟāḷ; kālkaḷil kūriya cukkāṉkaṟkaḷ uṟuttukiṉṟaṉa; ataṉāl paṉṉaip paḻam pōṉṟu kālkaḷiṟ koppuḷaṅkaḷ uṇṭākiṉṟaṉa. avaḷ mēlum naṭattaṟku iyalātavaḷākiṉṟāḷ. yāṉaikaḷ ulāvukiṉṟa kāṭṭuppātai yiṉ ōrattē ilaikaḷ utirtta marāmaram niṟkiṉṟatu. ataṉ niḻal valai virittatu pōṉṟa aitākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. an niḻalil pāṭiṉi ciṟitu taṅki iruntu iḷaippāṟukiṉṟāḷ. avaḷ taṉatu pālaiyāḻai eṭukkiṉ ṟāḷ. aṅku uṟaiyum teyvaṅkaḷ makiḻumpaṭi narampukaḷaivāriyum, vaṭittum, untiyum, uṟaḻntum teyvaṅkaḷait tutikkum pāṭalkaḷaip pāṭukiṉṟāḷ.
yāḻiṉ vaṭivu
ap pālaiyāḻiṉ 1pattar māṉiṉ kuḷampu aḻuttiṉa iṭam pōṉṟu iraṇṭarukun tāḻntu naṭu uyarntuḷḷatu. iraṇṭu talaiyum kūṭṭit taikkappeṟṟa pōrvai poṉṉiṟat tōliṉālāṉatu. iraṇṭu pattaruñ cērvataṟkuk kaṭāviya āṇikaḷ naṇṭiṉ kaṇ pōṉṟavai; vāy eṭṭānāḷ tiṅkaḷpōṉṟu uṇṇākkillātatu; kariya taṇṭu pāmpu talaiyai nīṭṭiṉāṟpōṉṟa tōṟṟamuṭaiyatu. vārkkaṭṭukkaḷ peṇkaḷ kaiyilaṇiyum vaḷaiyalkaḷ pōṉṟavai. viralāl alaittu vācikkappaṭum narampukaḷ tiṉaiyiṉ kuṟṟalarici pōṉṟavai.
paricil peṟṟuvarum pāṇaṉ porunaṉai etirppaṭal
ivvāṟu pāṇaṉ viṟaliyuṭaṉum taṉatu cuṟṟattuṭaṉum iḷaippāṟi yirukka avvaḻiyē iṉṉōr pāṇaṉ taṉatu cuṟṟattōṭu varukiṉṟāṉ. avaṉ karikāṟ peruvaḷattāṉaip pāṭip paricil peṟṟu varukiṉṟava ṉāvaṉ. vaṟumaiyāl melivuṟṟirukkiṉṟa pāṇaṉai avaṉ nōkkik kūṟukiṉṟāṉ.
viḻāk koṇṭāṭuṅkālattuc cuṟṟattuṭaṉ mikunta cōṟṟai uṇṭu makiḻntum atu kaḻintapiṉ piṟi tōrviḻāk koṇṭāṭum ūrai aṭaintum tirikiṉṟa porunaṉē! yāḻaiyuṭaiya kūttarukkut talaivaṉē! piṟar pukaḻai aracar capaiyil uyarvaṭaivikka valla vaṉē! yāṉ kūṟukiṉṟavaṟṟai virumpik kēṭpāyāka. vaḻi aṟiyāmaiyāl ivvaḻiyait tappi vēṟoru vaḻiyiṟ ceṉṟu viṭātē. iv vaḻiyiṭattē nī eṉṉaik kaṇṭatu nī muṟpiṟappiṟ ceyta nalviṉaiyiṉ viḷaivākum. uṉṉaiyum uṉatu kariya cuṟṟattaiyum eṉṟum varuttukiṉṟa paciyaip pōkkikkoḷḷa virumpukiṉṟāyāyiṉ viraivil eḻuntiru. yāṉum paḻa maraṅkaḷait tēṭi alaiyum paṟavaikaḷaippōlap paricil taruvārait tēṭi alaintēṉ.
taṉṉaik karikāl vaḷavaṉ varavēṟṟup paricil vaḻaṅkiya vakaiyiṉaip pāṇaṉ eṭuttu moḻital
oru nāḷ karikāṟ cōḻaṉatu āravāramikka kōpuravāyilai aṭaintēṉ; vāyil kāppō ṉiṭamuṅ kūṟātu uḷḷē ceṉṟēṉ. celkiṉṟa makiḻcciyiṉāl eṉatu iḷaippu nīṅkiṟṟu. eṉatu uṭukkaiyiṉ kaṇkaḷ kaipaṭutaliṉāl pāmpiṉ paṭattiluḷḷa poṟikaḷ pōṉṟa taḻumpukaḷ peṟ ṟiruntaṉa. nāṉ ataṉiṭattē iraṭṭait tāḷattaip piṟappittu eṉatu vaṟumai tīrumpaṭi oru pāṭṭup pāṭiṉēṉ. appōtu viṭiveḷḷiyiṉ veḷiccam tōṉṟum viṭiyaṟkālam. uṭaṉē aracaṉ uṟaviṉaraippōlat taṉṉiṭattil nāṉ uṟavu koḷḷumpaṭi pala mukamaṉ moḻikaḷ kūṟiṉāṉ; tāṉ eppoḻutum pārkkakkūṭiya aṇmaiyil eṉṉai irukkumpaṭi ceytāṉ; eṉṉai aṉpuṭaṉ pārttāṉ; appārvaiyāl eṉatu elumpukaḷ urukumpaṭi kuḷirntaṉa; eṉatu āṭaiyil īrum pēṉum iruntu āṭci ceytaṉa; atu pala taiyalkaḷiṭap paṭṭatāyum viyarvaiyāl naṉaintatāyum iruntatu. avaṉ eṉatu paḻaiya āṭaiyaik kaḷaiyumpaṭi ceytāṉ. ataṟkup patil nūlpōṉaviṭam etu eṉṟu aṟiya muṭiyāta nuṇmaiyuṭaiyatum, pū vēlaippāṭuṭaiyatumākiya pāṭṭāṭaiyai uṭukkumpaṭi tantāṉ. araṇ maṉaiyilē aḻakiya āparaṇaṅkaḷai aṇinta peṇkaḷ iruntārkaḷ. avarkaḷ pāṭṭālum kūttālum aracaṉai makiḻvittārkaḷ. avarkaḷ poṉ kiṇṇattil 1kaḷḷai vārttut tantārkaḷ; avarkaḷ tarattara vaḻi naṭanta varuttam tīrum paṭi ataṉai niṟaiya uṇṭēṉ; uṇṭataṉāl itu varaiyum kaḷḷuṇṇa villaiyē eṉṉum varuttattaip pōkkiṉēṉ. mālaik kālattil kaḷippu mikuntavaṉāy araṇmaṉaiyiṉ oru puṟattil taṅkiṉēṉ; tavañ ceyvār tamatu uṭampai aḻiyaviṭātu attavattāṟ peṟum payaṉaip peṟuvatupōla vaḻi naṭanta varuttam ciṟitum illāmal pōkkiṉēṉ. kaḷ ḷuṇṭataṉāl uṇṭāṉa meyvaruttamallātu vēṟu varuttam yātumiṉṟi nittirai viṭṭeḻuntēṉ. aracaṉaik kāṇpataṟku mutalnāḷ colluk kaṭaṅkāta vaṟumaiyuṭaiyavaṉāyiruntēṉ. aracaṉaik kaṇṭa maṟṟa nāṭ kālaiyil eṉṉaik kaṇṭōr, nēṟṟu vantavaṉ ivaṉallaṉ eṉa aiyuṟṟa ṉar. nāṉ palavakai naṟumaṇap poruḷkaḷai uṭaliṟ pūciyiruntēṉ; ataṉāl vaṇṭukaḷ iṭaiyaṟātu eṉṉai moyttaṉa. itu kaṉavāyiruk kalāmeṉṟu niṉaittēṉ. itu kaṉavaṉṟu naṉavu eṉṟu eṉatu neñcu tuṇintu colliṟṟu. koṭiya vaṟumaiyāl varuntiya eṉatu cuṟṟattiṉar makiḻcci yaṭaintaṉar. “ippoḻutu māṟutalaṭaintirunta pōtum nēṟṟu vanta iravalaṉē nī” eṉṟu eṉṉuṭaṉ vanta iḷaiñar coṉṉārkaḷ. ataṉaik kēṭṭa aracaṉ avarkaḷaik kāvalāḷarkaḷ mūlam aḻaittu avarkaḷukkuc ceyyum muṟaimaikaḷaic ceytaṉuppiṉāṉ. yāṉum eṉ cuṟṟattiṉarum pullāṟṟiritta paḻutaiyait tiṉṟu vaḷarnta cemmaṟikkiṭā yiṉ puḻukkiya iṟaicciyai uṇṭōm; irumpukkampiyil kōttup poritta koḻutta iṟaiccip poriyalai vāyiṉ iṭappuṟattum valap puṟattum cērtti āṟṟi uṇṭu kaḷittōm. ivvāṟu iṟaiccip pori yalaiyum iṟaiccip puḻukkalaiyum uṇṭu aluttōm. iṉṉum pala vaṭi viṉaiyuṭaiya palakāraṅkaḷaiyum uṇṭōm. kaḷḷuṇṭu pala nāṭkaḷaik kaḻittōm. nīṅkaḷ cōṟum uṇṇutal vēṇṭum, eṉṟu kūṟi mullai mukai pōṉṟa cōṟṟaiyum porik kaṟiyaiyum kaḻuttuvaraiyum nirampum paṭi aracaṉ uṇṇattantāṉ. pakalu miravum iṟaicciyait tiṉṟataṉāl eṅkaḷ paṟkaḷ kollai nilattai uḻutakoḻuppōla muṉai maḻuṅkip pōyiṉa. iḷaippāṟa nēramiṉṟi uṇavukaḷai uṇṭu veṟuttōm. nāṅkaḷ namatu ūrukkuc cellutal vēṇṭumeṉa orunāḷ avaṉiṭattil metuvākak kūṟiṉōm. ataik kēṭṭuk kōpittavaṉpōl avaṉ yām varuntumpaṭi pārttu, “ematu kūṭṭattai viṭṭup pōkiṉṟīrō” eṉak kūṟiṉāṉ. avaṉ yāṉaikaḷaik kaṉṟukaḷuṭaṉ koṭuttāṉ; iṉṉum ūrtikaḷ, āṭaikaḷ, aṇikalaṉkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟaiyum tantāṉ. avaṟṟai vārikkoṇṭu iṉi ekkālamum emakku vaṟumai illaiyākumpaṭi nām varukiṉṟōm.
karikālaṉiṉ ciṟappukkaḷ
iravalaṉē! karikāṟ peruvaḷattāṉiṉ ciṟappukkaḷ cilavaṟṟaik kūṟu kiṉṟēṉ kēḷ! avaṉ murukak kaṭavuḷiṉ cīṟṟampōṉṟa kōpamuṭaiya vaṉ. avaṉ tāy vayiṟṟiliruntē aracurimaiyaip peṟṟup piṟantāṉ. tavaḻuṅkālam toṭṭē avaṉ mika valimai peṟṟiruntāṉ. avaṉ ātti mālaiyait tarittiruppāṉ. paṉaṅkuruttiṉāṟ kaṭṭiya mālaiyaṇinta cēraṉaiyum, arattiṉvāy pōṉṟa vēppilaiyāṟ ṟoṭutta mālaiyait talaiyilaṇinta pāṇṭiyaṉaiyum avaṉ veṇṇi eṉṉum pōrk kaḷattē porutu veṉṟāṉ.
iravalaṉē! avaṉmuṉ nī uṉatu cuṟṟattuṭaṉ uṉatu vaṟumai tōṉṟumpaṭi niṟpāyāyiṉ kaṟṟā taṉatu kaṉṟukkup pāl curantu koṭukka virumpuvatu pōla avaṉ uṅkaḷ vaṟumai nīṅkap pōtumāṉa paricil taruvāṉ; uṅkaḷ pāṭalkaḷaik kēṭpataṉmuṉ koṭṭaip pāciyiṉ vēr pōla aḻukkaiyum taiyalkaḷaiyumuṭaiya uṅkaḷ tuṇikaḷaik kaḷaiyum paṭi ceyvāṉ; ciṟu kuñcaṅkaḷaik karaiyiluṭaiya paṭṭāṭaikaḷai uṭukkumpaṭi taruvāṉ; pūmaṇaṅ kamaḻkiṉṟa kāramuḷḷa kaḷḷaip pōtum pōtum eṉṉumpaṭi poṉ vaḷḷattil vārttu nāḷtōṟum nalkuvāṉ; pāṭiṉiyiṉ curuṇṭa mayiriṭattē poṟṟāmarai malaraic cūṭṭuvāṉ; aṇintukoḷvataṟkup poṉṉari mālaiyaiyum muttumālaiyaiyum koṭuppāṉ. yāṉait tantattiṉāl ceyta tāmarai mukaiyiṉaiyuṭaiya tēril nāṉku kutiraikaḷaippūṭṭi atil uṉṉai ēṟṟi ēḻaṭi piṉ ceṉṟu vaḻiviṭuvāṉ; piṉ “nilaiyillā ulakattup pukaḻai niṉaintu nī peṟṟa paricilaip piṟarkkuṅ koṭuttuc celluti” eṉak kūṟip pala maruta nilattu ūrkaḷaiyum, pala yāṉaikaḷaiyun taruvāṉ.
cōḻanāṭṭu vaḷam
cōḻanāṭu mikka ceḻippuṭaiyatu. tiṭarkaḷ tōṟum neṟkūṭukaḷ (kutir)kiṭakkum. makkaḷ teṉṉañ cōlaikaḷiṉ naṭuvē vīṭukaḷamaittu vāḻvar. kākkaikaḷ irattam aḷainta cōṟṟai uṇṇum; uṇṭu vīṭṭaic cūḻnta noccic ceṭiyiṉ niḻaliṟ poritta āmaippārppukaḷai eṭuttup pacikkum pōtu uṇpataṟku maṟaittuvaikkum. kaṭaṟkarai maṇaṟ kuṉṟukaḷil uḻattiyar vaṇṭal iḻaittu viḷaiyāṭuvar. pākaṟ paḻattai yum palāppaḻattaiyum tiṉṟa mayiṟ cēvalkaḷ vaḷainta kāñci marattilum marutilum iruntu peṭaikaḷ aḻaikkumpōtu paṟantu cellum; ceṉṟu yāḻiṉ ōcai pōṉṟa vaṇṭiṉ pāṭalaik kēṭṭu ataṟkup porunta nilavupōṉṟa maṇalil niṉṟu āṭum.
nāṭṭilē karumpum nellum ceḻittuvaḷarum; aṭumpum pakaṉṟaiyum, puṉkum, ñāḻalum ēṉaiya maraṅkaḷum neruṅki niṟkum. aṅkuṟaivōr nāṭṭu vāḻkkaiyai veṟuttārāyiṉ nīla niṟamuṭaiya mullaik koṭipaṭarnta kāṭu cārnta nilattē ceṉṟu vāḻvar. aṅkut taḷavum, mullaiyum, tēṟṟāvum, koṉṟaiyum kāyāvumākiya marañ ceṭikoṭikaḷ taḻaittu niṟkum. mullai maruta nilattu vāḻvōr aṅku vāḻtalai veṟuttārāyiṉ vēṟu nilattē ceṉṟu vāḻvar. kaṭaliṭattē iṟāl mīṉait tiṉṟa nārai puṉṉaik kompil vantu taṅkum; piṉpu puṉṉai marattiṉmītu kaṭal cintukiṉṟa nīriṉ āravārattukkup payantu eḻuntu vāḻai marattil taṅkum. kaṭaṟkarai ūrkaḷilē kulaikaḷaiyuṭaiya teṅkukaḷum, kulaikkamukum, kulaivāḻaikaḷum, pūkkaḷ malarnta curapuṉṉaiyum kāṭci aḷikkum. avaṟṟiṉ ceṟivilē pērāntaikaḷ uṟaiyum. aṅku uṟaiyum paratavar neytal nilattai veṟuttārkaḷāṉāl kuṟiñci nilattē ceṉṟu vāḻvar; tēṉum kiḻaṅkum viṟṟa kuṟiñci nila makkaḷ mīṉiṉ neyyōṭu kaḷḷaiyum paṇṭamāṟṟuc ceytu pōvar. maruta nilattu vīṭukaḷil niṟkum kōḻikaḷ tiṉaiyai uṇṇum. malaiyiṭattē tirikiṉṟa mantikaḷ uppaṅkaḻiyil mūḻkum; kaḻiyiliruntu eḻumpiya nāraikaḷ malaiyiṭattut taṅkum. ivvakai nāṉku nilaṅkaḷum mayaṅki irukkum ulakattukku aruḷ ceytaṟkuc ceṅkōlai eṭutta talaivaṉ vāḻvāṉāka!
veyyiliṉ veppa mikutiyāl tāmaraiyum kullaiyum tīyum; marak komparkaḷ kariyum; malaiyiṭattu aruvikaḷ vaṟaḷum; mēkaṅkaḷ kaṭalnīrai mukattal ceyyā. ivvakaiyāṉa vaṟaṭcik kālattum nuraiyait talaiyiluṭaiya kāvirinīr naṟaik koṭiyaiyum, narantam pullaiyum, akilaiyum, cantaṉamarattaiyum nīrāṭum tuṟaikaḷtōṟum taḷḷik kuḷatti lum marutanilat tūrkaḷilum pukum. nīrāṭu makaḷir nīr viḷaiyāṭuvar. nel aṟuppōr arivāḷāl arinta neṟpōriṉai malaipōl uyarumpaṭi aṭukkuvar. uḻavar tiṉamum kaṭāviṭṭup pōriṉai uḻakki nellait tiraṭṭuvar; tiraṭṭiya nellaik kutirkaḷ niṟaiyumpaṭi koṭṭuvar. varampu kaḷaiyuṭaiya vēlinilattil āyiram kalam nel viḷaiyum. karikāṟ peruvaḷattāṉ ivvāṟu vaḷam poruntiya kāviri nāṭṭukkut talaivaṉ. (iravalaṉē! nī kālantāḻātu ippoḻutē avaṉpāṟ ceṉṟu pāṭi uṉatum uṉatu cuṟṟattiṉaratum vaṟumaiyaik kaṭitiṟpōkki iṉitu vāḻvāyāka.)
3. 1ciṟupāṇāṟṟuppaṭai
mutuvēṉiṟ kālam
mūṅkilait tōḷākavuṭaiya nilamakaḷukku malaikaḷ mārpiṭattē poruntiya periya taṉaṅkaḷākum. malai ucciyiṉiṉṟum iṟaṅki āṟṟiṭaik kuṟaikaḷil piḷavupaṭṭup piṉ oṉṟu cērntu cellum kāṭṭāṟu avvaḻakiya taṉaṅkaḷil aṇiyappaṭum muttu vaṭamākum. kāṭṭāṟṟiṉāl kuḷircciyaṭainta maraṅkaḷ malarkaḷai niṟaiyap pūkkum. kuyilkaḷ alakiṉāl kōtutalāl utirnta pūkkaḷ ularntu karumai aṭaintirukkum; attōṟṟam iḷavēṉilākiya mātu taṉatu kūntalai virittāṟ pōṉṟatu. kāṭṭiṭattē vēlpōṟ kūriya parukkaik kaṟkaḷ kiṭakkum; mutuvēṉiṟ kālattil veyilāl veppamaṭainta avai vaḻi naṭappōriṉ kālkaḷaik kiḻikkum; ākavē avarkaḷ meṉmaiyāka naṭantu kaṭappamara niḻalil iruntu iḷaippāṟip pōvar. mutuvēṉiṟ kālam ñāyiṟṟiṉ veppattāl pālaittaṉmai uṭaiyatāyirukkum. pālait taṉmaiyāvatu kālaiyum mālaiyum naṇpakalaṉṉa kaṭumai kūrac cōlai tēmpik kūval māṟi nīrum niḻalumiṉṟi nilam payantuṟantu puḷḷum māvum pulampuṟṟu iṉpamiṉṟit tuṉpam perukuvatoru kālam.
viṟaliyiṉ tōṟṟam
ivvāṟu pālaittaṉmaiyuṭaiya kāṭṭuvaḻiyē, aḻakiya mullai cūṭutaṟkamainta kaṟpuṭaiya viṟali naṭattaliṉāl kaḷaippaṭaintu kaṭampamara niḻalil taṅki irukkiṉṟāḷ. avaḷuṭaiya nōkku māṉ pōl maruṇṭa taṉmaiyuṭaiyatu; kūntal karumaiyuṭaiyatu; aṭikaḷ ōṭi iḷaitta nāyiṉ nākkup pōṉṟavai; vāḻaittaṇṭupōṟ paruttu oṉṟōṭu oṉṟu neruṅkiya toṭaikaḷ yāṉaiyiṉ tutikkai pōṉṟavai; koṇṭai vāḻaip potti pōṉṟatu; aṅku tēmal paṭarnta taṉaṅkaḷ kōṅkiṉ mukai pōṉṟavai; paṟkaḷ aḻaku vāyntavai.
pāṇaṉ
pāṇaṉ poṟkampi pōṉṟu muṟukkiya narampuṭaiya cīṟiyāḻai iṭap pakkattē taḻuvi naṭṭa pāṭai eṉṉum paṇṇai vācikkiṉṟāṉ. paricil aḷikkum vaḷḷalkaḷaip peṟāmal kāṭṭiṭattē ippāṇaṉ ivvāṟu iruntu varuntum pōtu, nallik kōṭaṉaip pāṭip paricil peṟṟu mīḷkiṉṟa oru pāṇaṉ avaṉai etirppaṭṭuk kūṟukiṉṟāṉ:
cēra nāṭṭiṉ ceḻumai
pāṇar talaivaṉē! mēṟkē uḷḷa cēranāṭu mikka ceḻippuṭaiyatu. aṅku erumaikaḷ koḻutta mīṉkaḷ noṟuṅkumpaṭi vayalkaḷil naṭantuceṉṟu ceṅkaḻunīrp pūvai uṇṇum; uṇṭu miḷaku paṭarnta palāmara niḻalil kāṭṭu mallikaiyākiya paṭukkaiyil kiṭakkum; kāṟṟukkuk koṭi acaiyum; mañcaḷiṉ melliya ilaikaḷ ataṉ mutukait taṭavum; ivvāṟu kiṭantu atu tāṉ uṇṭa ceṅkaḻunīrppūvaittēṉ nāṟumpaṭi meṉṟu acaiyiṭṭut tuyilkoḷḷum. ivvāṟu ceḻumai mikunta nāṭukaḷai yum, valiya tōḷiṉaiyum, ōṭukiṉṟa tēriṉaiyumuṭaiya cēraṉatu vañcimūtūril kiṭaikkum paricilō nalliyak kōṭaṉ koṭukkum paricilaiviṭak kuṟaivuṭaiyatu.
koṟkai nakariṉ vaḷam
teṟkē uḷḷa koṟkai nakarukkut talaivaṉ pāṇṭiyaṉ. annakar tirai koḻikkum kaṭalai ellaiyākavuṭaiyatu. kaṭaṟkaraikaḷiliruntu uppu vāṇikariṉ vaṇṭikaḷ cellum. 1avvaṇṭikaḷiṉ accuttaṅkum kuṭaṅkaḷ tātu paṉikkiṉṟa pūkkaḷuṭaiya muṟṟiya nuṇāttaṭiyai uḷiyiṉāl kaṭaintu ceytu cevvarakku pūcappaṭṭavai. kiṭēccaiyāl ceyta pūmālaiyai ceviyaṭiyil neṟṟimālaiyāka aṇiyap peṟṟa valiya erutukaḷ vaṇṭiyai iḻuttuc cellum. uppu vāṇikar piḷḷaikaḷaip pōla vaḷarkkiṉṟa kuraṅkukaḷ vaṇṭikaḷōṭu varum. uppu vāṇikariṉ kuḻantaikaḷ peṇkaḷiṉ paṟkaḷ pōṉṟa veḷḷiya muttaiyum vāḷpōṉṟa kiḷiñcilaiyum 2araiyilē āparaṇamāka aṇintiruppar. akkuḻantaikaḷōṭu mantikaḷ kilukiluppaiyaik kaiyil vaittuk kilukki viḷaiyāṭum. tamatu kaṇavarait taḻuvum umaṭṭiyar kūntalai aintu vakaiyākak kōti muṭintiruppar. ivvāṟu ciṟappuṭaiya koṟkai nakarai yum veṇkoṟṟak kuṭaiyaiyum tēraiyum muttu mālaiyaiyumuṭaiya pāṇṭiyaṉiṭattiṟ ceṉṟu peṟum paricililum pārkka nalliyakkōṭaṉiṭam peṟum paricil periyatākum.
cōḻa nāṭṭiṉ ciṟappu
cōḻanāṭṭiluḷḷa maruta nilattē malaipōla vaḷarnta kaṭampa maraṅkaḷ nerukkamākap pūttirukkum. ampūkkaḷiṉ tampalap pūccikaḷaip pōṉṟa tātu utirntu parantiruttalāl nīruṇṇum tuṟaiyiṉ karai ōviyam pōl aḻaku peṟṟu viḷaṅkum. attuṟaiyiṭattē malarntirukkum tāmarai malarākiya pīṭattil tumpikaḷ tamatu peṭaiyait taḻuvi makiḻntiruntu ‘cīkāmaram’ eṉṉum paṇṇaippāṭum. kiḻakkup pakkattē ivvāṟu ceḻippu mikkuḷḷa nāṭṭukkut talaivaṉākiya cōḻaṉ tūṅkeyil eṟinta vīravaḷai viḷaṅkum kaiyuṭaiyaṉ. avvaracaṉatu uṟantai nakariṟ ceṉṟu peṟum paricil nalliyak kōṭaṉiṭam peṟum paricilaiviṭac ciṟiyatākum.
paruvam poyyātu maḻai peytalāl. celvamikunta malaip pakkattē tiriyum mayil kūviyataik kēṭṭa pēkaṉ eṉṉum vaḷḷal atu kuḷir mikutiyāl kūvukiṉṟatu eṉa eṇṇit taṉatu pōrvaiyai ataṟku aḷittāṉ. ciṟiya pūkkaḷaiyuṭaiya mullaik koṭi, ciṟupuṉṉaiyiṉ naṟiyapū tēṉ tuḷikkiṉṟa vaḻiyiṭattē koḻukom piṉṟik koḻuntu eṟintu niṉṟatu. ataṉaik kaṇṭa paṟampumalaikkut talaivaṉākiya pāri atu paṭartaṟkut taṉatu tēraik koṭuttāṉ.
koṭaip perum vaḷḷal eḻuvar
kāri eṉṉum vaḷḷal maṇiyaiyum talaiyāṭṭattaiyu muṭaiya kutiraiyōṭu taṉatu nāṭṭaiyum irappavarkkuk koṭuttāṉ. āy eṉṉum vaḷḷal pāmpu īṉṟu koṭutta oḷiyuṭaiya nīlap paṭṭāṭaiyai āliṉ kīḻ amarnta kaṭavuḷukku aṉpu mikutiyāl koṭuttāṉ.
iḷamaiyaik koṭuttaliṉ amiḻtattiṉ taṉmaiyuṭaiya nellikkaṉiyaik kamukam pūkkaḷuṭaiya pakkamalaiyiṉiṉṟu peṟṟa atikaṉ ataṉai oḷavaiyārukku aḷittāṉ. naḷḷi eṉṉum vaḷḷal taṉṉai naṭpuc ceytōr makiḻntu illaṟam naṭattutaṟku vēṇṭum poruḷkaḷai avarkku nāṭōṟum nalkiṉāṉ.
kāri eṉṉum kutiraiyaip peṟṟa kāri eṉṉum vaḷḷaluṭaṉ pōr ceyta ōri eṉṉum vaḷḷal mutirnta curapuṉṉaiyum kuṟiya malaikaḷu muṭaiya nallanāṭukaḷaik kūttāṭuvōrukkuk koṭuttāṉ.
nalliyak kōṭaṉiṉ ōṅku pukaḻ
pāṇar talaiva! ivveḻuvaruṭaiya koṭaikaḷaiyum tāṅki niṟkum oruvaṉākiya nalliyak kōṭaṉiṭattu nī uṉatu cuṟṟattuṭaṉ celvā yāka. avaṉ pakaivaratu mārpilum mukattilum veṭṭiṉa vīravāḷaiyum, pulipōṉṟa valimaiyaiyu muṭaiyaṉ; ōviyar kuṭiyil utittōṉ. naṟum pūkkaḷaiyuṭaiya curapuṉṉaiyaiyum, akilaiyum, cantaṉamarattaiyum nīrāṭun tuṟaiyil puṉalāṭum makaḷirkkut teppamākat tarukiṉṟa nīrmōtum māvilaṅkaiyiṉ aracar tōṉṟutaṟku iṭaṉāyuḷḷa ōviyar kuṭiyiṟ piṟantōṉ. avaṉatu piṉṉiṭāta aṭikaḷil vīrakkaḻal kiṭantu acaiyum; maḻaipōṉṟu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟa avaṉ kaikaḷō kūttarai varavēṟṟup piṭikaḷaik koṭukkum.
paricil peṟa muṉ iravalaṉiṉ vaṟumai
iravalaṉē! yāṅkaḷ nalliyakkōṭaṉaiyum avaṉ tantaiyiṉ malaiyiṭattēyuḷḷa celvattaiyum pāṭic ceṉṟēm. appoḻutu namatu vaṟumai collukkaṭaṅkātatāka viruntatu. namatu vīṭṭil tāyiṉ mulai uṇṇāmal irukkamāṭṭāta nāykkuṭṭikaḷ paci mikutiyāl cattamiṭṭaṉa. aṭukkaḷaiyiṉ iṟapput taṭikaḷ kaṭṭaṟuntu kīḻē viḻuntaṉa. cuvaril eḻunta kaṟaiyāṉ vīṭṭiṉ variccukaḷai uṇṭatu. oṭuṅkiya nuṇṇiya iṭaiyum vaḷaiyalaṇinta kaiyum uṭaiyavaḷum paciyāl varuntukiṉṟavaḷumākiya kiṇaiyaṉuṭaiya makaḷ kuppaiyiṉiṉṟum nakattiṉāṟ kiḷḷiya kīraiyai uppillāmal camaittuk kariya cuṟṟattuṭaṉ paci nīṅkumpaṭi uṇṭāḷ. ivvakaiyāṉa koṭiya vaṟumaiyum paciyum iṉi namakku orupōtum illaiyākumpaṭi nām matañcoriyum yāṉaikaḷaiyum periya tēraiyum peṟṟu avviṭattiṉiṉṟum varukiṉṟōm.
neytalnilam
iravalaṉē! kaṭaṟkaraiyiṭattuḷḷa tāḻai aṉṉam pōl pūvai īṉum; cerunti poṉ pōṟ pūvai malarum; kaḻiyiṭattuḷḷamuḷḷi nīlampōṟ pūvai malarttum; puṉṉai muttuppōla arumpukaḷai mukiḻk kum. ivvakaik kaḻicūḻnta ūrkaḷaiyum kuḷircci poruntiya kuḷaṅkaḷai yumuṭaiya eyiṟpaṭṭiṉattē celvāyāka. aṅku muṟukkaviḻnta arumpu kaḷāl kaṭṭiya mālaiyai aṇintavaṉum tōṭṭaṅkaḷ pala uṭaiyavaṉu mākiyakiṭaṅkil eṉṉum ūrkkaracaṉaik kāṇpāy. avaṉaip pāṭuvāyā yiṉ avaṉ ataṟku makiḻntu viṟaliyum uṉatu cuṟṟamum makiḻntu uṇ ṇumpaṭi cuṭṭa kuḻalmīṉait taruvāṉ; paratavar, nīviruṇṇumpaṭi kaḷḷai yum tarukuvar. avar uṅkaḷukkuk koṭukkum kaḷ, tiraiyiṉāṟ koṇṭu varappaṭṭu uṟaṅkukiṉṟa oṭṭakam pōṟkuvikkappaṭṭa akil viṟakaip periya tōḷum mati pōl maṟuvillāta mukamum vēlpōṉṟa kaṇṇu muṭaiya nuḷaicciyar tīmūṭṭi erittuk kāycci arittatākum.
mullai nilam
neytal nilattaik kaṭantu kāṭṭuvaḻiyē celvāyāṉāl aṅku avarai pavaḷampōl pūttirukkum; kāyā mayiliṉ kaḻuttuppōl pūtta kariya arumpukaḷai uṭaiyatāyirukkum; kāntaṭkoṭikaḷ kaiviralkaḷ pōl malarntu kāṭciyaḷikkum; vaḻiyiṟ cellumiṭattu vāṉavil tōṉṟum; mullaik koṭikaḷ paṭarntirukkum. veṭippukaḷil nīr kutikkum aruvikaḷaiyuṭaiya malaiyil ñāyiṟu maṟaiyum. ivvāṟu eḻum mālaik kālattē nī vēlūrai aṭaivāyāyiṉ nī uṉatu cuṟṟattuṭaṉē uṇṭu paci tīrumpaṭi camaitta cōṟṟaip puḷiṅkaṟiyōṭum āmāṉiṉ cūṭṭiṟaicci yōṭum eyiṟṟiyar taruvar.
āmūriṉ maruta nila vaḷam
mālai toṭuttāṟ pōṉṟu malarnta pūkkaḷuṭaiya kiḷaitta kāñcimarattiṉ kiḷaikaḷil irukkum poṉṉiṟa vāyiṉaiyum nīla maṇiyiṉ niṟattiṉaiyumuṭaiya ciccilip paṟavai nīril mūḻki mīṉkaḷaip piṭikkum. ciccilip paṟavaiyiṉ kālkaḷ kiḻitta ilaikaḷuṭaiya tāmarai yiṉ pūkkaḷil tēṉ nukarkiṉṟa nīla niṟattiṉaiyum civanta kaṇṇiṉaiyu muṭaiya vaṇṭukaḷ tiṅkaḷaic cērnta karum pāmpu pōlat tōṉṟum.
ivvāṟu aḻakiya vayalkaḷaiyuṭaiya āmūr, ariya kāvalaiyum, akaṉṟa vīṭukaḷaiyum, antaṇar kuṭiyiruppaiyum uṭaiyatu. vīṭṭiṉ uḷḷē irukkum uḻavaruṭaiya maṉaiviyar yāṉaiyiṉ tutikkai pōṉṟu piṉṉāl toṅkum piṉṉiya kūntalaiyum, vaḷai aṇinta kaiyiṉaiyu muṭaiya tam piḷḷaikaḷaikkoṇṭu num ellōraiyum pōkātapaṭi taṭuppar; taṭuttup pūṇulakkaiyāṟ kuttiya ariciyaic camaitta cōṟṟai naṇṭum pīrkkaṅkāyum kalanta puḻukkuṭaṉ uṇṇumpaṭi taruvar.
nalliyakkōṭaṉiṉ mūtūr
nalliyak kōṭaṉatu viḻāk koṇṭāṭum mūtūr cēymaiyi laṉṟu. mēl nōkki erikiṉṟa neruppuc cāyntāl otta nākkum, veḷḷāṭṭu maṟikaḷ kiṭantu acaikiṉṟa kātum, kavaitta kālumuṭaiya pēymakaḷ niṇattait tiṉṟu cirikkumpōtu viḷaṅkit tōṉṟukiṉṟa paṟkaḷ pōṉṟa vum, pōrkkaḷattē piṇattalaikaḷaip periya kālkaḷāl taḷḷutalāl civappēṟiṉavumākiya nakaṅkaḷaiyuṭaiya yāṉaikaḷiṉ mataaruvi cintit teruvil eḻum tūciyai aṭakkum.
avaṉatu kōpuravāyil pāṇarukkum aṟivuṭaiyōrukkum aṭaikkappaṭātu
kōpuravāyil mēru kaṇviḻittup pārttāṟpōl, tōṉṟum. kiṇaip porunarukkum, aṟivuṭaiyōrukkum antaṇarukkum ataṉ vāyil orupōtum aṭaikkappaṭātu; ēṉaiyōrukku aṭaikkappaṭ ṭirukkum. aracaṉ piṟar taṉakkuc ceyta naṉṟiyaic ceyvāṉ; aṟivum oḻukkamum illātār avaṉiṭattil iṭam peṟār.
nalliyakkōṭaṉiṉ iṉiya paṇpukaḷ
taṉṉai nōkkiṉārkku avaṉ ekkālamum iṉiya mukamuṭaiyaṉ. taṉatu vīrattaik kaṇṭu añci aṭipaṇintārkkut taṉṉuṭaṉ iruppōr pukaḻumpaṭi aruḷuvāṉ; pōrmukattē keṭṭa taṉatu paṭaiyait tāṅki vāḷvaliyiṟ ciṟanta vīrar pukaḻumpaṭi tāṉ virumpiyavaṟṟai muṭippāṉ; peṇkaḷ vacattaṉākāta taṉmaiyaṉ; paricilar taramaṟintu avar peṟattak kavaṟṟaik koṭukkum iyalpiṉaṉ. kalvi yillāta paricil vēṇṭuvōrk kum koṭātiruttalai mēṟkoḷḷātu avarkaḷ tarattukkut takkavaṟṟaik koṭuppāṉ; iyalicai nāṭakaṅkaḷālum iṉiya moḻikaḷālum makiḻc ciyait tarum kūṭṭattiṉarōṭu pala viṇ mīṉkaḷukku naṭuvirukkum matiyam pōl viḷaṅkuvāṉ.
kuraṅkaṉ kaiyaip pāmpu cuṟṟiyatu pōṉṟa yāḻiṉ vārkkaṭṭu
iravalaṉē! nī ivviyalpuṭaiyaṉāy irukkum nalliyak kōṭaṉaikkaṇṭatum, kuraṅku pāmpiṉ talaiyaip piṭittāl appāmpu orukāl iṟukavum orukāl nekiḻavum ataṉ kaiyai eppaṭic cuṟṟumō appaṭiyē yāḻttaṇṭiṉiṭattē nekiḻa vēṇṭiya iṭattu nekiḻntum iṟuka vēṇṭiya iṭattu iṟukiyum cuṟṟiṉa narampu tuvakkum vārkkaṭṭum, kāvi niṟam ūṭṭappaṭṭa pōrvaiyum, amiḻtattait tammiṭattē potinta taṉmaiyuṭaiya narampukaḷuṭaiya cīṟiyāḻaik kaiyil eṭuppāyāka. eṭuttu aracaṉ, uvāttiyāyaṉ, tāy, tantai, tamaiyaṉ mutaliyōrkkup palakālum kuvitta kaiyai yuṭaiyavaṉē! vīrar eṟitaṟku makiḻntu koṭutta mārpaiyuṭaiyavaṉē! ēriṉaiyuṭaiya kuṭikaḷukku niḻal ceyyum ceṅkōluṭaiyavaṉē! eṉṟu avaṉatu cila pukaḻkaḷaik kūṟuvāyāka.
aracaṉ pāṇarkkup paricilaḷikkum muṟaimai
nī avvāṟu kūṟuvataṉmuṉ mūṅkil āṭaiyai urittāṟpōṉṟa kuṟṟamillāta uṭaiyai uṭukkumpaṭi taruvāṉ. pāmpiṉ nañcu ēṟi mayaṅkiṉāṟ pōla mayakkam tarum kaṭṭeḷi vaittaruvāṉ. vīmacēṉaṉatu maṭainūlil kūṟiya muṟaippaṭi camaitta cōṟṟaip poṟkalatti liṭṭut tāṉ uṭaṉ niṉṟu uṇṇumpaṭi ceyvāṉ. muṭivēntariṉ araṇkaḷai aḻittu appakaivar nāṭṭiṟ peṟṟa poruḷaik koṇṭu tammai virumpivantavarkaḷatum, pāṇaratum vaṟumaiyaip pōkkuvāṉ. taccar uḷikoṇṭu vēlaippāṭu ceyta tērōṭu yāṉai, kutirai, aṇikalaṉ mutaliyavaṟṟait tantu uṉṉaic cellumpaṭi vaḻi viṭuvāṉ. ātalāl, nī kuṟiñci nilattaiyuṭaiyavaṉum taḻaiviravit toṭutta mālai yaṇinta vaṉum perum pukaḻuṭaiyavaṉumākiya nalliyakkōṭaṉiṭattuc celvāyāka.
4. 1perumpāṇāṟṟuppaṭai
pāṇaṉum cuṟṟattiṉarum alaintu tirital
mutuvēṉiṟ kālattil orunāḷ pala katirkaḷaiyuṭaiya ñāyiṟu paranta ākāyattil niṟainta iruḷaippōkki viḷaṅkukiṉṟatu. ñāyiṟum tiṅkaḷum valañceykiṉṟa mēruvai uṭaiyatum kaṭal cūḻntatumākiya ivvulakilē tammai ātarippār iṉmaiyāl yāḻai iṭappakkattē aṇaitta perumpāṇaṉ oruvaṉ taṉatu kariya periya cuṟṟattuṭaṉ alaintu tirikiṉṟāṉ. avaṉ tōṭpuṟattē aṇaittirukkum yāḻ uḷnākkillāta vaṟuvāyuṭaiyatu; ataṉ kavaikkaṭai piṟai ēntiṉāṟ pōṉṟatu. vārkkaṭṭup peṇkaḷ kaiyil aṇiyum vaḷaiyalai oppa nekiḻavēṇṭiya iṭam nekiḻntu iṟuka vēṇṭiya iṭam iṟukappeṟṟatu; kariya periya taṇṭu nīlamaṇi oḻukiṉāṟpōṉṟatu; narampukaḷ iḻukkappaṭṭa poṟkampikaḷ pōṉṟaṉa; pōrvai viriyāta kamukam pūvaippōlat taiyalaḻuntumpaṭi tōlai mūṭṭic ceyyappaṭṭaṉa.
paricil peṟṟu varum pāṇaṉ perum pāṇaṉai etirppaṭṭatu
ivvakai yāḻai iṭattōḷmītu cārttiya pāṇaṉ, maḻai illāmaiyāl paḻutta marattait tēṭat tiriyum paṟavaipōla ōriṭattil taṅkāmal āvicūḻnta malaiyiṭattil ōṭittirikiṉṟāṉ. toṇṭaimāṉ iḷantiraiyaṉaippāṭi avaṉpāl paricil peṟṟu mīḷkiṉṟapāṇaṉ oruvaṉ ipperum pāṇaṉai vaḻiyiṭattē etirppaṭukiṉṟāṉ; avaṉ ivaṉuṭaiya melivaik kaṇṭu kūṟukiṉṟāṉ: poli viḻinta tōṟṟattiṉaiyum kalviyai veṟuttuk kūṟukiṉṟa nāviṉaiyum uṭaiya iravalaṉē! yāṉ iḷantiraiyaṉatu aracirukkaiyākiya kāñci nakariṉiṉṟum piṟarkkuk koṭukkak kuṟaivupaṭāta celvattaiyum, veḷḷiya talaiyāṭṭattai yuṭaiya kutiraikaḷaiyum yāṉaikaḷaiyum paricilāka vārikkoṇṭu varukiṉṟēṉ. cēracōḻa pāṇṭiyariṉum mikka talaimai yuṭaiyavaṉum cōḻar kuṭiyiṟ piṟantavaṉumākiya iḷantiraiyaṉai nīyum uṉatu cuṟṟat tiṉarum niṉaippīrāyiṉ uṅkaḷ vaṟumai oḻintuviṭum. nīvir cella vēṇṭiya vaḻiyiṉ iyalpiṉaik kūṟukiṉṟēṉ. kēṭpīrāka!
iravalaṉē! avaṉuṭaiya nakaril vaḻippōkkarai alaṟum paṭi veṭṭi avaṉ poruḷaik koḷḷai koḷḷum tīyōr illai. avaṉuṭaiya kāṭṭil iṭiyēṟum iṭiyātu; pāmpukaḷum tīṇṭā; puli mutaliya vilaṅkukaḷum tīṅkiḻaiyā. ivviyalpuṭaiya kāṭṭiṭattē nīṅkaḷ iruntu iḷaippāṟic celluṅkaḷ.
uppēṟṟic cellum umaṇar vaṇṭikaḷ
kāṭṭiṭattē cellum kavarpaṭṭa vaḻikaḷ uṇṭu; avvaḻi kaḷil umaṇariṉ uppēṟṟiya vaṇṭikaḷ cellum. avvaṇṭi kaḷiṉ accumaram taṅkum kuṭaṅkaḷ mattaḷattiṉ vaṭiviṉa. akkuṭaṅ kaḷil accumaram māṭṭappaṭṭirukkum. accumarattiṉūṭē ēṇik kāl pōṉṟu nīṇṭa tiraṇṭa maraṅkaḷ kaṭāvappaṭṭirukkum. am maraṅkaḷil kuttukkālkaḷ pōla uyaramāṉa kampukaḷ nāṭṭappaṭ ṭirukkum. kampukaḷ nāṭṭiya muṉpuṟam tottuḷippāyāl vēyappaṭ ṭirukkum. atu, tiṉaippuṉattai yāṉaimēyātu iruntu kāval ceyva taṟku amaitta paraṇmītu kaṭṭiya kuṭilaippōl tōṟṟamaḷikkum. ataṉ muṉpuṟattē kōḻik kūṭukaḷ kiṭakkum. mēlē marattiṉāṟ ceytatum yāṉaiyiṉ muḻantāḷ pōṉṟatumākiya uṟi tuḷai iṭṭuk kaṭṭit toṅka viṭappaṭṭirukkum. ataṉmītu, nāṭakamakaḷir āṭukaḷattil koṭṭappaṭum mattaḷattai oppa varintu kaṭṭappaṭṭa kāṭippāṉaikaḷ vaikkappaṭ ṭirukkum. umaṇap peṇ vaṇṭiyiṉ muṉpuṟattē yiruntu erutukaḷai aṭittu ōṭṭuvāḷ. avaḷ kaiyiṭattē piḷḷaiyaiyum ataṟkuk kāvalāka pūvōṭu kūṭiya vēppilaiyaiyum vaittiruppāḷ. valuvēṟiṉa uṭaliṉa rākiya umaṇar taḻaiyiṉāl toṭutta mālaiyai mārpiṭattē aṇin tiruppar; vaṇṭiyai iḻuttuc cellum erutukaḷ iḷaittu viṭṭāl avaikkup patil pūṭṭutaṟkup pala erutukaḷai aṭittu ōṭṭic celvar. pala tuḷaikaḷaiyuṭaiya nukattil orumukappaṭak kayiṟṟiṉāṟ kaṭṭiya pala erutukaḷ vaṇṭikaḷai iḻuttuc cellum. umaṇar avaṟṟiṉ pakkattē accu muṟiyātapaṭi kāttucceṉṟu uppiṉ vilaiyaik kūṟuvar. ivvakai vaṇṭikaḷ pala kavarpaṭṭa vaḻikaḷiṟ cellum.
vaṇikar miḷaku potikaḷaik kaḻutaikaḷil ēṟṟic cellum kāṭci
piṟa nāṭukaḷiṉiṉṟum vanta vaṇikar māṇikkam, muttu, cantaṉam mutaliyavaṟṟai evviṭaṅkaḷilum koṇṭu ceṉṟu viṟpar. avarkaḷiṉ mārpil ampukaḷ kiḻittalāl uṇṭāṉa puṇkaḷ āṟiya taḻumpukaḷ kiṭakkum. avarkaḷ araiyiṟkaṭṭiya uṭaiyilē uṭaivāḷ ceruki yiruppar; yāṉait tantattaik kaṭaintu vāḷaiyiṭṭu ataṉai variyuṭaiya tuṇiyil kōttut tōḷiṭattē toṅkaviṭṭiruppar; kālil cerupput tarittiruppar. ivviyalpiṉarākiya vaṇikar palāppaḻattaip pōṉṟu otta kaṉamākac cērkkappaṭṭa miḷaku potikaḷaic cumantu cellum kaḻutai kaḷiṉ piṉṉē kavarpaṭṭa vaḻikaḷāṟ celvar. kaḻutaikaḷukku nīṇṭa cevikaḷum mutukilē taḻumpukaḷum uṇṭu.
pālainila makkaḷiṉ kuṭicaikaḷ
ivvāṟu vaṇṭikaḷum kaḻutaikaḷum celkiṉṟa vaḻikaḷ kūṭukiṉṟa cantiyil aracaṉāl niyamikkappaṭṭa cuṅkaṅkoḷvōr viṟpaṭaiyuṭaṉ kāttu niṟpar. avar taṅkiniṟkum kāṭṭiṭattil eyiṉar kuṭiyiruppu uṇṭu. eyiṉariṉ ciṟu kuṭicaikaḷ aṇilum eliyum nuḻaiya muṭiyātapaṭi īntiṉ ōlaiyāl vēyappaṭṭu muṭpaṉṟiyiṉ mutukupōlat tōṉṟum. piḷḷai kaḷaip peṟṟa eyiṟṟiyar akkuṭicaikaḷiṉ orupuṟattē māṉ tōlil muṭaṅkik kiṭappar. ēṉaiya peṇkaḷ pāraiyiṉāl karampai nilattaik kiḷaṟi eṟumpu cērttuvaitta pullariciyai eṭuttu varuvar; eṭuttuvantu 1pārvaimāṉ kaṭṭi niṟkum viḷāmaram niḻal ceykiṉṟa muṟṟattilē tōṇṭappaṭṭuḷḷa nila uralil iṭuvar; iṭṭu vayira ulakkaiyiṉāl kuttuvar; āḻnta kiṇaṟṟil uvarnīrai moḷvar; moṇṭu koṇṭuvantu paḻaiya oṟuvāy pōṉa pāṉaiyilē ulaivārttu murinta aṭuppilē vaittu ākkuvar. avar kuṭicaikaḷai nīvir aṭaivīrāyiṉ avarkaḷ tām camaitta pullaricic cōṟṟai uppuk kaṇṭattuṭaṉ uṇṇumpaṭi tēk kilaiyil iṭṭuttaruvar.
kāṉavar paṉṟi vēṭṭaiyāṭutal
pālai nilattē nīriṉmaiyāl alaintu tirikiṉṟa māṉkaḷiṉ aṭiccuvaṭuṭaiya vaḻikaḷ uṇṭu. avvaḻikaḷiṉ pakkattē maḻai vaṟaṇṭa kālattu nīr niṟṟaṟkut tōṇṭiya kuḻikaḷ kāṇappaṭum. kāṉavar avaṟṟiṟ patuṅkiyiruntu naṭu iravil nīruṇṇa varukiṉṟa attippūppōṉṟu vaḷainta maruppuṭaiya paṉṟikaḷai vēṭṭaiyāṭuvar. pakaṟkālattē vaḷainta vēliyiṭattē valaikaḷai māṭṭi, aṅkānta vāyuṭaiya nāy kaḷuṭaṉē ceṉṟu putarkaḷai aṭittut tāmaraip pūviṉ puṟavitaḻ pōṉṟa ceviyuṭaiya muyalkaḷai avaṟṟiṟkiṭavāmal valaiyiṭattē cellumpaṭi ōṭṭi vēṭṭaiyāṭuvar.
ivviṭattēviṭṭu appāṟ celvīrkaḷāṉāl eyiṉariṉ kōṭṭai tōṉṟum. ataṉ uyarnta matiṟcuvar ūkampullāl vēyappaṭṭirukkum. uḷḷē muḷvēliyālum ataṉ puṟattē kāvaṟ kāṭṭālum cūḻappaṭṭa akaṉṟa vīṭukaḷ kāṇappaṭum. avaṟṟiṉ vāyilil caṅkiliyiṟ kaṭṭiya nāykaḷ kāval kāttu niṟkum. tiraṇṭa maraṅkaḷiṉāl vīṭukaḷiṉ uṭ katavukaḷ tāḻiṭappaṭṭirukkum. kaḻukukaḷ niraiyāka irukkum vīṭukaḷiṉ uṭpuṟattē pakaivaraik kuttiyatāl kūrmaḻuṅkiya vēlkaḷum, maṇi kaṭṭiya paricaikaḷum, viṟkaḷum, ampukaḷum cārttivaikkappaṭṭirukkum. talaivāyilkaḷil malaiyiṭattuḷḷa tēṉkūṭṭaip pōṉṟa kīḻppuṟattai uṭaiya ampuk kaṭṭukaḷum mikka ōcaiyuṭaiya paṟaiyum tūṅkum. iv viyalpiṉatākiya ūriṭattē celvīrāyiṉ īntiṉ vitaipōṉṟa cōṟṟai nāy vēṭṭaiyāṭik koṇṭuvanta caṅkumaṇi pōṉṟa muṭṭaikaḷai yuṭaiya uṭumpiṉ poriyalālē maṟaittu eyiṉar tara nīvir maṉaikaṭōṟum peṟukuvir.
kuṟiñci nilattu maṟavaṉ
pālai nilattaik kaṭantu appāṟ celvīrāyiṉ kuṟiñci nilattai aṭaivīrkaḷ. annilattut talaivaṉ tōṉṟiya maṟakkuṭiyiluḷḷōr valimaiyāl koḷḷaiyaṭittuṇṇum uṇaviṉaiyum, vāṭpōr ceyyum toḻilaiyumuṭaiyar. akkuṭiyiluḷḷa cūṟkoṇṭa makaḷir yāṉai etirppaṭṭālum, pāmpu tammītu ēṟi ūrntu ceṉṟālum, mēkam iṭintālum añcār. ivvāṟu maṟam mikka kuṭiyiṟ piṟanta annāṭṭut talaivaṉ taṉatu coṟkēḷāta pakaivaruṭaiya nāṭṭakattē tuṇaivar kaḷuṭaṉ kuṟivaitta vilaṅkait tappāmaṟ piṭikkiṉṟa vēṭṭai nāyaip pōṟ celvāṉ; avarkaḷiṉ pacukkūṭṭaṅkaḷaik kavarntuvantu kaḷḷukku vilaiyākak koṭuppāṉ. piṉ taṉatu vīṭṭilē vaṭikkappaṭṭa kaḷḷai uṇṭu palarum kūṭum ūrnaṭuvē uḷḷa marattaṭiyilē kiṭāyai aṟuttu ataṉ iṟaicciyai uṇṭu mattaḷaṅkoṭṭa iṭattōḷai valappuṟattē vaḷaittu makiḻcci uṇṭākumpaṭi āṭuvāṉ.
mullai nilattu ūr
ivviyalpiṉatākiya ūraik kaṭantu ceṉṟāl mullai nilattu ūrkaḷai aṭaivīrkaḷ. aṅku vīṭukaḷ kuṟukiya taṭikaḷaik kālāka iṟukki avaikaḷ mītu kaṭṭappaṭṭirukkum. kālkaḷil āṭṭu mantaikaḷ niṉṟu tiṉṉumpaṭi taḻaikaḷ kaṭṭappaṭṭirukkum. vīṭṭiṉ vāyilkaḷil ciṟu putarkaḷ kāṇappaṭum. vīṭṭiṉ katavukaḷ kayiṟṟāl variyappaṭṭavai. kayiṟṟi ṉāl varintu varaku vaikkōl parappappaṭṭa paṭukkaiyil kiṭāyiṉ tōlai virittu ataṉ mītu mutiyavaṉ kāvalākap paṭuttiruppāṉ. muṟṟattē aṟaiyappaṭṭuḷḷa muḷaikaḷil pacukkaḷaik kaṭṭum kayiṟukaḷ kiṭakkum ūraic cūḻntu vēliyiṉ pakkattē veḷḷāṭum cemmaṟiyāṭum paṭut tirukkum.
mōr viṟkum iṭaippeṇ
ivviyalpiṉatākiya mullai nilattu ūriṭattē paṟavaikaḷ tuyil eḻukiṉṟa viṭiyaṟkālattē iṭaip peṇkaḷ mattukaḷ āravārikkumpaṭi tayiraik kaṭaintu veṇṇeyai eṭuppar. tāḷuruvi acaikiṉṟa kātum poṉṉiṟa mēṉiyumuṭaiya āymakaḷ puḷḷiyākat tayirteṟitta pāṉaiyi luḷḷa mōraip pūvāṟceyta cummāṭṭiṉ mītu talaiyilvaittuk kuṟiñci nilattē ceṉṟu viṟpāḷ; viṟṟataṉāl kiṭaitta nel mutaliyavaṟṟaik koṇṭu cuṟṟattārai uṇṇumpaṭi ceyvāḷ. kuṟiya kariya kūntalai yuṭaiya iṭaimakaḷ tāṉ neyyai viṟṟavilaikkuk kaṭṭiyākiya pacum poṉṉai vāṅkāḷāy pālerumaiyaiyum, nalla pacuvaiyum erumai nākaiyum koḷvāḷ. ivviyalpiṉarākiya peṇkaḷum vāyaimaṭittuc cīḻkkai aṭikkiṉṟa varumākiya iṭaiyarum uṟaiyum kuṭiyiruppil taṅkuvīrāyiṉ avarkaḷ cilutta tiṉaiyaricic cōṟṟaip pāluṭaṉ taruvārkaḷ.
iṭaiyar mantai mēykkum pulveḷi
mullai nilattu ūraik kaṭantu cellum pōtu mantaikaḷai mēykkum iṭaiyaraik kāṇpīrkaḷ. cerupput tarittiruttalāl avar aṭikaḷil taḻumpu ēṟiyirukkum; kōṭāriyaik kaiyil piṭittiruppatāl avarkaḷiṉ kai kāyttu viṟaittirukkum. avar kaiyilē pacukkaḷai aṭittu ōṭṭukiṉṟa kōlai vaittiruppar. kāvaṭiyiṉ iraṇṭu talaikaḷi lum uṟikaḷait tūkkic celvatāl tōḷkaḷ kāyttu mayiruṭaiyaṉavā yirukkum. avarkaḷ mayiriṭattē pāṟkaṟaiyait taṭaviyiruppar; marak kompu kaḷiṉ ucciyiliruntum, koṭikaḷiliruntum paṟitta pūkkaḷai mālaiyākak kaṭṭi aṇintiruppar; araiyil oru uṭaiyai uṭuttiruppar; pāṟcōṟṟai uṇavākak koḷvar; kaṉṟukaḷuṭaiya pacuk kūṭṭattaik kāṭṭil mēyum paṭi viṭṭuttām maraniḻalkaḷiliruntu, neruppuk koḷḷiyāl iṭṭa tuḷai kaḷaiyuṭaiya kuḻalkaḷil pālaip paṇṇai iṉimaiyākap pāṭuvar; kuḻalai ūti veṟuttārkaḷāyiṉ kumiḻiṉ kompai vaḷaittukkaṭṭiya aralaiyiṉ tumpākiya narampaik kaiyiṉāl teṟittu, vaṇṭiṉaṅkaḷ tammiṉattiṉ ōcaiyeṉṟu kātukoṭuttuk kēṭkumpaṭi kuṟiñcippaṇ pāṭuvar.
tāṉiyam viḷaittum mantai mēyttum vāḻum iṭaiyar kuṭiyiruppu
ivvakaip pullaṭarnta iṭattaik kaṭantu muḷḷaṭarnta viṭarttar maraṅkaḷ cūḻnta māṭu taṅkumiṭattaiyuṭaiya ūrkaḷiṟ celvīrāyiṉ, vīṭukaḷiṉ muṟṟattē yāṉaik kūṭṭam niṉṟāṟpōla varaku mutaliya tāṉi yaṅkaḷ niṟaikkappaṭṭa kutirkaḷ tōṉṟum. talaivāyilkaḷil yāṉaiyiṉ kālkaḷpōṉṟa kutirkaḷ naṭappeṟṟirukkum. pukaipiṭitta koṭṭilkaḷiṉ cuvarkaḷil vaṇṭic cakkaraṅkaḷum kalappaikaḷum cārtti vaikkappaṭ ṭirukkum. vīṭukaḷiṉ kūraikaḷ kariya varaku vaikkōlāl vēyappaṭṭu mārikālattu mēkampōlat tōṉṟum. ivviyalpiṉavākiya kuṭiyiruppu kaḷai aṭaivīrāyiṉ varakuṭaṉ avarai vitaiyai iṭṭuc camaitta cōṟṟiṉai uṇṇumpaṭi aṅkuḷḷār taruvar.
maruta nilakkāṭci
ikkuṭiyiruppaik kaṭantu celvīrkaḷāyiṉ vīṭu niṟainta uṇavup poruḷkaḷuṭaiya uḻavariṉ vīṭukaḷaik kāṇpīrkaḷ. uḻavar erutukaḷai nukattil pūṭṭip peṇyāṉaiyiṉ vaḷainta vāy pōṉṟa kalappaiyiṉ uṭumpiṉ mukam pōṉṟa periya koḻukkaḷ maṟaiyumpaṭi amukki uḻutu vitaitta payirkaḷ tōṭṭaṅkaḷil viḷaintu kāṭci aḷikkum. avaṟ ṟiṉiṭaiyē taṅkiyirunta kāṭai aṟuppuk kālattukkaḷamariṉ āravārattuk kañcit taṉatu paṟakkalāṟṟātavum kaṭampiṉ pūppōṉṟu veṇṇiṟa muṭaiyaṉavumākiya kuñcukaḷaik kūṭṭik koṇṭu mullai nilattē ceṉṟu taṅkum.
amukki ūtukiṉṟa ulaiyil toḻil ceykiṟ kollaṉatu muṟinta kuṟaṭṭaippōṉṟa kavarpaṭṭa kālkaḷuṭaiya naṇṭukaḷ vayalkaḷilē, puṟṟukaḷil vāḻum. kōraip pullai vēruṭaṉ kompāl kutti eṭukkiṉṟa erutukaḷ naṇṭup puṟṟukaḷ aḻiyumpaṭi aṅku niṉṟu oṉṟōṭu oṉṟu caṇṭaiyiṭum. ivviyalpiṉatākiya vayalai uḻavar oppuravu ceytu nāṟṟu muṭikaḷai naṭuvar. vayalkaḷil vēlai purivōriṉ irumput takaṭupōṉṟu tiraiyāta melliya tōluṭaiya piḷḷaikaḷ caṇpakak kāyiṉ tātai mārpiṭattē pūciyiruppar. avarkaḷ kaḷai paṟippōr piṭuṅkip pōṭṭa neytaṟ pūvai aṇivatai veṟuttārkaḷāyiṉ muḷḷiyiṉ pūvaip paṟittup palliṉāl meṉṟu kiḻittu muṭinta kōrainārāl ataṉai mālaiyākak kaṭṭi īruṭaiya kariyatalai niṟaiyumpaṭi cūṭuvar. avarkaḷ paḻañcōṟṟai veṟuttu varampilē kaṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷa ciṟiya kuṭiliṉ muṟṟattil avaliṭippar. avvōcaikku añcik kiḷikaḷ paṟakkum. nellaṟuppōr vaḷainta katiruṭaiya muṟṟiya nelliṉ tāḷ kaḷai aṟuppar; aṟuttuk kaḷaṅkaḷil uṟaiyum teyvaṅkaḷ pali koḷ ḷumpaṭi pāmpuṟaiyum marutamara niḻalil pōraṭukkuvar. pōrkaḷiṉ aṭip pakkaṅkaḷil tuṇaṅkaiyāṭum pūtaṅkaḷiṉ veḷḷāṭaikaḷaip pōl cilanti nūlkaḷ cūḻntu kiṭakkum. pōrkaḷiṉ aṭiyaippiṭittu virittuk kaṭākkaḷāl mitittu vaikkōlaiyum kūḷattaiyum maṇiyiṉiṉṟum pirittu īram pularumpaṭi mēl kāṟṟil kaiyiṉāl tūvit tūṟṟiṉa poli mēruvaip pōla uyarntu tōṉṟum. ivvāṟu ciṟappu mikka maruta nilañ cūḻnta kuṭiyiruppukaḷilē vīṭukaḷiṉ pakkattu iṟukkap peṟṟa muḷaikaḷil āṉkaṉṟukaḷaik kaṭṭum kayiṟukaḷ kiṭakkum; talaiyait tiṟantu tāṉiyaṅkaḷ uḷḷē koṭṭappeṟṟa kutirkaḷ tōṉṟum. taccacciṟār ceyta ciṟu tērkaḷai uruṭṭic ceṉṟa kaḷaippāl piṉḷaikaḷ cevilit tāyaruṭaiya pālai niṟaiyavuṇṭu paṭukkaiyilē kaṇvaḷarvar. ivvāṟu vaṟumai teriyāta kuṭiyiruppiṉaiyuṭaiya ūrilē celvīrāyiṉ aṅkuḷḷār neṟcōṟṟiṉaik kōḻippēṭṭiṉ poriyalōṭu taruvar.
karumpu ālaikaḷ
vayalkaḷilē pukaicūḻnta koṭṭilkaḷ kāṇappaṭum. aṅkuk karumpu ālaikaḷ āṭukaiyiṉālē malaiyiṭattil ciṅkam pāytalāl yāṉai payantu cattamiṭṭāṟ pōṉṟu āravāram uṇṭākum. ivvāṟu āṭṭippiḻiyappaṭṭa karuppañ cāṟṟai aṅkuḷḷār kaṭṭiyākak kāyccu var. akkoṭṭilkaḷiṟ celvīrāyiṉ karuppañcāṟṟaiyum kaṟkaṇṭaiyum uṇṇumpaṭi peṟukuvir.
mīṉ piṭippōr kuṭiyiruppu
ikkoṭṭilkaḷaik kaṭantu appāṟ ceṉṟāl ērikaḷilum maṭukkaḷilum mīṉ piṭippāriṉ kuṭiyiruppaik kāṇpīrkaḷ. avarkaḷiṉ vīṭu kāñci vañci mutaliya maraṅkaḷiṉ kompukaḷaik kaimaraṅkaḷukku naṭuvē tūṇāka nāṭṭi mūṅkiṟṟaṭikaḷai variccāka niraittu vaittut, tāḻai nārāṟ kaṭṭit taruppaip pullāl vēynta iṟappukaḷai uṭaiyaṉa. talai vāyilkaḷ puṉṉaik kompiṉāl kaṭṭappaṭṭirukkum. avaṟṟiṉ kūrai kaḷil paṭaraviṭappaṭṭa curaik koṭikaḷil kāykaḷ toṅkum. muṟṟattē mīṉkaḷai vāri eṭukkum valaikaḷ kiṭakkum. iḷaiyavarkaḷum mutiyavar kaḷum talaivāyilil taṅki iruppārkaḷ; piṉpu iṟālum kayal mīṉum piṟaḻukiṉṟa āḻnta kuḷaṅkaḷil ulāvi mīṉkaḷaip piṭippārkaḷ. avarkaḷ kuṟṟāta koḻiyalariciyaik kaḻiyākat tuḻāvi aṭuvar; aṭṭa kūḻai akaṉṟa vāyuṭaiya taṭṭuppiḻāvilē viṭṭu āṟṟuvar; pāmpu irukkum puṟṟiṟ kiṭakkum puṟṟām paḻañcōṟṟai otta puṟattiṉaiyuṭaiya nel muḷaiyai iṭittuc cērumpaṭi atilē kalappar; piṉpu viralālē kalakki arippar. avar kuṭiyiruppil taṅkuvīrāyiṉ ivvāṟu arittuc cāṭiyil viṭṭu muṟṟiya kāramuḷḷa kaḷḷai mīṉ cūṭṭōṭu peṟukuvir.
ikkuṭiyiruppaik kaṭantu ceṉṟāl aṅku nīrnilaikaḷ kāṇap paṭum. tūṇṭilil kōkkum iraiyai iṭṭu vaikkum tōṟpaiyai uṭaiyavaṉum mīṉait tappāmaṟ piṭikkum tiṟamaiyuṭaiyavaṉumākiya pāṇaṉ mūṅkiṟṟaṭiyiṟ kaṭṭiya kayiṟṟiṭattuḷḷa tūṇṭilil iraiyaik kuṟṟi nīruḷviṭuvāṉ. avviraiyaik kavarntu akappaṭāmaṟ pōṉa vāḷaimīṉ nīr nilaiyiṉ pakkattē niṉṟa pirampiṉ niḻalaikkaṇṭu añcum. annīr nilaiyil pūtta kaṭavuḷ cūṭutaṟ kuriya tāmaraip pūvaic cūṭātu, poykaikaḷil vāṉaviṟ pōṉṟu pūttuḷḷa kuvaḷaiyaiyum nīlattaiyum piṟa pūkkaḷaiyum avaṟṟaip paṟippār uṅkaṭkuttara nīvir avaṟṟaip peṟṟuc cūṭik koṇṭu appāṟ celvīrāka.
pārppār illam
appāṟ ceṉṟāl pārppār illaṅkaḷaik kāṇpīrkaḷ. pacukkaḷ pacum puṟṟaraikaḷaittēṭi mēyac cella avaṟṟiṉ ceḻiya kaṉṟukaḷ pantaṟ kālkaḷil kaṭṭi niṟkum. kōḻi, nāy mutaliyavai av villaṅkaḷuḷ cellamāṭṭā. cāṇiyāl meḻukappaṭṭirukkum vīṭukaḷuḷ vaḻipaṭu teyvaṅkaḷiṉ uruvaṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭum. kiḷikaḷ vētam paṭikkum. arun tatiyaippōṉṟa ciṟantakaṟpum nalloḻukkamum vaḷaiyaṇinta kai kaḷumuṭaiya pārppaṉap peṇkaḷ nalla cuvaiyuḷḷa uṇavukaḷai ākku vārkaḷ. avvīṭukaḷai aṭaintāl irācāṉṉam eṉṉum cōṟṟiṉaiyum, mātuḷaṅkāykaḷaip pillaikaḷāka arintu miḷakuttūḷ tūvik karuvēppilai kūṭṭi neyyil vetuppiya poriyalkaḷaiyum, māṅkāy ūṟukāy mutaliyavaṟṟaiyum uṇṇumpaṭi peṟukuvir.
nīrāṭun tuṟai
nīrāṭun tuṟaikaḷilē makaḷir viḷaiyāṭip pōkaviṭṭup pōṉa kuḻaiyiṉai nīla niṟamuṭaiya ciccilip paṟavai eṭuttukkoṇṭu paṟavaikaḷ niṟaintirukkum paṉaiyiṟ cellātu, antaṇar yākacālaiyil naṭṭa yūpattiṉ mēlirukkum. atu cōṉakar pāymarattiṉ mēlēṟṟiya aṉṉa viḷakkaip pōlavum viṭi veḷḷiyaip pōlavum oḷiviṭṭu viḷaṅkum.
tuṟaimukak kāṭci
kaṭaṟkaraiyilē mēṟkut ticaiyiluḷḷa kutiraikaḷaiyum ticaiyiluḷḷa paṇṭaṅkaḷaiyum koṇṭu vanta marakkalaṅkaḷ cūḻntu niṟkum. maṇal mikkuṭaiya vītikaḷil toḻilāḷar kāvalpuriyum paṇṭa cālaikaḷum paratavar vāḻum vāṉai muṭṭum māḷikaikaḷum kāṇap peṟum. vīṭukaḷil viḷaiporuḷkaḷ niṟaintu kiṭakkum. vayalkaḷil uḻukiṉṟa erutukaḷaip pacukkaḷ neruṅkātapaṭi āṭṭukkaṭākkaḷum nāykaḷum cuḻaṉṟu tiriyum.
peṇkaḷ pantāṭutal
uyarnta māṭaṅkaḷil uṟaiyum pēraṇikalaṉkaḷai aṇinta makaḷir paciya maṇikōtta vaṭaṅkaḷ uṭaiya araiyil kiṭakkiṉṟa melliya tukil acaiya malaiyilē āravārikkiṉṟa mayilkaḷ pōla ulāvuvar; kāliṭattuḷḷa poṟ cilampuka ḷolikka nūliṉāl varintu ceyyappaṭṭa pantiṉai aṭittu viḷaiyāṭuvar; metteṉak kaiyiṟ ṟaritta vaḷaikaḷ acaiyumpaṭi poṟkaḻaṅku koṇṭu muntai otta vārnta maṇalil viḷaiyāṭuvar.
kaṭkaṭai
kaḷḷuṇpār palarum pukukiṉṟa kaḷ viṟkum kaṭai vāyilil paciya koṭikaḷ āṭikkoṇṭirukkum. muṟṟattē teyvattukkut tūviṉa pū vāṭalkaḷ kāṇappaṭum. avviṭattē kaḷḷaic camaikkiṉṟa makaḷir vīṭṭil kaḻuviyanīr vaṭitaliṉ nilam cēṟāṭiyirukkum. ac cēṟṟil puraḷu kiṉṟa peṇ paṉṟiyōṭu cērkkaik karuttāl cellāmal kuḻiyilviṭṭu, iṭittamāvai uṇavākak koṭuttu āṇpaṉṟi vaḷarkkappaṭum. nīvir neytal nilattiluḷḷa paṭṭiṉattiṟ ṟaṅku vīrāyiṉ appaṉṟiyiṉ tacaiyōṭu kaḷḷaiyum uṇṇumpaṭi peṟukuvir.
kalaṅkarai viḷakkam
tuṟaimukattil vāṉai muṭṭumpaṭi uyarntatum kaṟṟai mutaliya vaṟṟāl vēyappaṭātatumākiya kalaṅkarai viḷakkam tōṉṟum. atu kaṭaliṟ cellum marakkalaṅkaḷ tām cērum iṭattai aṟintu cellumpaṭi amaik kappaṭṭatu. avviṭattaik kaṭantu ceṉṟāl teṉṉaṅkīṟṟāl vēynta kūraiyaiyum muṟṟattē mañcaṭceṭikaḷaiyumuṭaiya vīṭukaḷaik kāṇ pīrkaḷ. avvīṭukaḷil taṅkuvīrāyiṉ iḷanīraiyum, vāḻaip paḻattaiyum, paṉaiyiṉ nuṅkiṉaiyum, vēṟu paṇṭaṅkaiyum muṟṟiṉa vaḷḷimutaliya kiḻaṅkukaḷaiyum uṇṇumpaṭi peṟukuvir. iccelvam poruntiya paṭṭiṉattē vaḻiccelvōr paci tīrumpaṭi cōṟṟai ākkukiṉṟa pāṉai acaintu viḻumpaṭi kamukiṉ pakkattē niṟkum teṅkiṉkāy viḻum.
tiru eḵkā
ippaṭṭiṉattai viṭṭuc ceṉṟāl pala maraṅkaḷ aṭarnta iṭaṅkaḷil amaikkappaṭṭa viṇṇaittoṭum māṭaṅkaḷ viḷaṅkum. ūrkaḷil peṇkaḷ vaḷḷikkūttu āṭuvar. ivvakaip puṟanāṭukaḷaik kaṭantapiṉ kāntaḷ vaḷarkiṉṟa malaiyiṭattu yāṉai kiṭantāṟ pōlap pāmpaṇai yilē tirumāl paḷḷi koḷkiṉṟa tiru eḵkāvai aṭaivīrkaḷ.
aṅku veyyil nuḻaiyātavum ilai nerukkattāl kuyilkaḷ nuḻaikiṉṟavumākiya iḷamarac cōlaikaḷ uṇṭu. accōlaikaḷuḷ uḷḷa kāñci marattilē paṭarntirukkutam kurukkattik koṭiyiṉ varikaḷai yuṭaiya pūkkaḷ, vārnta maṇaliṭattē kuḻikaḷil niṟkum nīril viḻuntu appavāṇikar cuṭṭa appam pālilē kiṭantāṟ pōl tōṉṟum. muṉpu nīrniṉṟa appoḻilkaḷiṭattē, iḷampiṟaiyaik karumpāmpu paṟṟiṉāṟ pōla makaravāyeṉṉum talaikkōlamum, vaṇṭukaḷ cūḻum neṟṟiyum, kaḷḷuṇṇalāl kuḷircci poruntiya kaṇkaḷumuṭaiya makaḷir pakaṟ poḻutil viḷaiyāṭuvar. avarkaḷōṭu nīvirum viḷaiyāṭuvīrkaḷāka. piṉpu, nīṅkaḷ periya nīrāṭum tuṟaiyil iḷavēṉil iṉpam nukarpavar kaḷōṭu iruntu iḷaippāṟuṅkaḷ; cūl koṇṭa kamukiṉ vayiṟṟaip pōṉṟa paccaik kuppikaḷai (toṉṉaikaḷai) uṇṭu kaḻittu nāṭōṟum periya makiḻccikkuriya avviṭattē iruṅkaḷ; nummuṭaiya kariya taṇṭiṉaiyuṭaiya iṉiya yāḻai vācittut tiru veḵkāvilirukkum kaṭavuḷai vāḻtti avviṭattiṉiṉṟum celluṅkaḷ.
kāñci mūtūr
appāṟ ceṉṟāl kāñci mūtūrai aṭaivīrkaḷ. aṅkuc cōlai kaḷil parikkōlaik kaiyilēyuṭaiya yāṉaippākar cōrntirukkum pōtu, cūluṭaiya manti yāṉaikku neyyuṭaṉ kalantu vaikkappaṭṭa ariciyait tiruṭikkoṇṭu ceṉṟu uṇṇum. tēr ōṭutalāl terukkaḷ kuḻintirukkum. pukaḻuṭaiya mūtūriṭattu vāḻum makkaḷ kaṭaikaḷil viṟṟa lum vāṅkalum ceyvar. ulakattē koṭuppāraik koṭātu taṭutta1 am mūtūr paricilarkku aṭaiyāta vāyiluṭaiyatu; ceṅkaṟkaḷāl eṭukkap paṭṭa matilkaḷ kāvaṟkāṭu cūḻnta pakkaṅkaḷai uṭaiyaṉa. annakar, pulāl nāṟṟa muṭaiya kaṭal cūḻanta ivvulakil, puṟākkaḷirukkiṉṟavum pūvāmaṟ kāyk kiṉṟavum maraṅkaḷuṭ ciṟanta palāvaip pōṉṟu ciṟappuṭaiyatu; palacamayatta varkaḷ toḻumpaṭi eṭukkappaṭum viḻākkaḷāl ciṟantatu.
aivar nūṟṟu varai veṉṟatu
aivar tāmaraimukaiyuṭaiya neṭiya tērai uṭaiyar. tarumaṉ mutalāṉōr, piṟaiyai mēlē cūṭi antik kālattē ulāvukiṉṟa mukilaip pōṉṟavum, veḷḷiya kompuṭaiyavumākiya yāṉaikaḷiṉ piṇattai iratta āṟu iḻuttuc cellumpaṭi porutu turiyōtaṉaṉ mutaliya nūṟṟuvarum maṭiyumpaṭi veṉṟāṉ. attarumaṉ mutalāṉōr pōla iḷantiraiyaṉ maṭṭuk kaṭaṅkāp paṭaiyuṭaṉ; avaṉ vanta pakaivarai veṉṟu paricilai virumpi aṭaikkalamākac ceṉṟārkkup pātukāvalākak kāñci nakaril vīṟṟirukkiṉṟāṉ.
aracaṉ kāñci mūtūril iravalarppurantu vīṟṟirukkum iyalpu
taṉṉōṭu māṟupaṭṭa aracaṉiṉ maṉṟukaḷil makkaḷiṉṟāmpaṭi aḻittalum, taṉṉai virumpivantōr nāṭu tirumaṭantaipolivu peṟṟirukkumpaṭi aḷittalum avaṉukku eḷitu. malaiyiṭattiṉiṉṟum iḻikiṉṟa aruvi am malaiyiluḷḷa paṇṭaṅkaḷai vārikkoṇṭu vantu kaṭalukkuk koṭuttāṟ pōlat tiṟaikaḷuṭaṉ vanta palanāṭṭu vēntar avaṉuṭaiya talaivāyilil kāttuniṟpar. aṉṉōr, kaṅkaiyaik kaṭakka virumpiṉōr tōṇikkuk kāttiruntu tūṅkiṉatu pōlayāṉai mutaliya celvaṅkaḷōṭu neruṅki niṉṟu kālam pārttiruppar.
iḷantiraiyaṉiṉ nalliyalpukaḷ 
māṭattirukkum cēvaṟpuṟā, yāṉaikku vaḷainta pūṇ ceykiṉṟa kollaṉatu kūṭattil eḻunta ōcaikku veruvit tuyil nīṅkum. kaṭavuḷ iruppataṟkum ilakkumi uṟaivataṟkum iṭamāyuḷḷa ittoṉṉakaril vēntaṉ iruppāṉ. avaṉ kaṭaliṉ naṭuvē ñāyiṟu tōṉṟi oḷiceytāṟ pōla mayakka millāta aṟivuṭaiyaṉ. avaṉ vaṟiyarāy naliveyti ṉōrkkum vaṟumaiyuṟ ṟirantōrkkum virumpiyavaṟṟaik koṭuppāṉ; pulavarukku āparaṇaṅkaḷai aḷippāṉ; paricilarukku aḷittuk koṭumai illāta amaiccarōṭu vīṟṟiruppāṉ. nī avaṉaip pārttu, “yāṉaiyaik kaṇṭa ciṅkakkuṭṭi ataṉ mattakattaik koḷḷa virumpi ṉāṟ pōlap pakaivaratu matilkaḷai aḻittu aracaruṭaiya muṭikkalam mutaliyavaṟṟai vāṅkikkoṇṭu vīramuṭi puṉaiyum veṟṟiyiṉaiyum, pakaip pulattukkoṇṭa koḷḷaiyākiya uṇaviṉaiyum, māṟupaṭṭa araca rōṭu cantuceyyum viruppamiṉmaiyaiyum uṭaiyavaṉē! toṇṭaik koṭiyaic cūṭiya marapil utittavaṉē! vīrarkku vīrattaik koṭukkiṉṟa vaṉē! koṭiyārkkuk koṭiyavaṉē! celvamuṭaiyōrkkuk koṭuttalai virumpukiṉṟavaṉē! pōrttoḻilil pukaḻpeṟṟavaṉē! kaṭaliṭattē ceṉṟu cūraṉaik koṉṟa murukaṉaip peṟṟa perumaiyuṭaiya vayiṟṟai yum pēykaḷāṭum tuṇaṅkaik kūttaiyumuṭaiya iṟaivikkup pēymakaḷ cila noṭi coṉṉāṟpōlak kuṟaiyāta koṭaiyuṭaiya niṉpukaḻiṟ cila vaṟṟaik kūṟip pukaḻntu vantēṉ; neṭitu vāḻka!” eṉṟu colli yāḻai vācikkum muṟaimaiyil vācippāyāka. mayilkaḷ āravārikkiṉṟa iḷamarak kāṭṭil mantikaḷ pāyvatāl cintikkiṭakkum malarkaḷaiyuṭaiya kāṭum, teyvaṅkaḷ uṟaikiṉṟavum, kiṉṉarap paṟavai pāṭukiṉṟavum, muṟṟattē māṉum puliyum tuyilkoḷḷa yāṉai muṟittukkoṇṭu vanta viṟakāl muṉivar ōmañceykiṉṟavumākiya cāralkaḷum pala aruvikaḷumuṭaiya malaikaḷukkut talaivaṉākiya aracaṉ celvamum yākkaiyum nilai yillāta ivvulakil pukaḻaip peṟutal naṉṟu eṉa uṇarntu uṉṉai virumpi aḻaippāṉ;
iḷantiraiyaṉ iravalar pukaḻakkēṭṭup paricil vaḻaṅkum taṉmai
aḻaittu uṉatu araiyiṟ kiṭakkum koṭṭaip pāciyiṉ vēraip pōṉṟa kiḻinta uṭaiyaip pōkkip pālāviyai ottu viḷaṅkukiṉṟa nūlāl ceyta paṭṭāṭaikaḷai nīyum uṉatu cuṟṟattiṉarum uṭukkumpaṭi taruvāṉ. vaḷainta arivāḷaip piṭittalāl kaikāyppēṟiṉa maṭaiyaṉ ākkiya iṟaicciyaiyum cōṟṟaiyum iṉiya cuvaiyuṭaiya piṟa uṇavu kaḷaiyum veḷḷik kalaṅkaḷiliṭṭu uṉatu piḷḷaikaḷiṉiṭaiyē vaittu ācaiyuṭaṉ uṇṇumpaṭi ceyvāṉ. kār kālattu miṉṉal tōṉṟiṉāṟ pōṉṟu viṟali mayiriṭattē cūṭumpaṭi poṉmālaiyaiyum poṟṟāmaraip pūvaiyum aḷippāṉ; caṅkiṉ niṟampōṉṟa veḷḷiya kutiraikaḷ pūṭṭiya poṟṟēriṉait taruvāṉ; atōṭu amaiyātu pakai aracariṭamiruntu kavarnta kutiraikaḷaiyum cēṇattaiyum koṭuppāṉ; nāvalan taṇ poḻil kuṟṟamiṉṟi viḷaṅkumpaṭi ivai oḻinta paricilkaḷaiyum vaḻaṅku vāṉ. iravalaṉē kālan tāḻttātu kaṭitil avaṉpāl ēkuka.
5. 1mullaippāṭṭu
naṟcoṟkēṭṭal
mullaikkuriya kārkālam nērntatu. pōrvayiṟ pirinta talaivariṉ pirivaip poṟukkamāṭṭāta irācamātēvi tuyaruṟṟut tuyil koḷḷap peṟāḷāyiṉāḷ. avaḷ kaṇkaḷ muttuppōṟ kaṇṇīrai utirttaṉa. avaḷ taṉatu nekiḻnta kaivaḷaikaḷait tiruttiṉāḷ; ampu taitta mayilpōla naṭunaṭuṅki aṇikalaṉkaḷ nekiḻappeṟṟāḷ. ataṉaik kaṇṭu varuntiya mutupeṇṭu pala iṉiyamoḻikaḷaik kūṟiṉāḷ; talaivaṉatu māṉam pukaḻ mutaliyavaṟṟaiyum, araciyal naṭappataṟku avaṉ piriya vēṇṭiya iṉṟiyamaiyāmaiyaiyum eṭuttukkāṭṭiṉāḷ. talaivitaṉatu āṟṟāmai mikutiyāl mutupeṇṭu kūṟiyavaṟṟait teḷiyum tiṟamai illāḷāyiṉāḷ. aññāṉṟu ammutupeṇṭu irācamātēviyai nōkki, “paṭaittalaivar ūrppuṟattuḷḷa pākkattē naṟcoṟkēṭpāraip pōkkiṉār. avarkaḷ tām nāḻiyiṟkoṇṭu ceṉṟa nellaiyum mullai malaraiyum tūvit teyvattai vaṇaṅki naṟcoṟ kēṭka niṉṟārkaḷ. kuḷirāl naṭuṅkum iṭaicci tōḷiṟ kaṭṭiṉa kaiyaḷāyp pōntu, tāmpiṟ piṇikkappaṭṭu niṟpaṉavum mulaiyuṇṇāmaiyāl cuḻaṉṟu varuntukiṉṟavu mākiya kaṉṟukaḷai nōkki, ‘uṅkaḷ tāymār nirampamēyntu ippoḻutē varuvar’ eṉṟāḷ. annaṟcolliṉ palaṉāka niṉtalaivar pakavaraiveṉṟu avariṭattiṟ peṟṟa tiṟaip poruḷkaḷuṭaṉ makiḻcci peruka ippoḻutē varuvar eṉak kūṟiṉāḷ. ataṉaic cevik koṇṭa talaivi talaivar ivvāṟu pirintālaṉṟi araciyal nikaḻāteṉa maṉatiṟ koḷvāḷāyiṉaḷ.
irācamātēvi aracaṉiṉ varavai etirnōkkik kiṭattal
cakkarattaiyum valampuriccaṅkaiyum tāṅkiya periya kaikaḷaiyum, ilakkumiyait tāṅkum mārpiṉaiyumuṭaiya tirumāl māvaliyiṭam kuṟaḷ vaṭiviṟ ceṉṟu mūvaṭi maṇṇirantār. ataṉai aḷikkavēṇṭi māvali aṅkaiyil nīr peyta ñāṉṟu tirumāl neṭiya vaṭivu koṇṭār. kaṭa liṭattu nīraikkuṭitta mukilkaḷ anneṭiya mālaippōla uyarntu malai yil taṅki mālaik kālattil maḻaiyaip peytaṉa. araṇmaṉaiyiṭattup paḷḷiyaṟaiyil poṟpāvai ēnti niṉṟa takaḷiyilē viḷakku niṉṟerin tatu. ēḻunilai māṭattiṉ mūṭṭuvāykaḷi ṉiṉṟum corikiṉṟa nīrttārai yiṉ ōcaikaḷ āravārittaṉa. ivvāṟu nikaḻtalum talaivaṉ varuvēṉ eṉṟu kūṟic ceṉṟa paruvakālam vantatātaliṉ avaṉ tavaṟātu varuvāṉ eṉṉum eṇṇattōṭu irācamātēvi paṭukkaiyil kiṭappāḷāyiṉaḷ. iḵtu ivvāṟākap pakai aracaṉmītu paṭai eṭuttuc ceṉṟa talaivaṉatu nilai mēl varumāṟuḷḷatu.
pācaṟai vakuttal
pakaimēṟ ceṉṟa aracaṉatu paṭaivīrar kāṭṭāṟu cūḻnta kāṭṭiṭattē piṭavam mutaliya putarkaḷai veṭṭiṉārkaḷ; pakaip pulattē kāval kāttu niṉṟa veṭṭuvar araṇkaḷai aḻittārkaḷ; araṇāka nāṟpuṟamum muḷ vēli iṭṭārkaḷ; ataṉ naṭuvē paranta pāṭi vīṭṭai amaittārkaḷ. aracaṉ appācaṟaiyiṉiṭattē, orukaiyaip paṭukkaiyiṉ mēl vaittum oru kaiyai muṭiyuṭaṉ cērttiyum iruntu, yāṉaiyai eṟintu paṭṭa vīrarai niṉaintum ampu taitta varuttattāl cevi cāyttup pulluṇṇāmal niṟkum kutiraikaḷai niṉaintum, ippaṭai nonta aḷavukku nāḷai ev vāṟu porutum eṉṟu niṉaintum varuntuvāṉ. appācaṟaiyil taṅkum pala moḻikaḷaip pēcum paṭai vīrarukku naṭuvil ōriṭattai aracaṉukkuk kōyilāka ellārum amaittārkaḷ; kālkaḷaik kūṭamāka naṭṭārkaḷ; kayiṟṟai vaḷaittuk kaṭṭiṉārkaḷ; valiya viṟkaḷai ūṉṟiṉārkaḷ. avaṟ ṟiṉ mītu tuṇikaḷait tūkkiṉārkaḷ. attōṟṟam mukkōl antaṇaṉ mukkōlil taṉatu kāvi uṭaiyai iṭṭuvaitta taṉmaiyai ottiruntatu. piṉpu pūttoḻilait talaiyiluṭaiya eṟikōlkaḷai ūṉṟiṉārkaḷ; paricaikaḷai niraiyāka vaittārkaḷ; pala niṟamūṭṭiya tuṇikaḷaik kuttuk kālkaḷil taittu avaṟṟai vaḷaittu vaittārkaḷ. ivvāṟu amaikkap paṭṭa aracaṉatu kūṭārattiṉ vāyilil caṭṭaiyiṭṭa valiya yavaṉar puliyaic caṅkaliyiṟ kaṭṭivaitta vaṭivaṉai eḻutiṉar.
aracaṉatu il
aṅkuk kālattiṉ aḷavai aḷantu colluvōraracaṉai vaṇaṅki vāḻtti nāḻikai vaṭṭiliṟ ceṉṟa nāḻikai ittaṉai eṉak kūṟiṉar. appōtu tūveṇtukiluṭuttapeṇkaḷ 1viḷakkaik koḷuttik kāṭṭiṉārkaḷ. aracaṉ tiraiccīlai toṅka viṭappaṭṭa uḷḷaṟaiyil tuyilumpaṭi ceṉṟāṉ.
pācaṟaik kāṭci
teruviṉ nāṟcanti taḻaikaḷāl vēyappaṭṭiruntatu. aṅkuk kāvalāka niṟuttappaṭṭa yāṉai karumpaiyum neṟkatirōṭu kalantu kaṭṭiya ilaiyaiyum atimaturat taḻaiyaiyum uṇṇātu avaṟṟāl taṉatu neṟṟiyaittuṭaittum kompiṉmītu iṭṭa taṉatu neṟṟiyiṭattē avaṟṟaikkoṇṭum niṉṟatu; yāṉaip pākar yāṉaip pēccākiya vaṭa moḻiyaic collik kavar uṭaiya parikkōlāl kavaḷattai uṇṇumpaṭi kuttiṉārkaḷ. ciṟṟāṭkaḷ ney koppaḷikkiṉṟa tirikkuḻāyil nīṇṭa tiriyaik koṇṭu oḻuṅkāyamaitta viḷakkukaḷaik koḷuttiṉārkaḷ. viḷakkukaḷ aviyuntōṟum tam kaiyiṭattuḷḷa pantattaik koḷut tiṉārkaḷ.
aracaṉ veṟṟi peṟṟu mīḷumiṭattu vaḻiyiṟ kāṇumkāṭci
talaippākaiyum caṭṭaiyum tarittu meykkāppāḷar aracaṉaik kāvalākac cūḻntu tirintaṉar. caṭṭaiyiṭṭa ūmarākiya milēccar paḷḷi koḷḷumiṭattaic cūḻntu tirintaṉar. ivvāṟu maṇiyōcai aṭaṅkiya naṭuccāmattum maṟṟaiyanāḷ ceyyavirukkum pōrai niṉaintu aracaṉ tuyil koḷḷāṉāyiṉāṉ. aṭuttanāḷ avaṉ pakaivaraikkollak karuti vaitta vāḷaip piṭittu veṟṟiyaip peṟṟāṉ; pakai aracar virumpum nilaṅkaḷai veṉṟa veṟṟiyiṉ aṭaiyāḷamāka veṟṟikkoṭiyai uyarttiṉāṉ. avaṉ mīṇṭu varum kāṭṭu vaḻiyilē kāyā kariya pūkkaḷaip pūttatu. koṉṟai poṉṉiṟa malarkaḷai malarntatu; veṇkāntaḷ uḷḷaṅkaipōl virintatu. muṟukiya kompuṭaiya āṇmāṉ peṇmāṉōṭu tuḷḷi viḷaiyāṭiṟṟu; tiraṇṭa tōṉṟi irattampōṟ pūttatu. veṟṟik kaṟikuṟiyākiya kompum caṅkum muḻaṅkiṉa. niraitta cēṉaikkum kutiraip paṭaikkum muṉ vanta aracaṉatu tēriṟ pūṭṭiya kutiraikaḷ irācamātēviyiṉ cevi nirampumpaṭi āravārittaṉa.1
6. 1maturaik kāñci
aracaṉ nallāṭciyil nikaḻvaṉa
ulakam periya tiraikaḷ vīcukiṉṟa kaṭalai ellaiyāka vuṭaiyatu. atu tēṉkūṭu tūṅkum ucciyaṉavākiya malaikaḷaiyuṭaiyatu. ticaikaḷ kuḷiravum, maraṅkaḷum palluyirum taḻaikkavum vāṉam poyyātu peytatu. ñāyiṟum tiṅkaḷum kaḷaṅkamiṉṟi viḷaṅkiṉa. viṇmīṉkaḷ cellum muṟaimaiyiṟ ceṉṟaṉa. makkaḷ paciyum piṇiyumiṉṟi vāḻntaṉar. aracaṉ ulakattaik kāttalāl tikku yāṉaikaḷ varuttan tīrntaṉa. ivvakai ulakaip poymoḻiyāta amaiccaruṭaṉ pala ūḻikaḷilum tamatu pukaḻ nilaikkumpaṭi āṇṭa uyarnta aracar marapil utittavaṉē! accantarum pōrkkaḷattē kuṟaittalaip piṇaṅkaḷ eḻuntu tāḷattukku āṭumpaṭi, kompukaḷuṭaiya yāṉaiyiṉ piṇantiṉṟa pēymakaḷ āravāram poruntiya tuṇaṅkaik kūttāṭuvāḷ.
irāvaṇaṉai akattiyar teṟkē iyaṅkātu tavirttatu
cōṟaṭukiṉṟa pēy, vīrariṉ talaiyākiya aṭuppil aracaruṭaiya irattamākiya ulai ciṉamākiya tīyiṉālē kotittup poṅkukaiyāl vīra vaḷaiyaṇinta tōḷuṭaiya kaikaḷai akappaiyākak koṇṭu tuḻāvic camaitta ūṉcōṟṟaip piṟaṅkiṭāta vīrarkku vēḷvi ceyyum. iv vakaittākiya pōrai vellum paṭaiyuṭaiya irāvaṇaṉ tamiḻ nāṭṭai āḷātapaṭi pōkkiṉa potiyiṉ malaiyil irukkum akattiyaṉukkup piṉ cāṉṟōṉāyirukkum ciṟappuṭaiyavaṉē!
vaṭimpalalampa niṉṟa pāṇṭiyaṉ
mukapaṭāttaiyum neṟṟippaṭṭattaiyum kōpattaiyum, matattaiyu muṭaiya malaipōluyarnta pōrkkaḷiṟu kōpittu vīraraik koṉṟu tirintatu; pakaivarmēṟ celkiṉṟa kutiraikaḷiṉ vēkattāl eḻukiṉṟa puḻuti ñāyiṟṟai maṟaittatu; kutiraikaḷ pūṭṭappaṭṭa tērkaḷ kāṟṟaip pōl viraintuceṉṟaṉa; vāḷvīrar vāḷiṉāl viraintu veṭṭiṉar. iv vāṟu naṭakkiṉṟa pōril cēra cōḻarkaḷākiya iruperuvēntarōṭu kuṟunila maṉṉarum iḷaikkumpaṭi porutu veṉṟatō ṭamaiyātu kuṟiñcinila maṉṉaraiyum veṉṟu pakaivar nilaṅkaḷaik kaikkoṇṭu poṉ ṉārattai mārpil aṇinta vaṭimpalampaniṉṟa pāṇṭiyaṉ marapil vanta vēntē! pakaivar kiṭṭutaṟkariya kāvaṟkāṭṭaiyum uyarnta kōpura vāyil kaḷaiyum nīṇṭa matilaiyum niraitta1 cūṭṭiṉaiyumuṭaiya ampukaḷ umiḻum araṇaiyum matiyātu ceṉṟu pakaivar nilaṅkaḷaik koṇṭavaṉē!
